[LGS_family_data_01] FamP: the original simultaneous family, fixed coefficients, ex, ties and fills
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/ParConsts.lean#L31-L100
Range SHA256: b6f0d62fca0168af12870f316f288b5be29b295605e8c1a650278780cdff6915
structure FamP (eps : ℝ) extends FamCore eps where

  params : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, ParameterChoice' (D x i) (L x i)
  PK : List ℝ
  params_K : ∀ x (hx : x1 < x) i, parConsts (params x hx i) = PK

  parT : ∀ x (hx : x1 < x) (i : Ix x), ParamsT (D x i) (L x i) (params x hx i)
  PT : List ℝ
  parT_K : ∀ x (hx : x1 < x) i, [(parT x hx i).CP, (parT x hx i).eP, (parT x hx i).Cp, (parT x hx i).Cb,
    (parT x hx i).cTO, (parT x hx i).ChT, (parT x hx i).CbN] = PT

  CTu : ℝ

  sizes : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, SizesAt (L x i)

  ties : ∀ x (i : Ix x), Ties (D x i)

  gatT : ∀ x (i : Ix x), GatTies (D x i)

  X : ∀ x (i : Ix x), DivX (D x i)

  Gx : ∀ x (i : Ix x), G.Inst (D x i) (L x i)
  Gy : ∀ x (i : Ix x), G.LatInst (D x i)
  GK : G.Consts
  GK_nonneg : GK.Nonneg

  supp_sat : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ (i : Ix x) w,
    (((D x i).what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ (L x i).sc.X'' (D x i).R.n → w ∈ (L x i).supp

  tw_supp : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ (i : Ix x) w, (D x i).what₂.muAt w ≠ (D x i).what₂.mu0 → w ∈ (L x i).supp

  fills : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ (i : Ix x) w, G.ParametersFill (Gx x i) GK w
  lats0 : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ (i : Ix x) w u,
    G.VV (Gx x i) GK (G.aL (L x i)) (G.bL (L x i)) ((D x i).what₂.places.resChar w) = ∅ →
    (D x i).what₂.places.resChar w ≠ ((D x i).mem u).A.T → (L x i).lenMN u w = 0

  latTied : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, G.LatTied (gatT x i) (Gx x i) (Gy x i)

  Csz : ℝ
  nuDef : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, DV.NuDef (D x i) (L x i)
  nprimeDef : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, DV.NprimeDef (D x i) (L x i)
  rrDef : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, DV.RRDef (D x i)
  h_eq : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ (i : Ix x) u, (D x i).div.h u = DV.hExpl6 (D x i) (L x i) (X x i) Csz u

  cX : ExtraC

  ex : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, Extra (D x i) (L x i) (gatT x i) (Gx x i) (Gy x i) GK (X x i) cX

  rho_le : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ i : Ix x, G.rhoB (D x i) GK (L x i).sc ≤ x ^ (-2 : ℝ)

  Cr : ℝ
  one_le_Cr : 1 ≤ Cr
  drop_iff : ∀ x, x1 < x → ∀ (i : Ix x) p, p.Prime →
    (p ∈ (L x i).drop ↔ NoGain (Gx x i) GK Cr p ∧ ∀ u, p ≠ ((D x i).mem u).A.T)

  fixed : GK.Pr1 = 1 ∧ GK.dl1 = 5 ∧ GK.dl2 = 4 ∧ cX.Ce = 1 ∧ cX.err.c6 = 12 ∧ cX.err.dig = 3 ∧ Cr = 1 ∧
    ∀ x (hx : x1 < x) (i : Ix x), (params x hx i).CnW = 10 ∧ 12 ≤ (parT x hx i).eP

  eta_eq : ∀ x (i i' : Ix x), (L x i).etaSz = (L x i').etaSz
  Sc_shared : ∀ x (i i' : Ix x), (L x i).Sc = (L x i').Sc
  hP_eq : ∀ x (i i' : Ix x) p, (G.hP (D x i) p).val = (G.hP (D x i') p).val
  bs_eq : ∀ x (i i' : Ix x) (h t : G.HH (D x i)) (h' t' : G.HH (D x i')), h.val = h'.val → t.val = t'.val →
    ((Gx x i).bs h t).val = ((Gx x i').bs h' t').val
  off_eq : ∀ x (i i' : Ix x) j (t : G.HH (D x i)) (t' : G.HH (D x i')), t.val = t'.val →
    (Gx x i).off j (false, t) = (Gx x i').off j (false, t')
  kapI_eq : ∀ x (i i' : Ix x), (Gx x i).kapI = (Gx x i').kapI
  ep_eq : ∀ x (i i' : Ix x), (Gx x i).ep = (Gx x i').ep

def DFamP : Type 1 := ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ε < 1 / 52 → FamP ε



[LGS_family_data_02] All literal SizesAt inequalities
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/ElevenLe.lean#L15-L144
Range SHA256: fc4825a055442eca4a6074d7351bdfa7b9183ce5ef2a11872ec018f27a1d5807
def szlA : ℝ := (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps / ((D.R.m : ℝ) * L.de2)

def szlStar : ℝ :=
  (D.R.m : ℝ) * L.sc.b /
    ((D.R.n : ℝ) * (D.R.t : ℝ) ^ 2 * ((D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) + D.R.t) * L.sc.L ^ 2 +
      (D.R.k : ℝ) * (D.R.n : ℝ) ^ 2 * (L.Sc + D.R.t + L.sc.L))

def szCond (_L : SizeData₂ D) : ℝ := (D.host.QS : ℝ) * D.host.q * D.host.d

def SizeN : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ (2 + 8 * L.sc.eps - szTh L) ≤ (D.R.n : ℝ) ∧ (D.R.n : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (2 + 8 * L.sc.eps + szTh L)

def SizeM : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ (-szTh L) * Real.sqrt (L.sc.x * L.sc.delta1) ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) ∧
    (D.R.m : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ szTh L * Real.sqrt (L.sc.x * L.sc.delta1)

def SizeK : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ (9 / 2 + 10 * L.sc.eps - szTh L) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) ∧
    (D.R.k : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (9 / 2 + 10 * L.sc.eps + szTh L)

def SizeKRel : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 5 * D.R.f * L.sc.L * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps ≤ D.R.k ∧
  8 * L.CGam * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 5 * D.R.f * L.sc.L ≤ D.R.k ∧
  (D.R.k : ℝ) ≤ Real.sqrt L.sc.x * D.R.n * rankBudget D.R L.sc.x L.sc.eps ∧
  Real.sqrt ((D.R.k : ℝ) / D.R.m) ≤ rankBudget D.R L.sc.x L.sc.eps ∧
  (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 2 * L.sc.L ≤ D.R.k ∧
  (D.R.m : ℝ) * Real.sqrt L.sc.x * Real.log L.sc.x ≤ D.R.k ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 6 * L.sc.L ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) * Real.log D.R.k ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 3 * L.sc.L * Real.log D.host.rdE ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) * Real.log D.R.k ^ 2 ∧
  Real.log D.R.f ≤ Real.sqrt D.R.rS ∧
  Real.log D.R.f ^ 2 ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 2 * L.sc.L ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * Real.log (Real.log L.sc.b) ≤ (D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * L.sc.L ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.log D.R.k ∧
  (D.R.f : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 2 * D.R.n ∧
  (D.R.f : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.r : ℝ) ^ 2

def SizeT : Prop :=
  1 ≤ (D.R.t : ℝ) ∧ (D.R.t : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.Ct + szTh L)

def SizeCond : Prop := szCond L ^ 36 * (D.R.t : ℝ) ^ 150 ≤ Real.sqrt L.sc.b

def SizeQD : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.ez / 6) ≤ Real.log D.host.q ∧
  L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.ez / 5 - L.sc.ez / 100) ≤ Real.log D.host.q ∧
  Real.log D.host.q ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 5 + szTh L) ∧
  L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.ez / 5 - L.sc.ez / 100) ≤ Real.log D.host.d ∧
  Real.log D.host.d ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 5 + szTh L) ∧
  |L.logDerivativeAtOne| ≤ L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps ∧
  1 ≤ D.host.rdE ∧ D.host.rdE ≤ (D.host.QS : ℝ) / 2 * Real.sqrt ((D.host.q : ℝ) * D.host.d)

def SizeTT : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ (-szTh L) * D.R.n * L.sc.delta1 ≤ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ∧ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ≤ szNst L

def SizeLa : Prop := L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps ≤ L.LaG ∧ L.LaG ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps + szTh L)

def SizeRho : Prop :=
  4 * szNst L * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps * L.LaG / D.R.k + L.sc.x ^ szTh L * Real.log L.sc.x * L.LaG / D.R.k ≤
    L.sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ)

def SizeBot : Prop :=
  ∀ t, (L.bT t : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.b' + szNst L * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps * L.LaG * L.sc.b' / D.R.k

def SizeDe2 : Prop := L.sc.x ^ (-L.sc.eps) ≤ L.de2

def SizeLw : Prop := (D.host.Lw : ℝ) ≤ Real.log L.sc.x ^ 2

def SizeEta : Prop :=
  (D.R.t : ℝ) ≤ L.etaSz ∧
  L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.b / ((D.R.t : ℝ) ^ 3 * D.R.f * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3) ∧
  Real.log L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) ∧
  (D.R.t : ℝ) * L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n / L.sc.x ∧
  (D.R.m : ℝ) * ((D.R.t : ℝ) + 1) * L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n ∧
  (D.R.m : ℝ) * (L.Sc + L.etaSz) ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n * L.sc.L ∧
  (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * L.etaSz ≤ L.de2 * D.R.m * L.sc.w'' D.R.n ∧
  L.etaSz ^ 2 * D.R.r * L.sc.L ≤ L.de2 * D.R.m * L.sc.w'' D.R.n ^ 2 ∧
  (D.R.r : ℝ) * (L.sc.b' + L.Sc) * Real.log L.sc.x * szlA L ≤ L.de2 * D.R.m * L.sc.b

def SizeNodes : Prop :=
  12 * L.sc.b / szlStar L + 2 * D.R.t + 8 ≤ L.etaSz / D.R.m ∧
  (D.R.k : ℝ) * D.R.t * L.sc.L ≤ L.etaSz / D.R.m ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * (D.R.r : ℝ) ^ 19 * (D.R.f : ℝ) ^ 5 * L.sc.L ≤ L.etaSz / D.R.m ∧
  4 * (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 3 + 2 * D.R.t + 6 ≤ L.etaSz / D.R.m ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * D.R.k * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ (9 / 2 : ℝ) ≤ L.etaSz / D.R.m ∧
  2 * ((D.R.r : ℝ) + D.R.m * D.R.t) ^ 3 + D.R.t + 3 ≤ L.etaSz / D.R.m ∧
  (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 10 ≤ L.etaSz / D.R.m

def SizeLstar : Prop :=
  L.sc.b' + L.Sc + L.etaSz + L.sc.b / szlStar L < szlStar L ∧
  2 * L.etaSz + D.R.t + 8 * L.sc.b / szlStar L + 7 < szlStar L

def SizeDel : Prop :=
  (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 10 ≤ (D.s.Del : ℝ) ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * D.R.m * D.R.N D.R.m ≤ (D.s.Del : ℝ) ∧
  Real.log (D.s.Del : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) / 2 ∧
  (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.Sc ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * D.R.m * D.R.k * (D.s.Del : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n ∧
  4 * L.Sc ≤ L.LaG * L.sc.b' / D.R.k ∧
  4 * L.sc.x ^ szTh L * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * (D.s.Del : ℝ) ≤ L.LaG * L.sc.b' ∧
  L.sc.x ^ szTh L * D.R.m * Real.log D.host.v ≤ D.R.k

def SizeW : Prop := Real.sqrt L.sc.b ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n

def SizeBm (_L : SizeData₂ D) : Prop :=
  ∀ τ (h : 0 < D.R.m), (((D.hc τ).b ⟨D.R.m - 1, by omega⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ) ≤ 2 * (((D.hc τ).b ⟨0, h⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ)

def SizeXpp : Prop :=
  ∀ j τ j' τ', 1 ≤ j → j ≤ D.R.m → 1 ≤ j' → j' ≤ D.R.m → wj D j τ ≤ L.sc.X'' D.R.n * wj D j' τ'

def SizeG : Prop :=
  ∀ i j : Fin 2, ((D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.b) ^ 8 ≤ (D.host.g i : ℝ) ∧ (D.host.g i : ℝ) ≤ 2 * (D.host.g j : ℝ) ∧
    Real.log (D.host.g i : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ szTh L * L.sc.L

def SizeTu : Prop := ∀ u, Real.log ((D.mem u).A.T : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ szTh L * D.R.m * L.sc.L

def SizePde : Prop :=
  Real.log (D.host.pde : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.L ∧ Real.log (D.host.pde' : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.L

def SizeWords (_L : SizeData₂ D) : Prop := @Fintype.card D.what₂.Word D.wordFin ≤ 2 ^ (D.R.k - 3)

def SizesAt : Prop :=
  SizeN L ∧ SizeM L ∧ SizeK L ∧ SizeKRel L ∧ SizeT L ∧ SizeCond L ∧ SizeQD L ∧ SizeTT L ∧
  SizeLa L ∧ SizeRho L ∧ SizeBot L ∧ SizeDe2 L ∧ SizeLw L ∧ SizeEta L ∧ SizeNodes L ∧
  SizeLstar L ∧ SizeDel L ∧ SizeW L ∧ SizeBm L ∧ SizeXpp L ∧ SizeG L ∧ SizePde L ∧
  SizeWords L ∧ 11 ≤ D.host.splitBound

theorem SizesAt.eleven_le {D : D7} {L : SizeData₂ D} (S : SizesAt L) : 11 ≤ D.host.splitBound :=
  S.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2

end Formalism.MainNotions



[LGS_family_data_03] Original ParameterChoice numerical hypotheses
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalRingCardinality/DataParams.lean#L62-L332
Range SHA256: d87d15d231e9323f359fcde506e665d58a92985df0987a843b019ebaa4dd47eb
structure ParameterChoice' (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) : Type where

  CkLow : ℝ
  CkLow_pos : 0 < CkLow

  Ck6 : ℝ
  Ck6_pos : 0 < Ck6

  CfL : ℝ
  CfL_pos : 0 < CfL

  cLL : ℝ
  cLL_pos : 0 < cLL

  CnW : ℝ
  CnW_pos : 0 < CnW

  Crho : ℝ
  Crho_pos : 0 < Crho

  CDelG : ℝ
  CDelG_pos : 0 < CDelG

  CLw : ℝ
  CLw_pos : 0 < CLw

  Cj0 : ℝ
  Cj0_pos : 0 < Cj0

  Cw : ℝ
  Cw_pos : 0 < Cw
  cw : ℝ
  cw_pos : 0 < cw

  CDel : ℝ
  CDel_pos : 0 < CDel

  CCount : ℝ
  CCount_pos : 0 < CCount

  Ch0r : ℝ
  Ch0r_pos : 0 < Ch0r

  Ch0k : ℝ
  Ch0k_pos : 0 < Ch0k

  Ch0N : ℝ
  Ch0N_pos : 0 < Ch0N

  Cgap : ℝ
  Cgap_pos : 0 < Cgap

  CetaF : ℝ
  CetaF_pos : 0 < CetaF

  CF : ℝ
  CF_pos : 0 < CF

  CY : ℝ
  CY_pos : 0 < CY
  CYL : ℝ
  CYL_pos : 0 < CYL

  Cpde : ℝ
  Cpde_pos : 0 < Cpde

  CccK : ℝ
  CccK_pos : 0 < CccK

  qd_pow : L.sc.x ^ (2 * L.Ct + 5) < (D.host.q : ℝ) ∧ L.sc.x ^ (2 * L.Ct + 5) < (D.host.d : ℝ)

  logq_ge : L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.ez / 6) ≤ Real.log D.host.q

  logDerivative_le_power : |L.logDerivativeAtOne| ≤ L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps

  k_low : CkLow * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ jG * D.R.f * L.sc.L * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps ≤ D.R.k

  k_up : (D.R.k : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ ((1 : ℝ) / 2) * D.R.n * R0 L

  radius_ge_sqrt_ratio : Real.sqrt ((D.R.k : ℝ) / D.R.m) ≤ R0 L

  k_ge_m2L : (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 2 * L.sc.L ≤ D.R.k

  k_ge_mx : (D.R.m : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ ((1 : ℝ) / 2) * Real.log L.sc.x ≤ D.R.k

  klogk : Ck6 * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 6 * L.sc.L ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) * Real.log D.R.k

  logf_le : Real.log D.R.f ≤ Real.sqrt D.R.rS

  log_sq_conductor_le : Real.log D.R.f ^ 2 ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 2 * L.sc.L

  loglogb : Real.log (Real.log L.sc.b) ≤ cLL * D.R.r * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r

  flogk : CfL * L.sc.L ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.log D.R.k

  eta_le : L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.b / ((D.R.t : ℝ) ^ 3 * D.R.f * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3)

  condE_le : (condE D : ℝ) ^ 36 * (D.R.t : ℝ) ^ 150 ≤ L.sc.b ^ ((1 : ℝ) / 2)

  TT_card_le : (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ≤ Nstar L

  rho_le : rhoP L Crho ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ)

  S_le : 4 * L.Sc ≤ L.LaG * L.sc.b' / D.R.k

  Del_le : 4 * CDelG * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * (D.s.Del : ℝ) ≤ L.LaG * L.sc.b'

  Lw_j : ∀ l : ℕ, 11 ≤ l → ∃ j : ℕ, lA L ≤ ((l : ℝ) - 1) ^ j ∧ 2 * j + 2 ≤ D.host.Lw

  Lw_le : (D.host.Lw : ℝ) ≤ CLw * Real.log L.sc.x

  jetIndex_le_log : (L.j0 : ℝ) ≤ Cj0 * Real.log L.sc.x

  bT_le : ∀ t, (L.bT t : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.b' + Nstar L * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps * L.LaG * L.sc.b' / D.R.k

  topPlace_first_bound : ∀ τ (t : D.host.TT) (hm : 0 < D.R.m),
    (D.eTau τ : D.R.GG) = D.R.tau1 t →
    (((D.hc τ).b ⟨0, hm⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ) = (L.bT t : ℝ) + (⌊L.sc.b' / L.sc.x⌋ : ℤ)

  lows_far : ∀ τ (i j : Fin D.R.m),
    2 * L.sc.b / lStar L + (D.R.t : ℝ) / 2 + 3 ≤ |(((D.hc τ).b i - (D.hc τ).e j : ℚ) : ℝ)|

  X''_ratio : ∀ j j' τ τ', 1 ≤ j → j ≤ D.R.m → 1 ≤ j' → j' ≤ D.R.m →
    wj D j τ ≤ L.sc.X'' D.R.n * wj D j' τ'

  cloud_place_bound : ∀ τ (h0 : 0 < D.R.k - 1), (D.eTau τ : D.R.GG) ∉ D.host.topPlaces →
    (((D.hc τ).u ⟨0, by omega⟩ - (D.hc τ).v ⟨0, h0⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ) ≤ 2 * L.Sc

  uk_extreme : ∀ τ (hk : 2 ≤ D.R.k), (D.eTau τ : D.R.GG) ∉ D.host.topPlaces →
    (D.hc τ).u ⟨D.R.k - 1, by omega⟩ = (D.hc τ).v ⟨D.R.k - 2, by omega⟩ - 1 / 2

  w''_ge : (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ Cw ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n ∧ L.sc.b ^ cw ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n

  Del_ge : (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ CDel * (CDel * D.R.m) ^ 3 / D.div.etaF ^ CDel ≤ (D.s.Del : ℝ) ∧
    CDel * D.R.m * D.R.N D.R.m ≤ (D.s.Del : ℝ) ∧
    (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ CDel * (CDel * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 4 * L.sc.L) ^ 4 ≤ (D.s.Del : ℝ) ∧
    (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 10 ≤ (D.s.Del : ℝ)

  Del_count : ((D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * D.R.f * L.sc.L) ^ CCount ≤ (D.s.Del : ℝ)

  logDel : Real.log (D.s.Del : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) / 2

  kS_le : (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.Sc ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n

  bm_le : ∀ τ (hm : 0 < D.R.m), (D.hc τ).b ⟨D.R.m - 1, by omega⟩ ≤ 2 * (D.hc τ).b ⟨0, hm⟩

  h0_ge : 12 * L.sc.b / lStar L + 2 * D.R.t + 8 ≤ h0 L ∧
    (D.R.k : ℝ) * D.R.t * L.sc.L ≤ h0 L ∧
    Ch0r * (D.R.r : ℝ) ^ 19 * (D.R.f : ℝ) ^ 5 * L.sc.L ≤ h0 L ∧
    ((D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * D.R.f * L.sc.L) ^ CCount ≤ h0 L ∧
    4 * (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 3 + 2 * D.R.t + 6 ≤ h0 L ∧
    Ch0k * D.R.k * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ ((9 : ℝ) / 2) ≤ h0 L ∧
    2 * (Mrt D : ℝ) ^ 3 + D.R.t + 3 ≤ h0 L ∧
    120 * (D.R.r : ℝ) * (qSig D : ℝ) ^ ((3 : ℝ) / 2) ≤ h0 L ∧
    (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 10 ≤ h0 L ∧
    16 * (qSig D : ℝ) * D.R.N D.R.m ≤ h0 L

  heightBound_ge_rank_cubic : (D.R.t : ℝ) + 2 * ((D.R.k : ℝ) + D.R.m * (D.R.t + (D.R.r + 6) + 1)) ^ 3 + 1 ≤ h0 L

  heightBound_ge_degree_power : (D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) ^ Ch0N ≤ h0 L

  em_win : ∀ τ (hm : 0 < D.R.m),
    |((((D.hc τ).e ⟨D.R.m - 1, by omega⟩ - (D.hc τ).b ⟨D.R.m - 1, by omega⟩ : ℚ)) : ℝ) - h0 L / 2| ≤ 2

  nodes_gap : ∀ τ (j : ℕ) (h : j + 1 < D.R.m),
    h0 L ≤ (((D.hc τ).b ⟨j + 1, h⟩ - (D.hc τ).b ⟨j, by omega⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ) ∧
    (((D.hc τ).b ⟨j + 1, h⟩ - (D.hc τ).b ⟨j, by omega⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ) ≤ h0 L + 1

  e_centred : ∀ τ (j : Fin D.R.m), |((((D.hc τ).e j - (D.hc τ).b j : ℚ)) : ℝ) - h0 L / 2| ≤ 2

  sig_gaps : ∀ τ,
    (∀ (i : ℕ) (h : i + 1 < D.R.k - 1),
      gapS D Cgap < (((D.hc τ).v ⟨i, by omega⟩ - (D.hc τ).v ⟨i + 1, h⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ)) ∧
    (∀ (hk : 2 ≤ D.R.k) (hm : 0 < D.R.m),
      gapS D Cgap < (((D.hc τ).v ⟨D.R.k - 2, by omega⟩ + (D.hc τ).b ⟨0, hm⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ)) ∧
    (∀ (j : ℕ) (h : j + 1 < D.R.m),
      gapS D Cgap < (((D.hc τ).b ⟨j + 1, h⟩ - (D.hc τ).b ⟨j, by omega⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ))

  nW_ge : (4 * (D.R.f : ℝ) + 10) * D.R.r ≤ nW L CnW

  logeta : Real.log L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4)

  t_le_eta : (D.R.t : ℝ) ≤ L.etaSz

  teta : (D.R.t : ℝ) * L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n / L.sc.x

  mteta : (D.R.m : ℝ) * ((D.R.t : ℝ) + 1) * L.etaSz ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n

  mSeta : (D.R.m : ℝ) * (L.Sc + L.etaSz) ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n * L.sc.L

  Neta : (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * L.etaSz ≤ L.de2 * D.R.m * L.sc.w'' D.R.n

  eta2 : L.etaSz ^ 2 * D.R.r * L.sc.L ≤ L.de2 * D.R.m * L.sc.w'' D.R.n ^ 2

  rbS : (D.R.r : ℝ) * (L.sc.b' + L.Sc) * Real.log L.sc.x * lA L ≤ L.de2 * D.R.m * L.sc.b

  lstar1 : L.sc.b' + L.Sc + L.etaSz + L.sc.b / lStar L < lStar L

  lstar2 : 2 * L.etaSz + D.R.t + 8 * L.sc.b / lStar L + 7 < lStar L

  words_le : Fintype.card D.what₂.Word ≤ 2 ^ (D.R.k - 3)

  etaF_eq : D.div.etaF = CetaF * L.sc.x ^ (-CF)

  g_Y : ∃ Y : ℝ, ((D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.b) ^ 8 ≤ Y ∧
    (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 4 * (condE D : ℝ) ^ CY ≤ Y ^ ((1 : ℝ) / 4) ∧ Real.log Y ≤ CYL * L.sc.L ∧
    ∀ i, Y ≤ (D.host.g i : ℝ) ∧ (D.host.g i : ℝ) ≤ 2 * Y

  g_norm : ∀ i, ∃ π : 𝓞 D.host.E, (π : D.host.E) ∈ D.host.K ∧
    (π : D.host.E) * D.host.conj (π : D.host.E) = (D.host.g i : D.host.E)

  g_mod : ∀ i, D.host.g i % condE D = 1

  pde_mod : D.host.pde % 15 = 1 ∧ D.host.pde' % 15 = 1

  pde_split : (Ideal.primesOver (Ideal.span {(D.host.pde : ℤ)}) (𝓞 D.host.E)).ncard =
      Module.finrank ℚ D.host.E ∧
    (Ideal.primesOver (Ideal.span {(D.host.pde' : ℤ)}) (𝓞 D.host.E)).ncard = Module.finrank ℚ D.host.E

  pde_ne : ∀ i, D.host.pde ≠ D.host.g i ∧ D.host.pde' ≠ D.host.g i

  pde_log : Real.log D.host.pde ≤ L.sc.L ∧ Real.log D.host.pde' ≤ L.sc.L

  level_split : ∀ p ∈ D.host.levelPrimes, D.host.cls p = .splitK

  ccK_log : Real.log (Ideal.absNorm D.host.ccK) ≤ L.sc.L

  e_mu : ∀ j : Fin D.R.m, D.host.MuCond (fun τ => (D.eTau τ : D.R.GG)) (fun τ => (D.hc τ).e j)

  ccK_le : (Ideal.absNorm D.host.ccK : ℝ) ≤ CccK * D.host.d

namespace ParameterChoice'

variable {D : D7} {L : SizeData₂ D}

def SgWin (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (C0 : ℝ) : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ (-P.CF) / C0 ^ 2 ≤ sgS D ∧ sgS D ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-P.CF)

theorem bT_ge_half (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (τ : D.what₂.Tau) (t : D.host.TT) (hm : 0 < D.R.m)
    (ht : (D.eTau τ : D.R.GG) = D.R.tau1 t) :
    (((D.hc τ).b ⟨0, hm⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ) / 2 ≤ (L.bT t : ℝ) := by
  rw [P.topPlace_first_bound τ t hm ht]
  have hb := L.bT_ge t
  have hx : 1 < L.sc.x := L.sc.one_lt_alt
  have hbpos : 0 < L.sc.b' := by
    unfold Scale.b' Scale.a Scale.X' Scale.b
    have : 0 < L.sc.x := by linarith
    positivity
  have hfl : ((⌊L.sc.b' / L.sc.x⌋ : ℤ) : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.b' / L.sc.x := Int.floor_le _
  have hdiv : L.sc.b' / L.sc.x ≤ L.sc.b' := div_le_self hbpos.le hx.le
  linarith

end ParameterChoice'

theorem f_le_m_sq_n (R : Ranks) : R.f ≤ R.m ^ 2 * R.n := by
  unfold Ranks.f
  have h := R.two_le_m
  have : 4 ≤ R.m ^ 2 := by nlinarith
  nlinarith

theorem f_le_r_sq (R : Ranks) : R.f ≤ R.r ^ 2 := by
  have h1 := R.f_le_k
  have h2 := R.two_le_m
  have h3 : R.k ≤ R.r := by unfold Ranks.r; omega
  have h4 : R.r ≤ R.r ^ 2 := Nat.le_self_pow (by norm_num) _
  have h5 : R.f = 2 * R.ord := rfl
  omega

end Formalism.BasicNotions

end


[LGS_family_data_04] EpTie, NuUnserved, Extra and geometric ker field; ep_def is retained

def EpTie {D : D7} {L : SizeData₂ D} (X : G.Inst D L) : Prop :=
  ∀ u w hg, (D.gat u w hg).e = X.ep (D.what₂.places.resChar w)

def NuUnserved {D : D7} {L : SizeData₂ D} (X : G.Inst D L) (K : G.Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ u w, D.what₂.places.resChar w ≠ (D.mem u).A.T →
    G.VV X K (G.aL L) (G.bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) = ∅ →
    DV.LocEq (D.plEq w) (L.NuLat u) (DV.MramL D u)

def FillsAll {D : D7} (hT : GatTies D) (Y : G.LatInst D) : Prop :=
  ∀ u w hg (v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV) (s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v)), ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D,
    ((Y.Fl u w hg).w v j s : ℝ) = wj D j (hT.gat_pos u w hg ⟨v, s⟩) - G.kaJ D j

structure ExtraC where
  Ce : ℝ
  err : ErrC

structure Extra (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (hT : GatTies D) (Gi : G.Inst D L) (Gl : G.LatInst D)
    (K : G.Consts) (X : DivX D) (cX : ExtraC) where

  lev : LevelData D

  th : ThetaX D

  th_io : th.TieIo

  cmp : LatCmp D

  lat : LatDefs D L Gi Gl K cmp

  tw : TwForms D

  ker : ∀ u w (h : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    haveI : Fact (D.what₂.places.resChar w).Prime := ⟨D.what₂.places.resChar_prime w⟩
    LocalToGlobal₂ D hT cmp tw u w h (Gl.Ch u w h)

  ep_def : G.EpDef Gi cX.Ce
  ep_tie : EpTie Gi
  kapI_def : G.KapIDef Gi K
  fills_all : FillsAll hT Gl
  nu_unserved : NuUnserved Gi K

  idx : IdxDefs D X
  kap_def : DV.KapDef D
  lam_def : DV.LamDef D

  kapR_def : DV.KapRBase X


[LGS_family_data_05] Original fifteen coefficients, scales, windows and band radii
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/IsResidueClassZero.lean#L14-L75
Range SHA256: 9b57c5887f094ea11c5d7999ae9880ce46615a90adc3f75f433547c45998fa4e
structure Consts where
  La : ℝ
  rho : ℝ
  kII : ℝ
  tII : ℝ
  DII : ℝ
  szII : ℝ
  kII' : ℝ
  DI : ℝ
  kappaThresholdOne : ℝ
  kappaThresholdTwo : ℝ
  kI : ℝ
  dl1 : ℝ
  dl2 : ℝ
  Pr1 : ℝ
  CN : ℝ

def Consts.Nonneg (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  0 ≤ K.La ∧ 0 ≤ K.rho ∧ 0 ≤ K.kII ∧ 0 ≤ K.tII ∧ 0 ≤ K.DII ∧ 0 ≤ K.szII ∧ 0 ≤ K.kII' ∧ 0 ≤ K.DI ∧
    0 ≤ K.kappaThresholdOne ∧ 0 ≤ K.kappaThresholdTwo ∧ 0 ≤ K.kI ∧ 0 ≤ K.dl1 ∧ 0 ≤ K.dl2 ∧ 0 ≤ K.Pr1 ∧ 0 ≤ K.CN

abbrev HH (D : D7) : Type := D.R.A

def hG (s : Scale) : ℝ := 2 + 8 * s.eps

def KG (s : Scale) : ℕ := ⌈8 * (hG s + 2) / s.eps⌉₊

def rG (s : Scale) : ℕ := KG s + 1

def N1 (s : Scale) : ℕ := orbitWindowCount (rG s) s.delta1 (hG s) s.x

def Gp (s : Scale) : ℕ := goodG' (rG s) s.delta1 (hG s) s.x

def cG {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) : ℝ := 4 * L.Sc

def Nm (D : D7) : ℝ := (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ)

def L1 (D : D7) (s : Scale) (p : ℕ) : ℕ := ⌈Real.logb p (((D.R.k : ℝ) * s.x ^ Scale.A) ^ 4)⌉₊

def L2 (D : D7) (s : Scale) : ℕ :=
  2 * sSup {l | ∃ p : ℕ, p.Prime ∧ D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass ∧ l = L1 D s p}

def Nstar (D : D7) (s : Scale) : ℝ := 2 * (D.R.n : ℝ) * s.delta1

def La (K : Consts) (s : Scale) : ℝ := K.La * (N1 s : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.log s.a

def rho (D : D7) (K : Consts) (s : Scale) : ℝ :=
  4 * Nstar D s * s.x ^ s.eps * La K s / D.R.k + K.rho * (N1 s : ℝ) * La K s / D.R.k

def rhoB (D : D7) (K : Consts) (s : Scale) : ℝ :=
  4 * Nstar D s * s.x ^ s.eps * La K s / D.R.k + K.rho * K.CN * Real.log s.x * La K s / D.R.k

def CbLarge (K : Consts) (s : Scale) : Prop := (N1 s : ℝ) ≤ K.CN * Real.log s.x

theorem rho_le_rhoB (D : D7) (K : Consts) (s : Scale) (hr : 0 ≤ K.rho) (hLa : 0 ≤ La K s) (h : CbLarge K s) :
    rho D K s ≤ rhoB D K s := by
  unfold rho rhoB
  have hk : (0 : ℝ) ≤ D.R.k := Nat.cast_nonneg _
  have h1 : K.rho * (N1 s : ℝ) * La K s ≤ K.rho * (K.CN * Real.log s.x) * La K s := by
    apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right _ hLa
    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h hr
  have h2 := div_le_div_of_nonneg_right h1 hk


[LGS_model_sandwich_01] HidaSetting and actual lattice/model definitions
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/UnramifiedQuadraticSetting.lean#L12-L84
Range SHA256: 31dc3b034de4ba65daaae136645a5efadd5093bd50029f02027bc72efdfb2d1d
structure HidaSetting (p n' : ℕ) (W Qq O Kf : Type) [CommRing W] [IsDomain W] [IsDiscreteValuationRing W] [Field Qq]
    [Algebra W Qq] [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (ι : Qq →+* Kf) (σ : Qq ≃+* Qq) (j : W →+* O) (w : O) : Prop where
  prime : p.Prime
  five_le : 5 ≤ p
  two_le : 2 ≤ n'
  even : Even n'
  frac_alt : IsFractionRing W Qq
  frac_O : IsFractionRing O Kf
  p_unif : Irreducible (p : W)
  res_card : Nat.card (W ⧸ Ideal.span {(p : W)}) = p ^ n'
  eq_sum_herm_smul_frame_of_inW : IsAdicComplete (Ideal.span {(p : W)}) W
  frob : ∀ x : W, ∃ y : W, σ (algebraMap W Qq x) = algebraMap W Qq (x ^ p + (p : W) * y)
  period : σ ^ n' = 1
  ι_comp : ∀ x : W, ι (algebraMap W Qq x) = algebraMap O Kf (j x)
  finite : j.Finite
  complete_O : IsAdicComplete (IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal O) O
  w_sq : w ^ 2 = (p : O)

def UnramifiedQuadraticSetting (p n' : ℕ) (W Qq O Kf : Type) [CommRing W] [IsDomain W] [IsDiscreteValuationRing W] [Field Qq]
    [Algebra W Qq] [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (ι : Qq →+* Kf) (σ : Qq ≃+* Qq) (j : W →+* O) (w : O) : Prop :=
  HidaSetting p n' W Qq O Kf ι σ j w ∧ Irreducible w ∧ Nat.card (O ⧸ Ideal.span {w}) = p ^ (2 * n') ∧
    ∀ z : ℤ, ∃ y : O, y ^ 2 = (z : O)

def colSpan (R : Type*) {K : Type*} [CommRing R] [Field K] [Algebra R K] {k : ℕ}
    (g : Matrix (Fin k) (Fin k) K) : Submodule R (Fin k → K) :=
  Submodule.span R (Set.range fun c : Fin k => fun i => g i c)

def IsLattice (R : Type*) {K : Type*} [CommRing R] [Field K] [Algebra R K] {k : ℕ}
    (L : Submodule R (Fin k → K)) : Prop :=
  ∃ g : Matrix (Fin k) (Fin k) K, IsUnit g.det ∧ L = colSpan R g

def twistVec {Qq Kf : Type*} [Field Qq] [Field Kf] (ι : Qq →+* Kf) (σ : Qq ≃+* Qq) (s : ℕ) {k : ℕ}
    (v : Fin k → Qq) : Fin k → Kf :=
  fun i => ι ((σ ^ s) (v i))

def extO (O : Type*) {W Qq Kf : Type*} [CommRing W] [Field Qq] [Algebra W Qq] [CommRing O] [Field Kf]
    [Algebra O Kf] (ι : Qq →+* Kf) (σ : Qq ≃+* Qq) (s : ℕ) {k : ℕ} (L : Submodule W (Fin k → Qq)) :
    Submodule O (Fin k → Kf) :=
  Submodule.span O (twistVec ι σ s '' (L : Set (Fin k → Qq)))

def IsTypeV {n' : ℕ} (s : Fin n') : Prop := Even s.val

def posAdd {n' : ℕ} (s : Fin n') (e : ℕ) : Fin n' :=
  ⟨(s.val + e) % n', Nat.mod_lt _ (Nat.lt_of_le_of_lt (Nat.zero_le _) s.isLt)⟩

def IsBaseWeight {n' m : ℕ} (TT : Finset (Fin n')) (a b : Fin n' → ℤ) (c : ℕ)
    (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) : Prop :=
  ∀ s, (s ∈ TT → lam0 s 0 = a s ∧ lam0 s (Fin.last m) = -b s ∧
      ∀ i : Fin (m + 1), 0 < i.val → i.val < m → |lam0 s i| ≤ c) ∧
    (s ∉ TT → ∀ i, |lam0 s i| ≤ c)

def IsDominant {n' m : ℕ} (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) : Prop := ∀ s, Antitone (lam0 s)

def lamAt {n' m : ℕ} (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) (s : Fin n') : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ :=
  if Even s.val then lam0 s else fun i => -lam0 s i.rev

def H0 (O : Type*) {Kf : Type*} [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf] (m : ℕ) (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ)
    (Λ : Submodule O (Fin (m + 1) → Kf)) : Submodule O (MvPolynomial (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1)) Kf) :=
  if Antitone lam then
    ⨅ (g : Matrix (Fin (m + 1)) (Fin (m + 1)) Kf) (_ : IsUnit g.det ∧ Λ = colSpan O g), nablaLTw O Kf m lam g
  else ⊥

def Hten (O : Type*) {Kf : Type*} [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf] {n' m : ℕ}
    (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) (Λ : Fin n' → Submodule O (Fin (m + 1) → Kf)) :
    Submodule O (MvPolynomial (Fin n' × (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1))) Kf) :=
  tensorLat O Kf fun s => H0 O m (lamAt lam0 s) (Λ s)

def nuOf {m : ℕ} (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) : Fin (m + 1) → ℕ :=
  fun t => (lam t - lam (Fin.last m)).toNat

def Vsp (Kf : Type*) [Field Kf] {n' m : ℕ} (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) :


[LGS_model_sandwich_02] wExp, WeylSandwichG, ExpBoundG, model sandwich and tensor assembly
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/Joints.lean#L1462-L1578
Range SHA256: 104956e7de0dad448d8308e22f9bdd12b891a0959aa4b5759b1da4f9280f544d
def wExp (p m : ℕ) (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) : ℕ := N0 (m + 1) * deF p (m + 1) (ellWt lam)

def WeylSandwichG (D : D7) : Prop :=
  ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    ∀ (m : ℕ) (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) (Λ : Submodule (D.gat u w hg).O (Fin (m + 1) → (D.gat u w hg).Kf)),
      (∀ x ∈ H0 (D.gat u w hg).O m lam Λ,
        (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^ wExp (D.what₂.places.resChar w) m lam) • x ∈
          Dl0 (D.gat u w hg).O m lam Λ) ∧
      Dl0 (D.gat u w hg).O m lam Λ ≤ H0 (D.gat u w hg).O m lam Λ

def ExpBoundG (D : D7) {L : SizeData₂ D} (X : Inst D L) : Prop :=
  ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    ∑ v, ∑ s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v),
      wExp (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (D.gat u w hg).P.m (lamAt ((D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v) s) ≤
        X.ep (D.what₂.places.resChar w)

theorem fk_logb_ge_one (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (hpa : (p : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.a) :
    1 ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a := by
  have hp1 : (1 : ℝ) < p := by exact_mod_cast hp.one_lt
  have hlog : 1 ≤ Real.logb p L.sc.a := by
    rw [Real.le_logb_iff_rpow_le hp1 (by linarith), Real.rpow_one]; exact hpa
  have hf : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) := by
    have h1 := D.R.one_le_n
    have : 1 ≤ D.R.f := by unfold Formalism.BasicNotions.Ranks.f; omega
    exact_mod_cast this
  have hk : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 := by
    have h5 := D.R.five_le_k
    have : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast (by omega : 1 ≤ D.R.k)
    nlinarith
  calc (1 : ℝ) = 1 * 1 * 1 := by ring
    _ ≤ _ := mul_le_mul (mul_le_mul hf hk zero_le_one (by linarith)) hlog zero_le_one (by positivity)

theorem weyl_sandwich_of_factors (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (Ce : ℝ) (hCe : 0 ≤ Ce) (hEp : EpDef X Ce)
    (hW : WeylSandwichG D) (hE : ExpBoundG D X) : Formalism.MainNotions.AuxBlock8.adm9 D L X Ce := by
  refine ⟨fun p hp hpa => hEp.le p hCe (fk_logb_ge_one D L p hp hpa), fun u w hg _ => ?_⟩

  have hv : ∀ (L : (D.gat u w hg).P.Tup) (v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV), ∀ x ∈ Hten (D.gat u w hg).O ((D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v) (fun s => extO (D.gat u w hg).O ((D.gat u w hg).P.ι v) ((D.gat u w hg).P.σ v) s.val (L v)),
      (∏ s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v), (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^ wExp (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (D.gat u w hg).P.m (lamAt ((D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v) s))) • x ∈
        HtenW (D.gat u w hg).O ((D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v) (fun s => extO (D.gat u w hg).O ((D.gat u w hg).P.ι v) ((D.gat u w hg).P.σ v) s.val (L v)) := by
    intro L v
    exact tensorLat_smul _ _ _ fun s x hx => (hW u w hg (D.gat u w hg).P.m _ _).1 x hx
  have hv' : ∀ (L : (D.gat u w hg).P.Tup) (v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV), ∀ x ∈ HtenW (D.gat u w hg).O ((D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v) (fun s => extO (D.gat u w hg).O ((D.gat u w hg).P.ι v) ((D.gat u w hg).P.σ v) s.val (L v)),
      (∏ _s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v), (1 : (D.gat u w hg).O)) • x ∈
        Hten (D.gat u w hg).O ((D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v) (fun s => extO (D.gat u w hg).O ((D.gat u w hg).P.ι v) ((D.gat u w hg).P.σ v) s.val (L v)) := by
    intro L v
    exact tensorLat_smul _ _ _ fun s x hx => by rw [one_smul]; exact (hW u w hg (D.gat u w hg).P.m _ _).2 hx
  have hT : ∀ L : (D.gat u w hg).P.Tup, ∀ x ∈ (D.gat u w hg).P.Htup L,
      (∏ v, ∏ s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v), (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^ wExp (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (D.gat u w hg).P.m (lamAt ((D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v) s))) • x ∈ (D.gat u w hg).P.HtupW L :=
    fun L => tenV_smul (D.gat u w hg).P _ _ _ fun v x hx => hv L v x hx
  have hT' : ∀ L : (D.gat u w hg).P.Tup, ∀ x ∈ (D.gat u w hg).P.HtupW L, (∏ _v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV, ∏ _s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' _v), (1 : (D.gat u w hg).O)) • x ∈ (D.gat u w hg).P.Htup L :=
    fun L => tenV_smul (D.gat u w hg).P _ _ _ fun v x hx => hv' L v x hx
  have hprod : (∏ v, ∏ s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v), (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^ wExp (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (D.gat u w hg).P.m (lamAt ((D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v) s))) =
      ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^ (∑ v, ∑ s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v), wExp (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (D.gat u w hg).P.m (lamAt ((D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v) s)) := by
    simp only [Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum]
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_add_of_le (hE u w hg)
  refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
  · rintro _ ⟨F, hF, rfl⟩
    simp only [LinearMap.smul_apply, LinearMap.id_apply]
    show (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^ X.ep (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) • F ∈ (D.gat u w hg).M
    rw [hk, pow_add, mul_comm, mul_smul]
    have : (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^ (∑ v, ∑ s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v), wExp (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (D.gat u w hg).P.m (lamAt ((D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v) s))) • F ∈ (D.gat u w hg).M := by
      rw [GAtP.M, GAtP.funMod, Submodule.mem_pi]
      intro i _
      rw [← hprod]
      exact funMod_smul ((D.gat u w hg).lev i) _ _ _ (fun L _ x hx => hT L x hx) (F i) ((Submodule.mem_pi.1 hF) i (Set.mem_univ _))
    exact Submodule.smul_mem _ _ this
  · intro F hF
    rw [GAtP.Mna, GAtP.funMod, Submodule.mem_pi]
    intro i _
    have := funMod_smul ((D.gat u w hg).lev i) _ _ (∏ _v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV, ∏ _s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' _v), (1 : (D.gat u w hg).O)) (fun L _ x hx => hT' L x hx)
      (F i) ((Submodule.mem_pi.1 hF) i (Set.mem_univ _))
    simpa using this

def ModelSandwichG (D : D7) : Prop :=
  ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w))
    (m : ℕ) (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ), Antitone lam →
    (∀ x ∈ Submodule.map (MvPolynomial.mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf)).toLinearMap
        (dualWeyl (D.gat u w hg).O (m + 1) (m + 1) (nuOf lam)),
      (((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^ wExp (D.what₂.places.resChar w) m lam) • x ∈
        Submodule.map (MvPolynomial.mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf)).toLinearMap
          (weyl (D.gat u w hg).O (m + 1) (m + 1) le_rfl (nuOf lam))) ∧
    Submodule.map (MvPolynomial.mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf)).toLinearMap
        (weyl (D.gat u w hg).O (m + 1) (m + 1) le_rfl (nuOf lam)) ≤
      Submodule.map (MvPolynomial.mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf)).toLinearMap
        (dualWeyl (D.gat u w hg).O (m + 1) (m + 1) (nuOf lam))

theorem weylSandwich_of_model (D : D7) (h : ModelSandwichG D) : WeylSandwichG D := by
  intro u w hg m lam Λ
  by_cases hlam : Antitone lam
  · obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := h u w hg m lam hlam
    unfold H0 Dl0
    rw [if_pos hlam, if_pos hlam]
    constructor
    · intro x hx
      rw [Submodule.mem_iInf] at hx ⊢
      intro g
      have hx' := hx g
      rw [Submodule.mem_iInf] at hx' ⊢
      intro hgL
      obtain ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩ := hx' hgL
      refine ⟨(((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : (D.gat u w hg).O) ^ wExp (D.what₂.places.resChar w) m lam) • y, ?_, ?_⟩
      · unfold Formalism.GradedWeylDivisibility.nablaL at hy
        unfold deltaL
        rw [SetLike.mem_coe, Submodule.mem_comap, LinearMap.map_smul]
        exact h1 _ (Submodule.mem_comap.1 hy)
      · simp only [LinearMap.coe_restrictScalars, LinearMap.lsmul_apply]
        rw [smul_comm]
    · intro x hx
      rw [Submodule.mem_iInf] at hx ⊢
      intro g
      have hx' := hx g
      rw [Submodule.mem_iInf] at hx' ⊢
      intro hgL
      obtain ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩ := hx' hgL
      refine ⟨y, ?_, rfl⟩
      unfold deltaL at hy
      unfold Formalism.GradedWeylDivisibility.nablaL


[LGS_model_sandwich_03] Primitive polynomial normalization, standard-model comparison and actual Hida-setting transport
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/GroupLattices/FockModels/Sandwich.lean#L42-L218

      ∃ z0 ∈ weyl O k k le_rfl ν, (y : PK (KK O) k) = map (algebraMap O (KK O)) z0 := by
  intro y hy

  | zero => exact ⟨0, Submodule.zero_mem _, by simp⟩

theorem top_coord [CharZero (KK O)] (hν : Antitone ν) (y0 : MvPolynomial (Fin k × Fin k) O)
    (hy0 : y0 ∈ dualWeyl O k k ν) :
    ∃ c : O, ∃ z ∈ otherWts (EV (KK O) k ν) (fun a => (ν a : ℤ)),
      (z : PK (KK O) k) = map (algebraMap O (KK O)) y0 - algebraMap O (KK O) c • hw (KK O) le_rfl ν := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨b, hb, -⟩ := sSBasis O k k ν
  rw [← hb.2.1] at hy0
  induction hy0 using Submodule.span_induction with
  | mem x hx =>
    obtain ⟨i, rfl⟩ := hx
    have hbi : b i ∈ dualWeyl O k k ν := by rw [← hb.2.1]; exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨i, rfl⟩
    have hK := dualWeylMap O (KK O) (algebraMap O (KK O)) k k ν (b i) hbi
    have hh := sw_hom_map (algebraMap O (KK O)) (rowW k k) _ (b i) (hb.2.2 i)
    by_cases hi : rowWt i.1 = ν
    · refine ⟨eval (idPoint O k k) (b i), 0, Submodule.zero_mem _, ?_⟩
      have h1 := sw_line (KK O) le_rfl ν hν _ hK (by rw [rowNu_self, ← hi]; exact hh)
      have h2 : eval (idPoint (KK O) k k) (map (algebraMap O (KK O)) (b i)) =
          algebraMap O (KK O) (eval (idPoint O k k) (b i)) := by
        rw [map_eval]
        congr 2
        funext v
        simp only [idPoint, Function.comp]
        split_ifs <;> simp
      rw [h2] at h1
      rw [← h1, sub_self]
      rfl
    · refine ⟨0, ⟨_, hK⟩, ?_, by simp⟩
      refine Submodule.mem_iSup_of_mem (fun a => (rowWt i.1 a : ℤ)) (Submodule.mem_iSup_of_mem ?_ (wt_mem _ _ hh))
      intro h
      apply hi
      funext a
      have := congrFun h a
      exact_mod_cast this
  | zero => exact ⟨0, 0, Submodule.zero_mem _, by simp⟩
  | add x y _ _ hx hy =>
    obtain ⟨c1, z1, h1, e1⟩ := hx
    obtain ⟨c2, z2, h2, e2⟩ := hy
    refine ⟨c1 + c2, z1 + z2, Submodule.add_mem _ h1 h2, ?_⟩
    rw [Submodule.coe_add, e1, e2, map_add, map_add, add_smul]
    abel
  | smul a x _ hx =>
    obtain ⟨c1, z1, h1, e1⟩ := hx
    refine ⟨a * c1, algebraMap O (KK O) a • z1, Submodule.smul_mem _ _ h1, ?_⟩
    rw [Submodule.coe_smul, e1, sw_map_smul, map_mul, smul_sub, smul_smul, algebraMap_smul]

theorem nabla_mem [CharZero (KK O)] (hν : Antitone ν) (x0 : MvPolynomial (Fin k × Fin k) O)
    (hx0 : x0 ∈ dualWeyl O k k ν) :
    (⟨map (algebraMap O (KK O)) x0, dualWeylMap O (KK O) (algebraMap O (KK O)) k k ν x0 hx0⟩ : VK (KK O) k ν) ∈
      Nabla (O := O) (EV (KK O) k ν) (fun a => (ν a : ℤ)) (hwV (KK O) k ν hν) := by
  intro u hu
  obtain ⟨w, hwu⟩ := rel_mon (K := KK O) (k := k) (ν := ν) u hu
  have hy0 : applyWord O w x0 ∈ dualWeyl O k k ν := sw_applyWord_mem O ν w x0 hx0
  obtain ⟨c, z, hz, hzeq⟩ := top_coord O hν (applyWord O w x0) hy0
  refine ⟨c, ?_⟩
  have e : u ⟨map (algebraMap O (KK O)) x0, dualWeylMap O (KK O) (algebraMap O (KK O)) k k ν x0 hx0⟩ -
      (algebraMap O (KK O) c) • hwV (KK O) k ν hν = z := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    rw [Submodule.coe_sub, Submodule.coe_smul, hwu, hzeq, ← lclosedeq_applyWord_map]
    rfl
  rw [e]
  exact hz

end Lat

theorem sandwichO (D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) {p n' : ℕ} (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O]
    (S : UniformizedDVR p O) (hG : AdmissibleDVR p n' O) (m : ℕ) (ν : Fin (m + 1) → ℕ) (hν : Antitone ν) :
    ∀ x0 ∈ dualWeyl O (m + 1) (m + 1) ν,
      ((p : O) ^ (N0 (m + 1) * deF p (m + 1) (ellWt (fun a => (ν a : ℤ))))) • x0 ∈
        weyl O (m + 1) (m + 1) le_rfl ν := by
  intro x0 hx0
  haveI : CharZero (KK O) := Formalism.MainNotions.G.GLWeightTheory.dvr_fraction_field_char_zero_prime S hG.prime
  have hT := (Formalism.MainNotions.G.GLWeightTheory.nabla_delta_comparison_field D p n' O S hG (m + 1) (fun a => (ν a : ℤ))
    (VK (KK O) (m + 1) ν) (EV (KK O) (m + 1) ν) (hwV (KK O) (m + 1) ν hν) (isIrrHW hν)).1
  have h1 := hT _ (nabla_mem O hν x0 hx0)
  obtain ⟨z0, hz0, hz⟩ := delta_le O hν _ h1
  have hinj : Function.Injective
      (MvPolynomial.map (algebraMap O (KK O)) : MvPolynomial (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1)) O → _) :=
    MvPolynomial.map_injective _ (IsFractionRing.injective O (KK O))
  have he : ((p : O) ^ (N0 (m + 1) * deF p (m + 1) (ellWt (fun a => (ν a : ℤ))))) • x0 = z0 := by
    apply hinj
    rw [← hz, sw_map_smul]
    rfl
  rw [he]
  exact hz0

section Set

variable {p n' : ℕ} {W Qq O Kf : Type} [CommRing W] [IsDomain W] [IsDiscreteValuationRing W] [Field Qq]
  [Algebra W Qq] [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
  {ι : Qq →+* Kf} {σ : Qq ≃+* Qq} {j : W →+* O} {w : O}

def dvrSettingOfSetting (h : HidaSetting p n' W Qq O Kf ι σ j w) : UniformizedDVR p O where
  w := Classical.choose (IsDiscreteValuationRing.exists_irreducible O)
  w_irr := Classical.choose_spec (IsDiscreteValuationRing.exists_irreducible O)
  p_mem := by
    rw [IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal, mem_nonunits_iff]
    exact ja_p_nonunit (mkG h 0 0 (fun _ _ => 0) (fun _ _ _ _ => le_rfl)) (0 : Fin 1)
  p_ne := by
    intro h0
    apply kp_p_ne_zero (mkG h 0 0 (fun _ _ => 0) (fun _ _ _ _ => le_rfl)) (0 : Fin 1)
    show algebraMap O Kf (p : O) = 0
    rw [h0, map_zero]
  complete := h.complete_O

theorem good_ring_of_setting (h : HidaSetting p n' W Qq O Kf ι σ j w) : AdmissibleDVR p n' O := by
  refine ⟨h.prime, h.five_le, h.two_le, h.even, ?_⟩
  have hmax : (Ideal.span {(p : W)}).IsMaximal :=
    PrincipalIdealRing.isMaximal_of_irreducible h.p_unif
  letI : Field (W ⧸ Ideal.span {(p : W)}) := Ideal.Quotient.field _
  have hker : Ideal.span {(p : W)} ≤ RingHom.ker ((IsLocalRing.residue O).comp j) := by
    rw [Ideal.span_le, Set.singleton_subset_iff, SetLike.mem_coe, RingHom.mem_ker,
      RingHom.comp_apply, map_natCast, IsLocalRing.residue_eq_zero_iff]
    exact (dvrSettingOfSetting h).p_mem
  let f : W ⧸ Ideal.span {(p : W)} →+* FF O := Ideal.Quotient.lift _ ((IsLocalRing.residue O).comp j) hker
  refine ⟨f.fieldRange, ?_⟩
  rw [← h.res_card, ← Nat.card_range_of_injective f.injective]
  exact Nat.card_congr (Equiv.subtypeEquivRight fun y => by simp [RingHom.mem_fieldRange, Set.mem_range])

theorem ellWt_nuOf (m : ℕ) (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) :
    ellWt (fun a => (nuOf lam a : ℤ)) = ellWt lam := by
  have hl : lastIdx (m + 1) = Fin.last m := by
    ext
    simp [lastIdx]
  simp [ellWt, nuOf, hl]
  omega

theorem sandwich (D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) (h : HidaSetting p n' W Qq O Kf ι σ j w) (m : ℕ) (lam : Fin (m + 1) → ℤ)
    (hlam : Antitone lam) :
    (∀ x ∈ Submodule.map (MvPolynomial.mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId O Kf)).toLinearMap
        (dualWeyl O (m + 1) (m + 1) (nuOf lam)),
      ((p : O) ^ Formalism.MainNotions.G.Joints.wExp p m lam) • x ∈
        Submodule.map (MvPolynomial.mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId O Kf)).toLinearMap
          (weyl O (m + 1) (m + 1) le_rfl (nuOf lam))) ∧
    Submodule.map (MvPolynomial.mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId O Kf)).toLinearMap
        (weyl O (m + 1) (m + 1) le_rfl (nuOf lam)) ≤
      Submodule.map (MvPolynomial.mapAlgHom (Algebra.ofId O Kf)).toLinearMap
        (dualWeyl O (m + 1) (m + 1) (nuOf lam)) := by
  have hν : Antitone (nuOf lam) := fun a b hab =>
    Int.toNat_le_toNat (sub_le_sub_right (hlam hab) _)
  refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
  · rintro x ⟨x0, hx0, rfl⟩
    have h1 := sandwichO D O (dvrSettingOfSetting h) (good_ring_of_setting h) m (nuOf lam) hν x0 hx0
    rw [ellWt_nuOf] at h1
    exact ⟨_, h1, LinearMap.map_smul _ _ _⟩
  · exact Submodule.map_mono (Formalism.GradedWeylDivisibility.weylLeDual O (m + 1) (m + 1) le_rfl (nuOf lam) hν)

end Set

theorem modelSandwichG_of_places (D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) (hpos : Formalism.MainNotions.G.Inhab.GatHasPlaces D) :
    Formalism.MainNotions.G.Joints.ModelSandwichG D := by
  intro u w hg m lam hlam
  exact sandwich D ((D.gat u w hg).P.setting ⟨0, hpos u w hg⟩) m lam hlam

theorem modelSandwichG_of_ties (D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) (hT : Formalism.BasicNotions.GatTies D) :
    Formalism.MainNotions.G.Joints.ModelSandwichG D := by
  exact modelSandwichG_of_places D (Formalism.MainNotions.G.Inhab.gatHasPlaces_of_ties hT)

end Formalism.L2.G.Model


[LGS_model_sandwich_04] Actual top/off-top spreads, both Frobenius parities and all coefficient factors
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalRingCardinality/ColumnWeightSpreadOffTop.lean#L34-L250
Range SHA256: fc0e7e34ae954feeecae4c253cd8ee3280d8aaa360ef9eab2b4ab8a4778d5126
theorem Ranks.one_le_n (R : Ranks) : 1 ≤ R.n := R.ord_pos

theorem Ranks.five_le_k (R : Ranks) : 5 ≤ R.k := by
  have h1 := R.two_le_m
  have h2 := R.m_add_three_le_k
  omega

namespace ParameterChoice'

variable {D : D7} {L : SizeData₂ D}

theorem five_le_k (_P : ParameterChoice' D L) : (5 : ℝ) ≤ D.R.k := by exact_mod_cast D.R.five_le_k

theorem LaG_pos (P : ParameterChoice' D L) : 0 < L.LaG := by
  have hk : (0 : ℝ) < D.R.k := by linarith [P.five_le_k]
  have h1 : 0 < L.LaG * L.sc.b' / D.R.k := by linarith [P.S_le, L.Sc_pos]
  have h2 : 0 < L.LaG * L.sc.b' := by
    have := mul_pos h1 hk
    rwa [div_mul_cancel₀ _ hk.ne'] at this
  exact (pos_iff_pos_of_mul_pos h2).mpr L.sc.b'_pos

theorem Sc_ge (P : ParameterChoice' D L) : 4096 * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 ≤ L.Sc := by
  have hk := P.five_le_k
  have hD := P.Del_ge.2.2.2
  have hm : (2 : ℝ) ≤ D.R.m := by exact_mod_cast D.R.two_le_m
  have hm10 : (1024 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 10 := by
    calc (1024 : ℝ) = 2 ^ 10 := by norm_num
      _ ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 10 := pow_le_pow_left₀ (by norm_num) hm 10
  have hN : (D.R.k : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) := by
    have : D.R.k ≤ D.R.N D.R.m := by unfold Ranks.N; omega
    exact_mod_cast this
  have h1 : (D.R.k : ℝ) * 1024 ≤ (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) * (D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 10 :=
    mul_le_mul hN hm10 (by norm_num) (by linarith)
  rw [L.Sc_eq]
  nlinarith

theorem kSc_le_a (P : ParameterChoice' D L) : (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.Sc ≤ L.sc.a :=
  P.kS_le.trans (L.sc.w''_le_a _ D.R.one_le_n)

theorem Sc_le (P : ParameterChoice' D L) : 5 * L.Sc ≤ L.sc.a := by
  have := P.kSc_le_a
  nlinarith [P.five_le_k, L.Sc_pos]

theorem k_le_a (P : ParameterChoice' D L) : (D.R.k : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.a := by
  have h1 := P.kSc_le_a
  have h2 := P.Sc_ge
  have h3 := P.five_le_k
  nlinarith

theorem four_le_a (P : ParameterChoice' D L) : 4 ≤ L.sc.a := by linarith [P.k_le_a, P.five_le_k]

theorem bT_le' (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (t : D.host.TT) : (L.bT t : ℝ) ≤ 5 / 4 * L.sc.b' := by
  have hk : (0 : ℝ) < D.R.k := by linarith [P.five_le_k]
  have hx := L.sc.one_lt_alt
  have hlog : 0 < Real.log L.sc.x := Real.log_pos hx
  have h2 : 0 ≤ P.Crho * Real.log L.sc.x * L.LaG / D.R.k :=
    div_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg P.Crho_pos.le hlog.le) P.LaG_pos.le) hk.le
  have h3 : L.sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ) ≤ 1 := Real.rpow_le_one_of_one_le_of_nonpos hx.le (by norm_num)
  have h4 := P.rho_le
  unfold LevelStarExponents.rhoP at h4
  have h5 : LevelStarExponents.Nstar L * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps * L.LaG / D.R.k ≤ 1 / 4 := by
    have : 4 * LevelStarExponents.Nstar L * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps * L.LaG / D.R.k =
      4 * (LevelStarExponents.Nstar L * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps * L.LaG / D.R.k) := by ring
    linarith
  have h6 : LevelStarExponents.Nstar L * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps * L.LaG * L.sc.b' / D.R.k =
      (LevelStarExponents.Nstar L * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps * L.LaG / D.R.k) * L.sc.b' := by ring
  have h7 := P.bT_le t
  rw [h6] at h7
  nlinarith [L.sc.b'_pos]

end ParameterChoice'

theorem SizeData₂.aT_le' {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) (t : D.host.TT) : (L.aT t : ℝ) ≤ 6 / 5 * L.sc.a := by
  have hk : (5 : ℝ) ≤ D.R.k := by exact_mod_cast D.R.five_le_k
  have hx : (1 : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) :=
    Real.one_le_rpow L.sc.one_lt_alt.le (by unfold Scale.A; norm_num)
  have h5 : (5 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) := by nlinarith
  have h1 : L.sc.a / ((D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ≤ L.sc.a / 5 :=
    div_le_div_of_nonneg_left L.sc.a_pos.le (by norm_num) h5
  linarith [(L.aT_win t).2]

section Spread

variable {D : D7}

def lamFst (D : D7) : Fin (D.R.N 0) := ⟨0, by have := D.R.five_le_k; simp only [Ranks.N]; omega⟩

def lamLst (D : D7) : Fin (D.R.N 0) := ⟨D.R.k - 1, by have := D.R.five_le_k; simp only [Ranks.N]; omega⟩

theorem columnWeight_spread_eq (u : D.what₂.Word) (τ : D.what₂.Tau) :
    ((((D.fm u).lam 0 τ).w (lamFst D) - ((D.fm u).lam 0 τ).w (lamLst D) : ℤ) : ℝ) =
      (((D.hc τ).u ⟨0, by have := D.R.five_le_k; omega⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ) -
        (((D.hc τ).u ⟨D.R.k - 1, by have := D.R.five_le_k; omega⟩ : ℚ) : ℝ) - ((D.R.k : ℝ) - 1) := by
  have hk := D.R.five_le_k
  have h0 := (D.fm u).lam_eq 0 (Nat.zero_le _) τ (lamFst D)
  have h1 := (D.fm u).lam_eq 0 (Nat.zero_le _) τ (lamLst D)
  have c0 : (lamFst D).1 < D.R.k := by simp only [lamFst]; omega
  have c1 : (lamLst D).1 < D.R.k := by simp only [lamLst]; omega
  rw [dif_pos c0] at h0
  rw [dif_pos c1] at h1
  simp only [hcToWeightQ, rhoVec, lamFst, lamLst] at h0 h1
  rw [D.hcW_first u τ (by omega)] at h0
  rw [D.hcW_last u τ (by omega)] at h1
  have hc : ((D.R.k - 1 : ℕ) : ℚ) = (D.R.k : ℚ) - 1 := by
    rw [Nat.cast_sub (by omega)]; simp
  rw [hc] at h1
  have hq : ((((D.fm u).lam 0 τ).w (lamFst D) - ((D.fm u).lam 0 τ).w (lamLst D) : ℤ) : ℚ) =
      (D.hc τ).u ⟨0, by omega⟩ - (D.hc τ).u ⟨D.R.k - 1, by omega⟩ - ((D.R.k : ℚ) - 1) := by
    push_cast
    simp only [lamFst, lamLst]
    rw [h0, h1]
    push_cast
    ring
  have := congrArg (fun q : ℚ => (q : ℝ)) hq
  push_cast at this ⊢
  linarith

theorem columnWeight_spread_nonneg (u : D.what₂.Word) (τ : D.what₂.Tau) :
    0 ≤ ((D.fm u).lam 0 τ).w (lamFst D) - ((D.fm u).lam 0 τ).w (lamLst D) := by
  have := ((D.fm u).lam 0 τ).anti (show lamFst D ≤ lamLst D from Nat.zero_le _)
  omega

variable {L : SizeData₂ D}

theorem columnWeight_spread_at_top (L : SizeData₂ D) (u : D.what₂.Word) (τ : D.what₂.Tau) (t : D.host.TT)
    (ht : (D.eTau τ : D.R.GG) = D.R.tau1 t) :
    ((D.fm u).lam 0 τ).w (lamFst D) - ((D.fm u).lam 0 τ).w (lamLst D) = L.aT t + L.bT t := by
  have h := columnWeight_spread_eq u τ
  obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := L.top_u τ t (by have := D.R.five_le_k; omega) ht
  rw [h1, h2] at h
  have : ((((D.fm u).lam 0 τ).w (lamFst D) - ((D.fm u).lam 0 τ).w (lamLst D) : ℤ) : ℝ) = ((L.aT t + L.bT t : ℤ) : ℝ) := by
    rw [h]; push_cast; ring
  exact_mod_cast this

theorem columnWeight_spread_off_top (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (u : D.what₂.Word) (τ : D.what₂.Tau)
    (ht : (D.eTau τ : D.R.GG) ∉ D.host.topPlaces) :
    ((((D.fm u).lam 0 τ).w (lamFst D) - ((D.fm u).lam 0 τ).w (lamLst D) : ℤ) : ℝ) ≤ 3 * L.Sc := by
  have hk := D.R.five_le_k
  have hkR := P.five_le_k
  rw [columnWeight_spread_eq u τ]
  have h1 := P.cloud_place_bound τ (by omega) ht
  have h2 := P.uk_extreme τ (by omega) ht
  have h3 := (L.cloud_v τ ⟨0, by omega⟩).2
  have h4 := (L.cloud_v τ ⟨D.R.k - 2, by omega⟩).1
  rw [h2]
  push_cast at h1 ⊢
  linarith

theorem columnWeight_spread_le (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (u : D.what₂.Word) (τ : D.what₂.Tau) :
    ((((D.fm u).lam 0 τ).w (lamFst D) - ((D.fm u).lam 0 τ).w (lamLst D) : ℤ) : ℝ) ≤ 3 * L.sc.a := by
  by_cases ht : (D.eTau τ : D.R.GG) ∈ D.host.topPlaces
  · obtain ⟨t, htT, hte⟩ := Finset.mem_image.mp ht
    rw [columnWeight_spread_at_top L u τ ⟨t, htT⟩ hte.symm]
    have h1 := L.aT_le' ⟨t, htT⟩
    have h2 := P.bT_le' ⟨t, htT⟩
    have h3 := L.sc.b'_le_a
    push_cast
    linarith [L.sc.a_pos]
  · have h1 := columnWeight_spread_off_top P u τ ht
    have h2 := P.Sc_le
    linarith [L.Sc_pos]

theorem gat_ell_le (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (T : GatTies D) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (h : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) (v : Fin (D.gat u w h).P.nV)
    (s : Fin ((D.gat u w h).P.n' v)) :
    (((D.gat u w h).P.lam0 v s 0 - (D.gat u w h).P.lam0 v s (Fin.last _) : ℤ) : ℝ) ≤ 3 * L.sc.a := by
  have hk := T.gat_k u w h
  rw [T.gat_lam u w h v s 0, T.gat_lam u w h v s (Fin.last _)]
  have e0 : (⟨(0 : Fin ((D.gat u w h).P.m + 1)).1, by
      have h1 := T.gat_k u w h; simp only [Ranks.N]; simp; omega⟩ : Fin (D.R.N 0)) = lamFst D := rfl
  have e1 : (⟨(Fin.last (D.gat u w h).P.m).1, by
      have h1 := T.gat_k u w h; simp only [Ranks.N]; simp; omega⟩ : Fin (D.R.N 0)) = lamLst D := by
    apply Fin.ext
    simp only [lamLst, Fin.val_last]
    omega
  rw [e0, e1]
  exact columnWeight_spread_le P u _

theorem gat_ellWt_le (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (T : GatTies D) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (h : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) (v : Fin (D.gat u w h).P.nV)
    (s : Fin ((D.gat u w h).P.n' v)) :
    (ellWt ((D.gat u w h).P.lam0 v s) : ℝ) ≤ 3 * L.sc.a := by
  have h1 := gat_ell_le P T u w h v s
  have h2 : 0 ≤ (D.gat u w h).P.lam0 v s 0 - (D.gat u w h).P.lam0 v s (Fin.last _) := by
    have := (D.gat u w h).P.dom v s (Fin.zero_le (Fin.last _))
    omega
  have h3 : lastIdx ((D.gat u w h).P.m + 1) = Fin.last _ := rfl
  unfold ellWt
  rw [h3]
  have h4 : ((((D.gat u w h).P.lam0 v s 0 - (D.gat u w h).P.lam0 v s (Fin.last _)).toNat : ℕ) : ℝ) =
      (((D.gat u w h).P.lam0 v s 0 - (D.gat u w h).P.lam0 v s (Fin.last _) : ℤ) : ℝ) := by
    have := Int.toNat_of_nonneg h2
    exact_mod_cast this
  rw [h4]
  exact h1

theorem ellWt_lamAt {n' m : ℕ} (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) (s : Fin n') :
    ellWt (lamAt lam0 s) = ellWt (lam0 s) := by
  have h3 : lastIdx (m + 1) = Fin.last _ := rfl
  unfold ellWt lamAt
  rw [h3]
  split_ifs
  · rfl
  · simp only [Fin.rev_zero, Fin.rev_last]
    congr 1
    ring

theorem gat_ellWt_lamAt_le (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (T : GatTies D) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (h : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) (v : Fin (D.gat u w h).P.nV)
    (s : Fin ((D.gat u w h).P.n' v)) :
    (ellWt (lamAt ((D.gat u w h).P.lam0 v) s) : ℝ) ≤ 3 * L.sc.a := by
  rw [ellWt_lamAt]
  exact gat_ellWt_le P T u w h v s

end Spread

end Formalism.BasicNotions


[LGS_model_sandwich_05] Exact actual exponent sum bounded by X.ep
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/Joints.lean#L1848-L1926
Range SHA256: ee125485aa4bcc20405b21f91017fa14a455c31b8beaa03b729c46b841be9cf6
theorem expBound_of (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (Ce : ℝ) (hCe : 1 ≤ Ce) (hEp : EpDef X Ce)
    (P : ParameterChoice' D L) (T : GatTies D) : ExpBoundG D X := by
  intro u w hg
  have hk := T.gat_k u w hg
  have ha4 : (4 : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.a := P.four_le_a
  have hka : (D.R.k : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.a := P.k_le_a
  have hk5 : (5 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) := P.five_le_k

  have hpos : ∀ v (s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v)),
      (wExp (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (D.gat u w hg).P.m (lamAt ((D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v) s) : ℝ) ≤
        (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) L.sc.a := by
    intro v s
    have hprime : (D.what₂.places.resChar w).Prime := ((D.gat u w hg).P.setting v).prime
    have hp1 : (1 : ℝ) < (D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast hprime.one_lt
    have hl := gat_ellWt_lamAt_le P T u w hg v s
    have hloga : 0 ≤ Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) L.sc.a := Real.logb_nonneg hp1 (by linarith)
    unfold wExp deF N0
    set kk := (D.gat u w hg).P.m + 1 with hkk_def
    have hkk : (kk : ℝ) = D.R.k := by exact_mod_cast hk
    set ℓ := ellWt (lamAt ((D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v) s) with hℓ
    have hN0 : (((kk * (kk - 1) / 2 : ℕ)) : ℝ) ≤ (kk : ℝ) ^ 2 / 2 := by
      have h1 : (((kk * (kk - 1) / 2 : ℕ)) : ℝ) ≤ ((kk * (kk - 1) : ℕ) : ℝ) / 2 := Nat.cast_div_le
      have h2 : ((kk * (kk - 1) : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ (kk : ℝ) ^ 2 := by
        have : kk * (kk - 1) ≤ kk * kk := Nat.mul_le_mul_left _ (Nat.sub_le _ _)
        calc ((kk * (kk - 1) : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ ((kk * kk : ℕ) : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast this
          _ = (kk : ℝ) ^ 2 := by push_cast; ring
      linarith
    have hk5' : (5 : ℝ) ≤ (kk : ℝ) := by rw [hkk]; exact hk5
    have hn1 : 1 ≤ ℓ + kk - 1 := by
      have : (5 : ℕ) ≤ kk := by exact_mod_cast hk5'
      omega
    have hlog : ((Nat.log (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (ℓ + kk - 1) : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤
        2 * Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) L.sc.a := by
      refine (Real.natLog_le_logb _ _).trans ?_
      have hcast : (((ℓ + kk - 1 : ℕ)) : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.a ^ 2 := by
        have e : (((ℓ + kk - 1 : ℕ)) : ℝ) = (ℓ : ℝ) + (kk : ℝ) - 1 := by
          rw [Nat.cast_sub (by omega)]; push_cast; ring
        rw [e, hkk]; nlinarith
      calc Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (((ℓ + kk - 1 : ℕ)) : ℝ)
          ≤ Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (L.sc.a ^ 2) :=
            Real.logb_le_logb_of_le hp1 (by exact_mod_cast Nat.pos_of_ne_zero (by omega)) hcast
        _ = 2 * Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) L.sc.a := by
            rw [Real.logb_pow]; push_cast; ring
    push_cast
    rw [← hkk]
    calc (((kk * (kk - 1) / 2 : ℕ)) : ℝ) * ((Nat.log (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (ℓ + kk - 1) : ℕ) : ℝ)
        ≤ ((kk : ℝ) ^ 2 / 2) * (2 * Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) L.sc.a) :=
          mul_le_mul hN0 hlog (Nat.cast_nonneg _) (by positivity)
      _ = (kk : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) L.sc.a := by ring

  have hcard : (∑ v, ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v : ℝ)) = (D.R.f : ℝ) := by
    have h1 : (∑ v, (D.gat u w hg).P.n' v) = Fintype.card (Σ v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV, Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v)) := by
      rw [Fintype.card_sigma]; simp
    have h2 : Fintype.card (Σ v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV, Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v)) = D.R.f := by
      rw [Fintype.card_congr (T.gat_pos u w hg), D.what₂.card_Tau]
    rw [← Nat.cast_sum, h1, h2]
  have hsum : ((∑ v, ∑ s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v),
      wExp (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (D.gat u w hg).P.m (lamAt ((D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v) s) : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤
      (D.R.f : ℝ) * ((D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) L.sc.a) := by
    push_cast
    calc (∑ v, ∑ s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v),
          ((wExp (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (D.gat u w hg).P.m (lamAt ((D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v) s) : ℕ) : ℝ))
        ≤ ∑ v, ∑ _s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v), (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) L.sc.a :=
          Finset.sum_le_sum fun v _ => Finset.sum_le_sum fun s _ => hpos v s
      _ = (∑ v, ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v : ℝ)) * ((D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) L.sc.a) := by
          simp [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, Finset.sum_mul]
      _ = _ := by rw [hcard]
  rw [hEp]
  refine Nat.cast_le.1 (le_trans ?_ (Nat.le_ceil _))
  have hloga : 0 ≤ Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) L.sc.a :=
    div_nonneg (Real.log_nonneg (by linarith)) (Real.log_natCast_nonneg _)
  have hx : 0 ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) L.sc.a := by positivity
  calc _ ≤ (D.R.f : ℝ) * ((D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) L.sc.a) := hsum
    _ = 1 * ((D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) L.sc.a) := by ring
    _ ≤ Ce * ((D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) L.sc.a) :=
        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hCe hx
    _ = _ := by ring

[LGS_orbit_sizes_01] Exact adm1 through adm5, including all original orbit and band clauses
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/SmallOrderPrimes.lean#L70-L120
Range SHA256: 00ee1526de4a116260b5e1767bbbd9089617e3385fcc8d5ca27a9f3490ac288d
def adm1 (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (Ce : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → IsResidueClassZero D p → L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) ≤ (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) →
    L2 D L.sc < goodU L.sc.delta1 → 3 * Gp L.sc ≤ addOrderOf (hP D p) →
    ∀ t0 ∈ reps X p, Good X p t0 →
      ((addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1 / (Ce * Real.log L.sc.x) ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) ∧
        (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) ≤ 40 * (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1) ∧
      (∀ s ∈ topsOn X p t0, IsV X p s → ∃ d : ℕ, 1 ≤ d ∧ d ≤ Gp L.sc ∧
        s + d • hP D p ∈ D.host.TT ∧ IsV X p (s + d • hP D p) ∧
        ((Finset.Ioo 0 d).filter (fun e => s + e • hP D p ∈ D.host.TT)).card ≤ N1 L.sc) ∧
      (∀ i ∈ topsOn X p t0,
        ((Finset.Icc 1 (L1 D L.sc p)).filter (fun d =>
          i - d • hP D p ∈ D.host.TT ∧ IsV X p (i - d • hP D p))).card < rG L.sc)

open Classical in

def primesResidueBelow (D : D7) (y : ℝ) : Finset ℕ :=
  (Finset.range ⌈y⌉₊).filter (fun p => p.Prime ∧ (p : ℝ) < y ∧ IsResidueClassZero D p)

open Classical in

def BTT (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) : ℝ :=
  ∑ p ∈ (primesResidueBelow D L.sc.X').filter (fun p => L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) ≤ (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ)),
    Real.log p / p * ((D.host.TT.filter (fun t => ¬ Good X p (X.bs (stepP D p) t))).card : ℝ)

def adm2 (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) : Prop :=
  ((D.R.n : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1 / 2 ≤ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ∧ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ≤ 2 * (D.R.n : ℝ) * L.sc.delta1) ∧
  BTT D L X ≤ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (-2 + L.sc.eps)

open Classical in

def adm3 (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) : Prop :=
  ∑ p ∈ (primesResidueBelow D (L.sc.X'' D.R.n)).filter
      (fun p => (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) < L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4)), Real.log p / p ≤
    L.sc.x ^ (-hG L.sc + L.sc.eps)

open Classical in

def ArchimedeanSignatureMatching (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  rho D K L.sc ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ) ∧
  ∑ p ∈ (Finset.range ⌈L.sc.X'⌉₊).filter (fun p => p.Prime ∧ InBand0 D K L.sc p), Real.log p / p ≤
    L.sc.x ^ (-1 : ℝ) ∧
  rho D K L.sc ≤ rhoB D K L.sc ∧ rhoB D K L.sc ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ) ∧
  ∑ p ∈ (Finset.range ⌈L.sc.X'⌉₊).filter (fun p => p.Prime ∧ InBand D K L.sc p), Real.log p / p ≤
    L.sc.x ^ (-1 : ℝ)

def adm5 (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (K : Consts) (δ : ℝ) : Prop :=
  L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps - δ) ≤ La K L.sc ∧ La K L.sc ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps + δ) ∧
  ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime →
    ((p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k → Real.log (Qp L.sc p) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 8 + δ)) ∧
    ((p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' → Real.log (Qp L.sc p) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (11 * L.sc.eps / 24 + δ))



[LGS_orbit_sizes_02] Uniform orbit constant, original FamilyWindowLogBound and band family endpoints
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/TopChoiceBounds.lean#L4392-L4438

theorem uniform_constant_orbits_holds (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (hx3 : 3 ≤ L.sc.x) :
    ∃ Ce : ℝ, 0 < Ce ∧ ∀ C ≥ Ce, AuxBlock8.adm1 D L X C := uniform_constant_at_instance D L X (master_orbit_lemma_holds D) hx3
theorem family_uniform_constant_holds {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) :
    ∃ C : ℝ, 0 < C ∧ ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, AuxBlock8.adm1 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) C :=
  family_uniform_constant_of_master Fm (family_master_orbit_holds Fm)

theorem stmtFG1_proved {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : CornerGLContinuity.FamilyLatticeGrowthOfGrowthHypotheses Fm := by
  rintro ⟨x0, h⟩
  exact ⟨⟨x0, fun x hx i => (gluing_statement_holds Fm.GK (Fm.Gx x i) (Fm.Gy x i) (h x hx i)).1⟩,
    ⟨x0, fun x hx i => (gluing_statement_holds Fm.GK (Fm.Gx x i) (Fm.Gy x i) (h x hx i)).2⟩⟩

theorem family_large_scale_conditions_hold {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : CornerGLContinuity.FamilyLargeScale Fm :=
  family_large_scale_of_master Fm (family_master_orbit_holds Fm) (family_inert_primes_holds Fm)

theorem family_log_bound_threshold (eps : ℝ) : ∃ C₀ : ℝ, ∀ Fm : FamP eps, C₀ ≤ Fm.GK.CN → CornerGLContinuity.FamilyWindowLogBound Fm := by
  by_cases hne : Nonempty (FamP eps)
  · obtain ⟨Fm0⟩ := hne
    have he := family_eps_pos Fm0
    obtain ⟨i0⟩ := Fm0.Ix_zero
    obtain ⟨Ce, hCe, hC⟩ := (master_orbit_lemma_holds (Fm0.D 0 i0)).2.1 (⌈8 * ((2 + 8 * eps) + 2) / eps⌉₊ + 1) (2 + 8 * eps)
      (by omega) (by positivity)
    refine ⟨Ce, fun Fm hCN => ⟨max 3 (Fm.x1 + 1), fun x hx i => ?_⟩⟩
    have a4 : 3 ≤ x := le_trans (le_max_left _ _) hx
    have a5 : Fm.x1 < x := by
      have := le_trans (le_max_right _ _) hx
      linarith
    have hLx := Fm.L_x x i a5
    have hLe := Fm.L_eps x i
    have hde0 : 0 < x ^ (-(eps / 8)) := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by linarith) _
    have hde1 : x ^ (-(eps / 8)) ≤ 1 := Real.rpow_le_one_of_one_le_of_nonpos (by linarith) (by linarith)
    obtain ⟨_, hN1, _, _⟩ := hC _ x hde0 hde1 a4
    unfold CornerGLContinuity.WindowLogBound CbLarge N1 rG KG Formalism.MainNotions.G.hG Scale.delta1
    rw [hLx, hLe]
    exact le_trans hN1 (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hCN (Real.log_nonneg (by linarith)))
  · exact ⟨0, fun Fm => absurd ⟨Fm⟩ hne⟩

theorem family_log_bound_closed (𝒞 : FamClass) : Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => CornerGLContinuity.FamilyWindowLogBound Fm := by
  refine ⟨1, one_pos, fun eps _ _ => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨C₀, h⟩ := family_log_bound_threshold eps
  refine ⟨⟨0, fun _ => 0, fun _ => 0, fun _ => C₀⟩, fun Fm CH _ hL => h Fm (hL.2.2.2 Fm.GK.CN ?_)⟩
  simp [FamP.cLast]

theorem family_band_sum_holds {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (hCN : CornerGLContinuity.FamilyWindowLogBound Fm) : CornerGLContinuity.FamilyOrdersLargePrimeBoundConstant Fm :=
  family_band_ratio_holds Fm (family_master_orbit_holds Fm) hCN

theorem family_band_ratio_statement {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (hCN : CornerGLContinuity.FamilyWindowLogBound Fm) :
    ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, AuxBlock8.ArchimedeanSignatureMatching (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) Fm.GK := family_band_sum_holds Fm hCN


[LGS_orbit_sizes_03] Original family size/orbit/order statements and finite-class predicate
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/PlaneCountTheoremWithConstants.lean#L1472-L1531
Range SHA256: 60d8b8e91e232d892939c269688e87491e29c9022c4bd3b118642e341ec241f4
theorem family_sizes_bound {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (hLa : 0 < Fm.GK.La) : Adm5F Fm := by
  intro δ hδ
  obtain ⟨i0⟩ := Fm.Ix_zero
  have heps : 0 < eps := by
    have := (Fm.L 0 i0).sc.eps_pos
    rwa [Fm.L_eps] at this
  obtain ⟨Ce, hCe, hC⟩ := (TopChoiceBounds.master_orbit_sizes_hold (Fm.D 0 i0)).1 (⌈8 * ((2 + 8 * eps) + 2) / eps⌉₊ + 1) (2 + 8 * eps)
    (by omega) (by linarith)
  obtain ⟨x0, hx0⟩ := log_cube_le (2 * Fm.GK.La * Ce ^ 2 + 38 * Ce + 1 / Fm.GK.La) δ hδ
  refine ⟨max (max (Fm.x1 + 1) 3) x0, fun x hx i => ?_⟩
  have hx1 : Fm.x1 < x := by
    have := le_trans (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) (le_max_left _ _)) hx
    linarith
  have hx3 : (3 : ℝ) ≤ x := le_trans (le_trans (le_max_right _ _) (le_max_left _ _)) hx
  have hxx := Fm.L_x x i hx1
  have hee := Fm.L_eps x i
  have hd0 : 0 < (Fm.L x i).sc.delta1 := sc_delta1_pos _
  have hd1 : (Fm.L x i).sc.delta1 ≤ 1 := by
    unfold Scale.delta1
    apply Real.rpow_le_one_of_one_le_of_nonpos (Fm.L x i).sc.one_lt_alt.le
    have := (Fm.L x i).sc.eps_pos
    linarith
  have hr : rG (Fm.L x i).sc = ⌈8 * ((2 + 8 * eps) + 2) / eps⌉₊ + 1 := by
    unfold rG KG hG
    rw [hee]
  have hh : hG (Fm.L x i).sc = 2 + 8 * eps := by
    unfold hG
    rw [hee]
  obtain ⟨hGb, hNb, _⟩ := hC (Fm.L x i).sc.delta1 (Fm.L x i).sc.x hd0 hd1 (by rw [hxx]; exact hx3)
  rw [← hr, ← hh] at hGb hNb
  refine sizes_bound_of_log_estimates Fm.GK δ Ce hLa hCe (by rw [hxx]; exact hx3) hGb hNb ?_ (by rw [hxx]; exact hx0 x (le_trans (le_max_right _ _) hx))
  have hk := (Fm.sizes x hx1 i).2.2.1.2
  refine le_trans hk (Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le (Fm.L x i).sc.one_lt_alt.le ?_)
  have := (Fm.L x i).sc.eps_lt
  have h0 := (Fm.L x i).sc.eps_pos
  unfold szTh
  linarith

end Sizes

[LGS_small_order_01] Actual hP and cyclotomic dyadic Frobenius identification
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/AdmissibleParameterJoins/GandSk.lean#L13-L55
Range SHA256: 1ed522d8c0c168284f89225c3cf467e07cac05b060dd4f27f378ac1b6c56cede
    ∃ h : Nat.Coprime p 2, D.R.hH (D.R.iota (hP D p)) =
      hcl D.R.e (ZMod.unitOfCoprime p (Nat.Coprime.pow_right (D.R.e + 2) h)) := by
  have hp2 : p ≠ 2 := by
    rintro rfl
    have h2 : D.host.cls 2 = PrimeClass.ram2 := Formalism.BasicNotions.primeClass_two D.host.toHostRaw
    have hc' : D.host.cls 2 = PrimeClass.inertClass := hc
    rw [h2] at hc'
    cases hc'
  have hcop : Nat.Coprime p 2 := (Nat.coprime_primes hp Nat.prime_two).mpr hp2
  refine ⟨hcop, ?_⟩
  have hN : ¬ p ∣ D.host.condN := Formalism.BasicNotions.not_dvd_conductor_of_inert hp (show D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass from hc)
  haveI : Fact p.Prime := ⟨hp⟩
  haveI : (Ideal.span {(p : ℤ)}).IsMaximal :=
    PrincipalIdealRing.isMaximal_of_irreducible (Nat.prime_iff_prime_int.mp hp).irreducible
  obtain ⟨P, hPm, hPl⟩ : ∃ P : Ideal (𝓞 D.host.E), P.IsMaximal ∧ P.LiesOver (Ideal.span {(p : ℤ)}) := by
    first
    | exact Ideal.exists_maximal_ideal_liesOver_of_isIntegral _
    | exact Ideal.exists_maximal_ideal_liesOver_of_isIntegral _ _
    | exact Ideal.exists_ideal_liesOver_maximal_of_isIntegral _
    | (obtain ⟨Q, hQm, hQc⟩ := Ideal.exists_ideal_over_maximal_of_isIntegral (S := 𝓞 D.host.E)
          (Ideal.span {(p : ℤ)}) (by simp)
       exact ⟨Q, hQm, ⟨hQc.symm⟩⟩)
  haveI : P.IsPrime := hPm.isPrime
  obtain ⟨⟨σ, hσ⟩, -, -, -⟩ := D.host.der.frob p hN P
  have hmem : D.host.galE σ ∈ frobSet D p := ⟨P, inferInstance, hPl, σ, hσ, rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨P', hP'p, hP'l, σ', hσ', hgal⟩ := Classical.epsilon_spec ⟨_, hmem⟩
  obtain ⟨-, -, h3, -⟩ := D.host.der.frob p hN P'
  obtain ⟨-, -, v, hv, hvh⟩ := h3 σ' hσ'
  unfold hP
  simp only [Formalism.HC.Frame.iota_iotaInv]
  rw [← hgal, show ZMod.unitOfCoprime p (Nat.Coprime.pow_right (D.R.e + 2) hcop) = v from
    Units.ext (by rw [ZMod.coe_unitOfCoprime, hv])]
  exact hvh

theorem two_readings_of_Frobenius (D : D7) : Formalism.MainNotions.CornerGLContinuity.FrobeniusClassCompatibility D := by
  intro p hp hc
  obtain ⟨h2, hh⟩ := hH_hP D p hp hc
  refine ⟨fun h => ?_, ?_⟩
  · subst h
    exact absurd h2 (by decide)
  unfold Formalism.MainNotions.GlobalTheoryTheory.hOf
  split_ifs with hcond
  rw [Formalism.BasicNotions.iotaH_iotaHInv, ← hh]

[LGS_small_order_03] Exact size/order normalization and power-saving budget
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/TopChoiceBounds.lean#L1670-L1762
Range SHA256: b5ca5766e30f646a1ae13cadc0a99384a6d193fbac778609b4916cac34ed139a
theorem order_statement_at_scale {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) (C₄ : ℝ) (h4 : UnusedSlot33.Conclusion4 D C₄) (hN : SizeN L)
    (h1 : 8 * Real.log L.sc.x ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 3))
    (h2 : 4 * |C₄| ≤ L.sc.x ^ (19 * L.sc.eps / 300)) : CornerGLContinuity.OrdersSmallPrimeBound D L := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨_, _, _, h4⟩ := h4
  obtain ⟨hN1, hN2⟩ := hN
  set x := L.sc.x with hxdef
  set ε := L.sc.eps with hεdef
  have hx1 : 1 < x := L.sc.one_lt_alt
  have hx0 : 0 < x := lt_trans one_pos hx1
  have hε : 0 < ε := L.sc.eps_pos
  have hε2 : ε < 1 / 52 := L.sc.eps_lt
  have hlx : 0 < Real.log x := Real.log_pos hx1
  have hθ : szTh L = ε / 100 := rfl
  rw [hθ] at hN1 hN2
  have hn0 : (0 : ℝ) < D.R.n := lt_of_lt_of_le (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hx0 _) hN1
  have hnN : 0 < D.R.n := by exact_mod_cast hn0
  have hn1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ D.R.n := by exact_mod_cast hnN
  have hJ : (1 : ℝ) ≤ x ^ (ε / 4) := Real.one_le_rpow hx1.le (by positivity)
  have key := h4 (x ^ (ε / 4)) hJ (L.sc.X'' D.R.n)
  unfold CornerGLContinuity.OrdersSmallPrimeBound
  refine le_trans (le_trans ?_ key) ?_
  · apply Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset_of_nonneg
    · intro p hp
      simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_range] at hp ⊢
      obtain ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4⟩ := hp
      exact ⟨lt_of_lt_of_le a1 (Nat.ceil_le_floor_add_one _), a2, a3, a4.le⟩
    · intro p _ _
      exact div_nonneg (Real.log_natCast_nonneg p) (Nat.cast_nonneg p)
  ·
    have hX' : Real.log L.sc.X' = x ^ (ε / 3) := by unfold Scale.X'; exact Real.log_exp _
    have hX'0 : 0 < L.sc.X' := by unfold Scale.X'; exact Real.exp_pos _
    have hlogn : Real.log D.R.n ≤ (2 + 8 * ε + ε / 100) * Real.log x := by
      rw [← Real.log_rpow hx0]
      exact Real.log_le_log hn0 hN2
    have hlogn0 : 0 ≤ Real.log D.R.n := Real.log_nonneg hn1
    have hB0 : 0 ≤ x ^ (ε / 3) := Real.rpow_nonneg hx0.le _
    have hno : (D.R.n : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.o : ℝ) * ((2 ^ D.R.e : ℕ) : ℝ) := by
      exact_mod_cast D.R.toFrame.ord_le_o_mul_two_pow
    have hmn : ((2 ^ D.R.e : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.n : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast D.R.two_pow_le_n
    have hm1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ ((2 ^ D.R.e : ℕ) : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast Nat.one_le_two_pow
    generalize ((2 ^ D.R.e : ℕ) : ℝ) = m at hno hmn hm1 ⊢
    have hm0 : 0 < m := lt_of_lt_of_le one_pos hm1
    have hlogm : Real.log m ≤ Real.log D.R.n := Real.log_le_log hm0 hmn
    have hlogm0 : 0 ≤ Real.log m := Real.log_nonneg hm1
    have hmlo : x ^ (2 + 8 * ε - ε / 50) ≤ m := by
      have hx100 : 0 < x ^ (ε / 100) := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hx0 _
      have ho : (D.R.o : ℝ) ≤ x ^ (ε / 100) := L.o_tie
      have e1 : x ^ (2 + 8 * ε - ε / 100) = x ^ (ε / 100) * x ^ (2 + 8 * ε - ε / 50) := by
        rw [← Real.rpow_add hx0]
        congr 1
        ring
      have h5 : x ^ (ε / 100) * x ^ (2 + 8 * ε - ε / 50) ≤ x ^ (ε / 100) * m :=
        e1 ▸ hN1.trans (hno.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right ho hm0.le))
      exact le_of_mul_le_mul_left h5 hx100
    have hLg : Real.log (m * L.sc.X'' D.R.n) =
        Real.log m + (Real.log x + Real.log D.R.n + x ^ (ε / 3)) := by
      rw [scaled_upper_length_eq L.sc D.R.n hnN, Real.log_mul hm0.ne' (by positivity), Real.log_mul (by positivity) hX'0.ne',
        Real.log_mul hx0.ne' hn0.ne', hX']
    have hLg0 : 0 ≤ Real.log (m * L.sc.X'' D.R.n) := by rw [hLg]; positivity
    have hLg1 : Real.log (m * L.sc.X'' D.R.n) ≤ 2 * x ^ (ε / 3) := by
      rw [hLg]
      have : (2 + 8 * ε + ε / 100) * Real.log x ≤ 3 * Real.log x := by
        apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right _ hlx.le
        linarith
      linarith
    have hsq : Real.log (m * L.sc.X'' D.R.n) ^ 2 ≤ (2 * x ^ (ε / 3)) ^ 2 :=
      pow_le_pow_left₀ hLg0 hLg1 2
    have hA0 : 0 ≤ x ^ (ε / 4) := Real.rpow_nonneg hx0.le _
    have hN00 : 0 < x ^ (2 + 8 * ε - ε / 50) := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hx0 _
    have s1 : C₄ * x ^ (ε / 4) * Real.log (m * L.sc.X'' D.R.n) ^ 2 ≤
        |C₄| * x ^ (ε / 4) * (2 * x ^ (ε / 3)) ^ 2 := by
      apply mul_le_mul _ hsq (by positivity) (by positivity)
      exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (le_abs_self _) hA0
    have s2 : C₄ * x ^ (ε / 4) * Real.log (m * L.sc.X'' D.R.n) ^ 2 / m ≤
        |C₄| * x ^ (ε / 4) * (2 * x ^ (ε / 3)) ^ 2 / x ^ (2 + 8 * ε - ε / 50) := by
      calc _ ≤ |C₄| * x ^ (ε / 4) * (2 * x ^ (ε / 3)) ^ 2 / m := by gcongr
        _ ≤ _ := by gcongr
    refine le_trans s2 ?_
    have e : x ^ (-hG L.sc + ε) =
        x ^ (19 * ε / 300) * (x ^ (ε / 4) * (x ^ (ε / 3)) ^ 2 / x ^ (2 + 8 * ε - ε / 50)) := by
      rw [← Real.rpow_natCast (x ^ (ε / 3)) 2, ← Real.rpow_mul hx0.le, ← Real.rpow_add hx0, ← Real.rpow_sub hx0,
        ← Real.rpow_add hx0]
      congr 1
      unfold hG
      push_cast
      ring
    rw [e]
    have : |C₄| * x ^ (ε / 4) * (2 * x ^ (ε / 3)) ^ 2 / x ^ (2 + 8 * ε - ε / 50) =
        (4 * |C₄|) * (x ^ (ε / 4) * (x ^ (ε / 3)) ^ 2 / x ^ (2 + 8 * ε - ε / 50)) := by ring
    rw [this]
    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h2 (by positivity)
