[LGS_power_bands_01] One-power and all-power finite interval sums and original x^-1 specialization
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/TopChoiceBounds.lean#L1907-L2086
Range SHA256: 18113febf6a4e5209a5ce404f3908d48ffebe3a41242dacdad9c353853fee146
theorem prime_power_band_log_sum_le (X ρ P₀ : ℝ) (d : ℕ) (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hX : 1 ≤ X) (hρ0 : 0 ≤ ρ) (hρ : ρ ≤ 1 / 3) (hP : 1 ≤ P₀)
    (S : Finset ℕ)
    (hS : ∀ p ∈ S, P₀ ≤ (p : ℝ) ∧ (p : ℝ) < X ∧ X * (1 - ρ) ≤ (p : ℝ) ^ d ∧ (p : ℝ) ^ d ≤ X * (1 + ρ)) :
    ∑ p ∈ S, Real.log p / p ≤ Real.log X * (3 * ρ + 1 / P₀) := by
  have hlX : 0 ≤ Real.log X := Real.log_nonneg hX
  rcases S.eq_empty_or_nonempty with rfl | hne
  · simp only [Finset.sum_empty]; positivity
  set a := S.min' hne with ha
  set b := S.max' hne with hb
  have haS : a ∈ S := S.min'_mem hne
  have hbS : b ∈ S := S.max'_mem hne
  have hab : a ≤ b := S.min'_le b hbS
  obtain ⟨a1, _, a3, _⟩ := hS a haS
  obtain ⟨_, _, _, b4⟩ := hS b hbS
  have ha0 : (0 : ℝ) < a := by linarith
  have hX0 : 0 < X := by linarith
  have h1ρ : 0 < 1 - ρ := by linarith
  have habR : (a : ℝ) ≤ b := by exact_mod_cast hab
  have ht1 : 1 ≤ (b : ℝ) / a := (one_le_div ha0).2 habR
  have ht : (b : ℝ) / a ≤ 1 + 3 * ρ := by
    calc (b : ℝ) / a ≤ ((b : ℝ) / a) ^ d := le_self_pow₀ ht1 (by omega)
      _ = (b : ℝ) ^ d / (a : ℝ) ^ d := div_pow _ _ _
      _ ≤ X * (1 + ρ) / (X * (1 - ρ)) := div_le_div₀ (by positivity) b4 (by positivity) a3
      _ = (1 + ρ) / (1 - ρ) := mul_div_mul_left _ _ hX0.ne'
      _ ≤ 1 + 3 * ρ := by
          rw [div_le_iff₀ h1ρ]
          nlinarith
  have hba : (b : ℝ) ≤ (1 + 3 * ρ) * a := (div_le_iff₀ ha0).1 ht
  have hcard : (S.card : ℝ) ≤ 3 * ρ * a + 1 := by
    have hsub : S ⊆ Finset.Icc a b := fun p hp => Finset.mem_Icc.2 ⟨S.min'_le p hp, S.le_max' p hp⟩
    have h1 : S.card ≤ b + 1 - a := by
      have := Finset.card_le_card hsub
      rwa [Nat.card_Icc] at this
    have h2 : (S.card : ℝ) ≤ (b : ℝ) + 1 - a := by
      have : S.card + a ≤ b + 1 := by omega
      have : (S.card : ℝ) + a ≤ b + 1 := by exact_mod_cast this
      linarith
    linarith
  have hterm : ∀ p ∈ S, Real.log p / p ≤ Real.log X / a := by
    intro p hp
    obtain ⟨p1, p2, _, _⟩ := hS p hp
    have hp0 : (0 : ℝ) < p := by linarith
    have hap : (a : ℝ) ≤ p := by exact_mod_cast S.min'_le p hp
    exact div_le_div₀ hlX (Real.log_le_log hp0 p2.le) ha0 hap
  calc ∑ p ∈ S, Real.log p / p ≤ ∑ _p ∈ S, Real.log X / a := Finset.sum_le_sum hterm
    _ = S.card * (Real.log X / a) := by rw [Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul]
    _ ≤ (3 * ρ * a + 1) * (Real.log X / a) := by gcongr
    _ = Real.log X * (3 * ρ + 1 / a) := by field_simp
    _ ≤ Real.log X * (3 * ρ + 1 / P₀) := by gcongr

theorem prime_power_band_log_sum_le_log_sq (X ρ P₀ : ℝ) (hX : 1 ≤ X) (hρ0 : 0 ≤ ρ) (hρ : ρ ≤ 1 / 3) (hP : 1 ≤ P₀) (S : Finset ℕ)
    (hS : ∀ p ∈ S, p.Prime ∧ P₀ ≤ (p : ℝ) ∧ (p : ℝ) < X ∧
      ∃ d : ℕ, 1 ≤ d ∧ X * (1 - ρ) ≤ (p : ℝ) ^ d ∧ (p : ℝ) ^ d ≤ X * (1 + ρ)) :
    ∑ p ∈ S, Real.log p / p ≤ (1 + 2 * Real.log X) * (Real.log X * (3 * ρ + 1 / P₀)) := by
  classical
  have hlX : 0 ≤ Real.log X := Real.log_nonneg hX
  have hX0 : 0 < X := by linarith
  let dOf : ℕ → ℕ := fun p =>
    if h : ∃ d : ℕ, 1 ≤ d ∧ X * (1 - ρ) ≤ (p : ℝ) ^ d ∧ (p : ℝ) ^ d ≤ X * (1 + ρ) then h.choose else 0
  have hdOf : ∀ p ∈ S, 1 ≤ dOf p ∧ X * (1 - ρ) ≤ (p : ℝ) ^ dOf p ∧ (p : ℝ) ^ dOf p ≤ X * (1 + ρ) := by
    intro p hp
    have h := (hS p hp).2.2.2
    simp only [dOf, dif_pos h]
    exact h.choose_spec
  have hmaps : ∀ p ∈ S, dOf p ∈ Finset.Icc 1 ⌊1 + 2 * Real.log X⌋₊ := by
    intro p hp
    obtain ⟨h1, _, h3⟩ := hdOf p hp
    refine Finset.mem_Icc.2 ⟨h1, Nat.le_floor ?_⟩
    have hp2 : (2 : ℝ) ≤ p := by exact_mod_cast (hS p hp).1.two_le
    have hl2 : (1 / 2 : ℝ) ≤ Real.log 2 := by
      have := Real.log_two_gt_d9
      linarith
    have e1 : (dOf p : ℝ) * Real.log 2 ≤ Real.log 2 + Real.log X := by
      calc (dOf p : ℝ) * Real.log 2 ≤ (dOf p : ℝ) * Real.log p := by
            apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (Real.log_le_log (by norm_num) hp2) (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
        _ = Real.log ((p : ℝ) ^ dOf p) := (Real.log_pow _ _).symm
        _ ≤ Real.log (2 * X) := by
            apply Real.log_le_log (by positivity)
            nlinarith
        _ = Real.log 2 + Real.log X := Real.log_mul (by norm_num) hX0.ne'
    have hl20 : 0 < Real.log 2 := by linarith
    have e2 : ((dOf p : ℝ) - 1) * Real.log 2 ≤ Real.log X := by linarith
    by_contra hcon
    rw [not_le] at hcon
    have : 2 * Real.log X * (1 / 2) ≤ ((dOf p : ℝ) - 1) * Real.log 2 := by
      apply mul_le_mul (by linarith) hl2 (by norm_num) (by linarith)
    have h4 : 2 * Real.log X < (dOf p : ℝ) - 1 := by linarith
    nlinarith
  rw [← Finset.sum_fiberwise_of_maps_to hmaps]
  have hB0 : 0 ≤ Real.log X * (3 * ρ + 1 / P₀) := by positivity
  calc ∑ d ∈ Finset.Icc 1 ⌊1 + 2 * Real.log X⌋₊, ∑ p ∈ S with dOf p = d, Real.log p / p
      ≤ ∑ _d ∈ Finset.Icc 1 ⌊1 + 2 * Real.log X⌋₊, Real.log X * (3 * ρ + 1 / P₀) := by
        apply Finset.sum_le_sum
        intro d hd
        apply prime_power_band_log_sum_le X ρ P₀ d (Finset.mem_Icc.1 hd).1 hX hρ0 hρ hP
        intro p hp
        obtain ⟨hpS, hpd⟩ := Finset.mem_filter.1 hp
        obtain ⟨_, q2, q3, _⟩ := hS p hpS
        obtain ⟨_, r2, r3⟩ := hdOf p hpS
        rw [hpd] at r2 r3
        exact ⟨q2, q3, r2, r3⟩
    _ = (⌊1 + 2 * Real.log X⌋₊ : ℝ) * (Real.log X * (3 * ρ + 1 / P₀)) := by
        rw [Finset.sum_const, Nat.card_Icc, nsmul_eq_mul]
        simp
    _ ≤ _ := by
        apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right _ hB0
        exact Nat.floor_le (by positivity)

theorem prime_power_band_sum_at_scale {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) (r : ℝ) (hρ : r ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ)) (hK : SizeK L)
    (h2 : 2 ≤ L.sc.x) (h12 : 12 ≤ L.sc.x ^ (1 - 2 * L.sc.eps / 3)) (Pd : ℕ → Prop) {hd : DecidablePred Pd}
    (hP : ∀ p : ℕ, Pd p → p.Prime ∧ L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ) ∧ (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' ∧
      ∃ d : ℕ, 1 ≤ d ∧ L.sc.X' * (1 - r) ≤ (p : ℝ) ^ d ∧ (p : ℝ) ^ d ≤ L.sc.X' * (1 + r)) :
    ∑ p ∈ (Finset.range ⌈L.sc.X'⌉₊).filter Pd, Real.log p / p ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-1 : ℝ) := by
  set x := L.sc.x with hxdef
  set ε := L.sc.eps with hεdef
  have hx0 : 0 < x := by linarith
  have hx1 : 1 ≤ x := by linarith
  have hε : 0 < ε := L.sc.eps_pos
  have hxm1 : 0 ≤ x ^ (-1 : ℝ) := Real.rpow_nonneg hx0.le _
  rcases lt_or_ge r 0 with hneg | hρ0
  · have hX0 : 0 < L.sc.X' := by unfold Scale.X'; exact Real.exp_pos _
    rw [Finset.sum_eq_zero]
    · exact hxm1
    · intro p hp
      exfalso
      obtain ⟨_, _, _, d, _, e1, e2⟩ := hP p (Finset.mem_filter.1 hp).2
      nlinarith
  · have hlX : Real.log L.sc.X' = x ^ (ε / 3) := by unfold Scale.X'; exact Real.log_exp _
    have hB1 : 1 ≤ x ^ (ε / 3) := Real.one_le_rpow hx1 (by positivity)
    have hX1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.X' := by
      unfold Scale.X'
      exact Real.one_le_exp (Real.rpow_nonneg hx0.le _)
    have hx2 : x ^ (-2 : ℝ) ≤ 1 / 4 := by
      rw [Real.rpow_neg hx0.le, ← one_div]
      apply one_div_le_one_div_of_le (by norm_num)
      have : x ^ (2 : ℝ) = x ^ 2 := by exact_mod_cast Real.rpow_natCast x 2
      rw [this]
      nlinarith
    have hk1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ D.R.k := by
      have h0 : (0 : ℝ) < D.R.k := lt_of_lt_of_le (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hx0 _) hK.1
      have : 0 < D.R.k := by exact_mod_cast h0
      exact_mod_cast this
    have hp0 : x ^ (2 : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.p0 D.R.k := by
      show x ^ (2 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * x ^ Scale.A
      have hA : x ^ (2 : ℝ) ≤ x ^ Scale.A := Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le hx1 (by unfold Scale.A; norm_num)
      have hk2 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 := one_le_pow₀ hk1
      have hA0 : 0 ≤ x ^ Scale.A := Real.rpow_nonneg hx0.le _
      calc x ^ (2 : ℝ) ≤ x ^ Scale.A := hA
        _ = 1 * x ^ Scale.A := (one_mul _).symm
        _ ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * x ^ Scale.A := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hk2 hA0
    have hx22 : 1 ≤ x ^ (2 : ℝ) := Real.one_le_rpow hx1 (by norm_num)
    have hp01 : 1 ≤ L.sc.p0 D.R.k := le_trans hx22 hp0
    have hinv : 1 / L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ x ^ (-2 : ℝ) := by
      rw [Real.rpow_neg hx0.le, ← one_div]
      exact one_div_le_one_div_of_le (by linarith) hp0
    have hb := prime_power_band_log_sum_le_log_sq L.sc.X' r (L.sc.p0 D.R.k) hX1 hρ0 (by linarith) hp01
      ((Finset.range ⌈L.sc.X'⌉₊).filter Pd)
      (fun p hp => hP p (Finset.mem_filter.1 hp).2)
    refine le_trans hb ?_
    rw [hlX]
    set B := x ^ (ε / 3) with hB
    have hm2 : 0 ≤ x ^ (-2 : ℝ) := Real.rpow_nonneg hx0.le _
    have s1 : (1 + 2 * B) * (B * (3 * r + 1 / L.sc.p0 D.R.k)) ≤ (3 * B) * (B * (4 * x ^ (-2 : ℝ))) := by
      apply mul_le_mul (by linarith) _ (by positivity) (by positivity)
      apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) (by linarith)
    refine le_trans s1 ?_
    have e : x ^ (-1 : ℝ) = x ^ (1 - 2 * ε / 3) * (B * B * x ^ (-2 : ℝ)) := by
      rw [hB, ← Real.rpow_add hx0, ← Real.rpow_add hx0, ← Real.rpow_add hx0]
      congr 1
      ring
    rw [e]
    have : 3 * B * (B * (4 * x ^ (-2 : ℝ))) = 12 * (B * B * x ^ (-2 : ℝ)) := by ring
    rw [this]
    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h12 (by positivity)

theorem band_sum_statement_at_scale {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) (K : Consts) (hle : rho D K L.sc ≤ rhoB D K L.sc)
    (hρ : rhoB D K L.sc ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ)) (hK : SizeK L)
    (h2 : 2 ≤ L.sc.x) (h12 : 12 ≤ L.sc.x ^ (1 - 2 * L.sc.eps / 3)) : CornerGLContinuity.OrdersLargePrimeBound D L K :=
  ⟨le_trans hle hρ, prime_power_band_sum_at_scale L (rho D K L.sc) (le_trans hle hρ) hK h2 h12 _ (fun _ hp => hp), hle, hρ,
    prime_power_band_sum_at_scale L (rhoB D K L.sc) hρ hK h2 h12 _ (fun _ hp => hp)⟩

def HypI (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (K : Consts) (p n' N : ℕ) : Prop :=
  let s := L.sc
  let Y := s.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)
  let Yb := s.x ^ s.eps / 20
  let Ib := Nstar D s * s.x ^ s.eps * La K s * s.b' / D.R.k
  (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Y ^ 2 ≤ p ∧ (p : ℝ) < s.a / s.b' ∧ rho D K s < 1 / 6 ∧
  2 * N1 s + 2 ≤ D.R.k - 1 ∧
  8 * Real.log ((N : ℝ) * Y) ≤ La K s ∧
  Yb * (N : ℝ) * La K s * s.b' / D.R.k ≤ Ib ∧ 2 * Ib ≤ s.b' ∧
  K.kI ≤ Yb ∧
  (D.R.k : ℝ) * Y ^ 2 * Yb * (N : ℝ) * La K s * Real.log s.a ≤ s.X' ∧
  K.kI * (N : ℝ) * La K s ^ 2 ≤ D.R.k ∧ 2 * Yb * (N : ℝ) * La K s < D.R.k ∧
  cG L ≤ La K s * s.b' / D.R.k ∧
  (N : ℝ) ≤ NplusF s n' ∧
  K.kI * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3 * (N : ℝ) * Qp s p ^ 4 *
    ((8 * ((DIf K L p n' : ℝ) + 1)) + (N : ℝ) * cG L + (n' : ℝ) * cG L) * Y ≤ s.b'

def adm7 (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ (p n' : ℕ) (W Qq O Kf : Type) [CommRing W] [IsDomain W] [IsDiscreteValuationRing W] [Field Qq]
    [Algebra W Qq] [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (ι : Qq →+* Kf) (σ : Qq ≃+* Qq) (j : W →+* O) (w : O),
    UnramifiedQuadraticSetting p n' W Qq O Kf ι σ j w →
    ∀ TT : Finset (Fin n'),
      IsGoodOrbit n' TT (L2 D L.sc) (KG L.sc) (rG L.sc) L.sc.delta1 (hG L.sc) L.sc.x →
      L2 D L.sc < goodU L.sc.delta1 → 3 * Gp L.sc ≤ n' →
    L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ) → (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' → ¬ InBand0 D K L.sc p →
    HypI D L K p n' TT.card →
    ∀ a b : Fin n' → ℤ, (∀ s ∈ TT, a s ∈ winA D L.sc ∧ b s ∈ winB D K L.sc) →
    ∀ lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (D.R.k - 1 + 1) → ℤ, IsBaseWeight TT a b ⌊cG L⌋₊ lam0 → IsDominant lam0 →
    LPprime (W := W) p n' ι σ w 1 lam0 ⌈tIf K L p n' TT a b⌉₊ (DIf K L p n') (X.kapI p n' TT a b)
      (8 * (DIf K L p n' + 1))

def adm8 (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (Cm : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (p n' : ℕ), p.Prime → ∀ TT : Finset (Fin n'),
    IsGoodOrbit n' TT (L2 D L.sc) (KG L.sc) (rG L.sc) L.sc.delta1 (hG L.sc) L.sc.x →
      L2 D L.sc < goodU L.sc.delta1 → 3 * Gp L.sc ≤ n' →
    L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ) → (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' → ¬ InBand0 D K L.sc p → HypI D L K p n' TT.card →
    (∀ a b, 0 ≤ X.kapI p n' TT a b) ∧
    meanLoc D K L.sc n' TT (X.kapI p n' TT) ≤
      Cm * (TT.card : ℝ) * (N1 L.sc : ℝ) / L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)

def StdSubAt (D : D7) : Prop :=
  ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w))
    (v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV),
    ∃ (Os Ks : Type) (_ : CommRing Os) (_ : IsDomain Os) (_ : IsDiscreteValuationRing Os) (_ : Field Ks)
      (_ : Algebra Os Ks) (ιs : (D.gat u w hg).P.Qq v →+* Ks) (js : (D.gat u w hg).P.W v →+* Os) (ws : Os)
      (φ : Os →+* (D.gat u w hg).O) (φK : Ks →+* (D.gat u w hg).Kf),
      (∀ x : Os, φK (algebraMap Os Ks x) = algebraMap (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf (φ x)) ∧ φ.Finite ∧
      UnramifiedQuadraticSetting (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v) ((D.gat u w hg).P.W v) ((D.gat u w hg).P.Qq v)
        Os Ks ιs ((D.gat u w hg).P.σ v) js ws ∧
      φK.comp ιs = (D.gat u w hg).P.ι v ∧ φ.comp js = (D.gat u w hg).P.j v ∧ φ ws = (D.gat u w hg).P.w

def HypIAt (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ p, Cand D L K p → L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ) → ∀ t0 ∈ goodReps X p,
    L2 D L.sc < goodU L.sc.delta1 ∧ 3 * Gp L.sc ≤ addOrderOf (hP D p) ∧
    Formalism.MainNotions.AuxBlock8.HypI D L K p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0).card ∧
    ∀ s ∈ topsPos D p t0, wPos D p t0 (aL L) s ∈ winA D L.sc ∧ wPos D p t0 (bL L) s ∈ winB D K L.sc

def HypIIAt (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ p, Cand D L K p → (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k → ∀ t0 ∈ goodReps X p,
    2 * L1 D L.sc p ≤ L2 D L.sc ∧ L2 D L.sc < goodU L.sc.delta1 ∧ 3 * Gp L.sc ≤ addOrderOf (hP D p) ∧
    ((topsPos D p t0).card : ℝ) ≤ NplusF L.sc (addOrderOf (hP D p)) ∧
    (∀ s ∈ topsPos D p t0, wPos D p t0 (aL L) s ∈ winA D L.sc ∧ wPos D p t0 (bL L) s ∈ winB D K L.sc) ∧
    (∀ s ∈ topsPos D p t0, (L.sc.a - cG L) + (⌊cG L⌋₊ : ℝ) ≤ (wPos D p t0 (aL L) s : ℝ) ∧
      (wPos D p t0 (aL L) s : ℝ) ≤ 2 * (L.sc.a - cG L) - (⌊cG L⌋₊ : ℝ) ∧
      (wPos D p t0 (bL L) s : ℝ) + 2 * (⌊cG L⌋₊ : ℝ) ≤ c0num L) ∧
    13 ≤ p ∧ 2 ≤ rG L.sc ∧ N1 L.sc ^ 2 ≤ rG L.sc * (topsPos D p t0).card ∧
    (rG L.sc : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ Scale.A ∧
    (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * L.sc.x ^ (3 * Scale.A) ≤ (p : ℝ) ^ ((L1 D L.sc p : ℤ) - 1) ∧
    256 * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * ((topsPos D p t0).card : ℝ) * (L.sc.x ^ Scale.A) ^ 2 ≤
      (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * L.sc.x ^ (3 * Scale.A) ∧
    N1 L.sc + 2 * rG L.sc + rG L.sc ≤ D.R.k ∧
    K.kII' * (N1 L.sc : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.log L.sc.a ≤ D.R.k ∧
    K.szII * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 3 * (rG L.sc : ℝ) ^ 2 * ((topsPos D p t0).card : ℝ) * Qp L.sc p ^ 5 *
        ((8 * ((DIIf K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) : ℝ) + 1)) +
          ((rG L.sc : ℝ) * ((topsPos D p t0).card : ℝ) * c0num L +
            2 * (rG L.sc : ℝ) * ((addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℕ) : ℝ) * cG L)) *
        L.sc.x ^ Scale.A ≤ L.sc.a - cG L

theorem linear_programming_input_of_hypotheses (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts)
    (h6 : Formalism.MainNotions.AuxBlock8.adm6 D L K) (h7 : Formalism.MainNotions.AuxBlock8.adm7 D L X K) (hStd : StdSubAt D) (hBC : LPBaseChange)
    (hI : HypIAt D L X K) (hII : HypIIAt D L X K) (hrho : rho D K L.sc ≤ rhoB D K L.sc) :
    Formalism.MainNotions.CornerGLContinuity.OrbitLatticeBounds D L X Y K := by
  intro hT hLT u w hg
  dsimp only
  intro t0 ht0
  simp only [VV] at ht0
  split_ifs at ht0 with hcs
  swap
  · exact absurd ht0 (Finset.notMem_empty _)
  obtain ⟨hcand, -⟩ := hcs
  have hgood := ht0
  obtain ⟨hrep, hGood⟩ := Finset.mem_filter.1 ht0
  obtain ⟨hmm, -, hc, hr, -, -, horb⟩ := hLT u w hg
  obtain ⟨hn', hpos⟩ := horb t0 hrep
  have hk := hT.gat_k u w hg
  have hc0 : IsResidueClassZero D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) := hcand.2.2.1
  set v := Y.vOf u w hg t0 with hv
  have hm : D.R.k - 1 = (D.gat u w hg).P.m := by omega
  refine LPprime_cast hn'.symm hm ((D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v) ?_

  have hcl : (Y.Fl u w hg).c ≤ ⌊cG L⌋₊ := Nat.le_floor hc
  have hmemTT : ∀ s : Fin (addOrderOf (hP D (D.what₂.places.resChar w))),
      (Fin.cast hn'.symm s ∈ (Y.Fl u w hg).TT v ↔ s ∈ topsPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0) := by
    intro s
    rw [(hpos (Fin.cast hn'.symm s)).2.1]
    simp only [topsPos, Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_univ, true_and, Fin.val_cast]
  have hbase : IsBaseWeight (topsPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0) (wPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0 (aL L)) (wPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0 (bL L)) ⌊cG L⌋₊
      (fun (s : Fin (addOrderOf (hP D (D.what₂.places.resChar w)))) (i : Fin (D.R.k - 1 + 1)) =>
        (D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v (Fin.cast hn'.symm s) (Fin.cast (by rw [hm]) i)) := by
    intro s
    have hB := (Y.Fl u w hg).base v (Fin.cast hn'.symm s)
    refine ⟨fun hs => ?_, fun hs => ?_⟩
    · have hs' := (hmemTT s).2 hs
      obtain ⟨h0, hl, hmid⟩ := hB.1 hs'
      obtain ⟨ha, hb⟩ := (hpos (Fin.cast hn'.symm s)).2.2.1 hs'
      refine ⟨?_, ?_, fun i hi0 him => ?_⟩
      · have e : (Fin.cast (by rw [hm]) (0 : Fin (D.R.k - 1 + 1)) : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.m + 1)) = 0 := Fin.ext rfl
        simp only [e, h0, ha, wPos, Fin.val_cast]
      · have e : (Fin.cast (by rw [hm]) (Fin.last (D.R.k - 1)) : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.m + 1)) = Fin.last (D.gat u w hg).P.m :=
          Fin.ext (by simp [hm])
        simp only [e, hl, hb, wPos, Fin.val_cast]
      · exact (hmid _ (by simpa using hi0) (by simp only [Fin.val_cast]; omega)).trans (by exact_mod_cast hcl)
    · have hs' : Fin.cast hn'.symm s ∉ (Y.Fl u w hg).TT v := fun h => hs ((hmemTT s).1 h)
      exact fun i => (hB.2 hs' _).trans (by exact_mod_cast hcl)
  have hdom : IsDominant (fun (s : Fin (addOrderOf (hP D (D.what₂.places.resChar w)))) (i : Fin (D.R.k - 1 + 1)) =>
      (D.gat u w hg).P.lam0 v (Fin.cast hn'.symm s) (Fin.cast (by rw [hm]) i)) := by
    intro s a b hab
    exact ((D.gat u w hg).P.dom v (Fin.cast hn'.symm s)) ((Fin.cast_le_cast _).2 hab)
  obtain ⟨Os, Ks, _, _, _, _, _, ιs, js, ws, φ, φK, hcomp, hfin, hSs, hι, hj, hw⟩ := hStd u w hg v
  have hSet := hSs
  rw [hn'] at hSet
  have hSetR : HidaSetting (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (addOrderOf (hP D (D.what₂.places.resChar w))) ((D.gat u w hg).P.W v)
      ((D.gat u w hg).P.Qq v) (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf (φK.comp ιs) ((D.gat u w hg).P.σ v) (φ.comp js) (φ ws) := by
    rw [hι, hj, hw, ← hn']; exact (D.gat u w hg).P.setting v
  rw [← hι, ← hw]
  refine hBC _ _ _ _ Os Ks _ _ ιs _ js ws φ φK hcomp hfin hSet.1 hSetR _ _ _ _ _ _ _ ?_
  have hNv : Nv X (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0 = (topsPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0).card := Nv_eq_card X hc0 hrep
  have hnP : nP D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) = addOrderOf (hP D (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) := period_eq_addOrderOf_class_zero hc0
  by_cases hp0 : ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k
  · obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3, h4, hwin, hbnd, h13, hr2, hN1, hrY, hpL, h256, hk3, hkLa, hsz⟩ := hII _ hcand hp0 t0 hgood
    have hrr : (D.gat u w hg).P.r = rG L.sc := by rw [hr, if_pos hp0]
    rw [hrr]
    have htv : tv X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0 = tIIf K L (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (addOrderOf (hP D (D.what₂.places.resChar w))) (topsPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0).card := by
      unfold tv; rw [if_pos hp0, hnP, hNv]
    have hDp : Dp K L (D.what₂.places.resChar w) = DIIf K L (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (addOrderOf (hP D (D.what₂.places.resChar w))) := by unfold Dp; rw [if_pos hp0, hnP]
    have hkap : kapv X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0 = kapIIf K L (topsPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0).card := by
      unfold kapv kapLoc; rw [if_pos hp0]
    rw [htv, hDp, hkap]
    exact h6 (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (addOrderOf (hP D (D.what₂.places.resChar w))) ((D.gat u w hg).P.W v) ((D.gat u w hg).P.Qq v) Os Ks ιs ((D.gat u w hg).P.σ v) js ws hSet
      (topsPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0) hGood h1 h2 h3 h4 hp0 (wPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0 (aL L)) (wPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0 (bL L)) hwin hbnd _ hbase hdom
      h13 hr2 hN1 hrY hpL h256 hk3 hkLa hsz
  · have hp0' : L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : ℝ) := le_of_not_gt hp0
    obtain ⟨h2, h3, hHI, hwin⟩ := hI _ hcand hp0' t0 hgood
    have hrr : (D.gat u w hg).P.r = 1 := by rw [hr, if_neg hp0]
    rw [hrr]
    have htv : tv X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0 =
        tIf K L (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (addOrderOf (hP D (D.what₂.places.resChar w))) (topsPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0) (wPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0 (aL L)) (wPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0 (bL L)) := by
      unfold tv; rw [if_neg hp0]
    have hDp : Dp K L (D.what₂.places.resChar w) = DIf K L (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (addOrderOf (hP D (D.what₂.places.resChar w))) := by unfold Dp; rw [if_neg hp0, hnP]
    have hkap : kapv X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0 =
        X.kapI (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (addOrderOf (hP D (D.what₂.places.resChar w))) (topsPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0) (wPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0 (aL L)) (wPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0 (bL L)) := by
      unfold kapv kapLoc; rw [if_neg hp0]
    rw [htv, hDp, hkap]
    exact h7 (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (addOrderOf (hP D (D.what₂.places.resChar w))) ((D.gat u w hg).P.W v) ((D.gat u w hg).P.Qq v) Os Ks ιs ((D.gat u w hg).P.σ v) js ws hSet
      (topsPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0) hGood h2 h3 hp0' hcand.2.1 (fun h0 => hcand.2.2.2.2 hp0' (inBand_of_inBand0 hrho h0)) hHI (wPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0 (aL L))
      (wPos D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) t0 (bL L)) hwin _ hbase hdom

theorem family_second_bound_of_theorem {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (C : ℝ) (hC0 : 0 ≤ C) (hCk : C ≤ Fm.GK.kII) (hCt : C ≤ Fm.GK.tII)
    (hCL : C ≤ Fm.GK.La) (hCD : C ≤ Fm.GK.DII) (hCs : C ≤ Fm.GK.szII) (hCk' : C ≤ Fm.GK.kII')
    (h14 : tableLineDegreeFive.OrbitValuationBound Fm C) : Adm6F Fm := by
  have h14' : Ev Fm fun x i => tableLineDegreeFive.aux₃ (Fm.D x i) C := h14
  obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := h14'.and (Ev.x1 Fm)
  refine ⟨x0, fun x hx i => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨hp, hx1⟩ := h x hx i
  have P := Fm.params x hx1 i
  obtain ⟨hk, _, hSc⟩ := Nm_le_Sc P
  obtain ⟨hw1, hw2⟩ := w''_le (Fm.L x i).sc (Fm.D x i).R.n (Fm.D x i).R.n_pos'
  have hkS := P.kS_le
  have hab := two_b'_le_a (Fm.L x i).sc
  have hkSc : 5 * (Fm.L x i).Sc ≤ ((Fm.D x i).R.k : ℝ) * (Fm.L x i).Sc :=
    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hk (by linarith)
  exact Joints.type_two_hypothesis_of_counts _ _ _ C hC0 hCk hCt hCL hCD hCs hCk' (by unfold cG; linarith) (by unfold cG; linarith) hp

    (by unfold cG; have := (Fm.L x i).Sc_pos; linarith) (h13 x i)

theorem hypIAt_ev {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (hLa : 0 < Fm.GK.La) (hCN : CornerGLContinuity.FamilyWindowLogBound Fm) :
    Ev Fm fun x i => Joints.HypIAt (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) Fm.GK := by
  obtain ⟨i0⟩ := Fm.Ix_zero
  have heps : 0 < eps := by
    have := (Fm.L 0 i0).sc.eps_pos
    rwa [Fm.L_eps] at this
  obtain ⟨C31, _, h1⟩ := TopChoiceBounds.family_uniform_constant_holds Fm
  have h1' : Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.adm1 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) C31 := h1
  have h4 : Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.ArchimedeanSignatureMatching (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) Fm.GK := TopChoiceBounds.family_band_ratio_statement Fm hCN
  have h5 := family_sizes_bound Fm hLa (eps / 48) (by positivity)
  have hx31 : Ev Fm fun x i => CornerGLContinuity.LargeScaleConditions (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) := TopChoiceBounds.family_large_scale_conditions_hold Fm
  obtain ⟨xa, hA⟩ := Filter.eventually_atTop.1 ((AuxBlock8.Pf.numMean_ev eps Fm.GK Fm.GK_nonneg 0 le_rfl).and
    (AuxBlock8.Pf.restHypI_ev eps Fm.GK Fm.GK_nonneg 0 C31))
  obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := ((((h1'.and h4).and h5).and hx31).and (eventually_sizes_admitted Fm)).and (Ev.x1 Fm)
  refine ⟨max x0 xa, fun x hx i => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨⟨⟨⟨⟨a1, a4⟩, a5⟩, a31⟩, aSz⟩, hx1⟩ := h x (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) hx) i
  obtain ⟨hNM, hRH⟩ := hA x (le_trans (le_max_right _ _) hx)
  have hxx := Fm.L_x x i hx1
  have hee := Fm.L_eps x i
  have NM := hNM (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) hxx hee aSz.1 a5
  have ZetaZeros := hRH (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) hxx hee aSz a5 a4 a1 NM
  obtain ⟨w⟩ := exists_place (Fm.D x i)
  obtain ⟨_, _, _, _, _, _, hLaG⟩ := Fm.fills x hx1 i w
  intro p hc hp0 t0 ht0
  exact ⟨a31.1, a31.2.2 _ hc.2.2.2.1, ZetaZeros p hc hp0 t0 ht0, windows_of Fm.GK (Fm.params x hx1 i) hLaG p t0⟩


[LGS_canonical_mean_01] Candidate, actual deficit, meanLoc, MeanKappaBound, Sv/tauP/ioP
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/IsResidueClassZero.lean#L270-L354
Range SHA256: c218f82cff51776de21bc12612cb960842bbb7a77d13f438d5a80df820c276cc
  if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k then DIIf K L p (nP D p) else DIf K L p (nP D p)

def tv (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : ℝ :=
  if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k then tIIf K L p (nP D p) (Nv X p t0)
  else tIf K L p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0) (wPos D p t0 a) (wPos D p t0 b)

def kapv (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : ℝ :=
  kapLoc X K p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0) (wPos D p t0 a) (wPos D p t0 b)

def Cand (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (K : Consts) (p : ℕ) : Prop :=
  p.Prime ∧ (p : ℝ) < L.sc.X' ∧ IsResidueClassZero D p ∧ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) ≤ (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) ∧
    (L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ) → ¬ InBand D K L.sc p)

open Classical in

def goodReps (X : Inst D L) (p : ℕ) : Finset (HH D) := (reps X p).filter (fun t0 => Good X p t0)

def Sig (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  (D.R.m : ℝ) * ∑ t0 ∈ goodReps X p, kapv X K a b p t0

open Classical in

def VV (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) : Finset (HH D) :=
  if Cand D L K p ∧ Sig X K a b p ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-L.sc.eps) then goodReps X p else ∅

def ServedOrbitsContained (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) : Prop :=
  ∀ p, Cand D L K p → Sig X K a b p ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-L.sc.eps) → goodReps X p ⊆ VV X K a b p

theorem served_orbits_in_set (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) : ServedOrbitsContained X K a b := by
  intro p hc hs
  have hv : VV X K a b p = goodReps X p := by
    unfold VV
    exact if_pos ⟨hc, hs⟩
  exact hv ▸ Finset.Subset.refl (goodReps X p)

def winA (D : D7) (s : Scale) : Finset ℤ :=
  Finset.Icc ⌈s.a⌉ ⌊s.a + s.a / ((D.R.k : ℝ) * s.x ^ (Scale.A / 2))⌋

def winB (D : D7) (K : Consts) (s : Scale) : Finset ℤ :=
  Finset.Icc ⌈s.b'⌉ ⌊s.b' + Nstar D s * s.x ^ s.eps * La K s * s.b' / D.R.k⌋

open Classical in

def box (D : D7) (W : Finset ℤ) : Finset (HH D → ℤ) :=
  Fintype.piFinset (fun t => if t ∈ D.host.TT then W else {0})

def meanW (D : D7) (K : Consts) (s : Scale) (F : (HH D → ℤ) → (HH D → ℤ) → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (∑ a ∈ box D (winA D s), ∑ b ∈ box D (winB D K s), F a b) /
    (((box D (winA D s)).card : ℝ) * ((box D (winB D K s)).card : ℝ))

open Classical in

def boxLoc (n' : ℕ) (TT : Finset (Fin n')) (W : Finset ℤ) : Finset (Fin n' → ℤ) :=
  Fintype.piFinset (fun i => if i ∈ TT then W else {0})

def meanLoc (D : D7) (K : Consts) (s : Scale) (n' : ℕ) (TT : Finset (Fin n'))
    (F : (Fin n' → ℤ) → (Fin n' → ℤ) → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (∑ a ∈ boxLoc n' TT (winA D s), ∑ b ∈ boxLoc n' TT (winB D K s), F a b) /
    (((boxLoc n' TT (winA D s)).card : ℝ) * ((boxLoc n' TT (winB D K s)).card : ℝ))

def MeanKappaBound (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ p, Cand D L K p → ∀ t0 ∈ goodReps X p,
    meanLoc D K L.sc (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0)
      (fun a b => kapLoc X K p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0) a b) ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (-12 : ℝ) ∧
    ((p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k → ∀ a b, kapv X K a b p t0 ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (-12 : ℝ))

def Sv (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : ℝ :=
  (⌈∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, max 0 (gU X K a b p j t0 - Pr X K b p j t0 - max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0)⌉ : ℤ)

open Classical in

def tauP (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  if VV X K a b p = ∅ then 0 else
    (X.ep p : ℝ) + ∑ t0 ∈ VV X K a b p,
      (D.R.m : ℝ) * ((⌈tv X K a b p t0⌉₊ : ℝ) + (Dp K L p : ℝ) + 1)

open Classical in

def ioP (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  if h : (VV X K a b p).Nonempty then
    2 * Sig X K a b p * (VV X K a b p).sup' h (fun t0 => Sv X K a b p t0)
  else 0

open Classical in



[LGS_canonical_mean_02] NumMean, pointwise small-prime bound, canonical large-prime mean and nonnegativity
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/LogarithmicSums/SlopeEstimateOfLemmaBSlopeLine.lean#L153-L257
Range SHA256: 630561a4e4c1a70021e0edac3534a6fb25d27bf92269feb31d6d595ccd0e15d1
structure NumMean (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (K : Consts) (Cm : ℝ) : Prop where

  kII : K.kII * (rG L.sc : ℝ) ^ 2 * (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (-Scale.A) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-12 : ℝ)

  kI : Cm * (N1 L.sc : ℝ) / L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-12 : ℝ)

  second_level_lt_good_bound : L2 D L.sc < goodU L.sc.delta1

  Gp : 3 * (Gp L.sc : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4)

  winA : 1 ≤ L.sc.a / ((D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2))

  winB : 1 ≤ Nstar D L.sc * L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps * La K L.sc * L.sc.b' / D.R.k

  tot : (L.sc.x ^ L.sc.eps + 1) * (D.R.m : ℝ) * (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (-12 : ℝ) *
    (L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 3) * (2 + L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 3))) ≤ L.sc.x ^ (-9 : ℝ) / 4

def RestHypI (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ p, Cand D L K p → L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ) → ∀ t0 ∈ goodReps X p,
    HypI D L K p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0).card

theorem inBand_of_inBand0 (D : D7) (K : Consts) (s : Scale) (h : rho D K s ≤ rhoB D K s) (p : ℕ)
    (hb : InBand0 D K s p) : InBand D K s p := by
  obtain ⟨h1, h2, d, hd, hl, hu⟩ := hb
  have hX : 0 ≤ s.X' := le_trans (Nat.cast_nonneg p) h2.le
  unfold InBand
  refine ⟨h1, h2, d, hd, le_trans ?_ hl, le_trans hu ?_⟩
  · exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) hX
  · exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) hX

theorem meanLoc_const_le (K : Consts) (s : Scale) (n' : ℕ) (TT : Finset (Fin n')) (c : ℝ) (hc : 0 ≤ c) :
    meanLoc D K s n' TT (fun _ _ => c) ≤ c := by
  unfold meanLoc
  simp only [Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul]
  rw [← mul_assoc, mul_div_right_comm]
  rcases eq_or_ne (((boxLoc n' TT (winA D s)).card : ℝ) * ((boxLoc n' TT (winB D K s)).card : ℝ)) 0 with h | h
  · rw [h, div_zero, zero_mul]
    exact hc
  · rw [div_self h, one_mul]

theorem kapIIf_nonneg (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (L : SizeData₂ D) (N : ℕ) : 0 ≤ kapIIf K L N := by
  have hk : 0 ≤ K.kII := hK.2.2.1
  have hx : 0 < L.sc.x := lt_trans zero_lt_one L.sc.one_lt_alt
  unfold kapIIf
  exact le_min zero_le_one (by positivity)

theorem kappa_nonneg_and_mean_bound (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (Cm : ℝ) (h8 : adm8 D L X K Cm) (N : NumMean D L K Cm)
    (hI : RestHypI X K) (hρ : rho D K L.sc ≤ rhoB D K L.sc) (p : ℕ) (hc : Cand D L K p) (hp : L.sc.p0 D.R.k ≤ (p : ℝ)) (t0 : HH D)
    (ht0 : t0 ∈ goodReps X p) :
    (∀ a b, 0 ≤ X.kapI p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0) a b) ∧
    meanLoc D K L.sc (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0) (X.kapI p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0)) ≤
      Cm * ((topsPos D p t0).card : ℝ) * (N1 L.sc : ℝ) / L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) := by
  have hg := (mem_goodReps ht0).2
  refine h8 p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) hc.1 (topsPos D p t0) hg N.second_level_lt_good_bound ?_ hp hc.2.1 ?_ (hI p hc hp t0 ht0)
  · have h2 : (3 * Gp L.sc : ℝ) ≤ (addOrderOf (hP D p) : ℝ) := N.Gp.trans hc.2.2.2.1
    exact_mod_cast h2
  · intro h0
    exact hc.2.2.2.2 hp (inBand_of_inBand0 D K L.sc hρ p h0)

theorem slopeEstimate_of_lemmaB_slopeLine (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (Cm : ℝ) (h8 : adm8 D L X K Cm)
    (N : NumMean D L K Cm) (hI : RestHypI X K) (hρ : rho D K L.sc ≤ rhoB D K L.sc) : Conclusion2 D L X K := by
  intro p hc t0 ht0
  have hr : t0 ∈ reps X p := (mem_goodReps ht0).1
  have hN := Nv_eq X p hc.2.2.1 t0 hr
  have hII : kapIIf K L (topsPos D p t0).card ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (-12 : ℝ) := by
    rw [hN]
    unfold kapIIf
    have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left N.kII (Nat.cast_nonneg (topsPos D p t0).card : (0 : ℝ) ≤ _)
    refine le_trans (min_le_right _ _) ?_
    calc K.kII * ((topsPos D p t0).card : ℝ) * (rG L.sc : ℝ) ^ 2 * (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (-Scale.A)
        = ((topsPos D p t0).card : ℝ) * (K.kII * (rG L.sc : ℝ) ^ 2 * (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (-Scale.A)) := by ring
      _ ≤ _ := this
  by_cases hp : (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k
  · constructor
    · simp only [kapLoc, if_pos hp]
      exact le_trans (meanLoc_const_le K L.sc _ _ _ (kapIIf_nonneg K hK L _)) hII
    · intro _ a b
      unfold kapv kapLoc
      rw [if_pos hp]
      exact hII
  · constructor
    · simp only [kapLoc, if_neg hp]
      refine le_trans (kappa_nonneg_and_mean_bound X K Cm h8 N hI hρ p hc (not_lt.1 hp) t0 ht0).2 ?_
      rw [hN]
      have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left N.kI (Nat.cast_nonneg (topsPos D p t0).card : (0 : ℝ) ≤ _)
      calc Cm * ((topsPos D p t0).card : ℝ) * (N1 L.sc : ℝ) / L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)
          = ((topsPos D p t0).card : ℝ) * (Cm * (N1 L.sc : ℝ) / L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) := by ring
        _ ≤ _ := this
    · intro h; exact absurd h hp

theorem kapv_nonneg (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (Cm : ℝ) (h8 : adm8 D L X K Cm)
    (N : NumMean D L K Cm) (hI : RestHypI X K) (hρ : rho D K L.sc ≤ rhoB D K L.sc) (p : ℕ) (hc : Cand D L K p) (t0 : HH D)
    (ht0 : t0 ∈ goodReps X p) (a b : HH D → ℤ) : 0 ≤ kapv X K a b p t0 := by
  unfold kapv kapLoc
  split_ifs with hp
  · exact kapIIf_nonneg K hK L _
  · exact (kappa_nonneg_and_mean_bound X K Cm h8 N hI hρ p hc (not_lt.mp hp) t0 ht0).1 _ _

theorem Sig_nonneg (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (Cm : ℝ) (h8 : adm8 D L X K Cm)
    (N : NumMean D L K Cm) (hI : RestHypI X K) (hρ : rho D K L.sc ≤ rhoB D K L.sc) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) (hc : Cand D L K p) :
    0 ≤ Sig X K a b p := by
  unfold Sig
  exact mul_nonneg (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
    (Finset.sum_nonneg fun t0 ht0 => kapv_nonneg X K hK Cm h8 N hI hρ p hc t0 ht0 a b)



[LGS_compatibility_01] Finite ring transport, exact GatIs, all five GroupCompatibilityAll fields and eventual family
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/GroupLattices/StdSubAtOfRing.lean#L21-L89
Range SHA256: 4b0683a77f6955ca70f94dd5d9f64e9a6d0cdc3180571cb1e629d09f3574b30d
def ExtOf.map (X : ExtOf Os Ks O Kf) (eO : O ≃+* O') (eK : Kf ≃+* Kf')
    (hc : ∀ x, eK (algebraMap O Kf x) = algebraMap O' Kf' (eO x)) (hfrac : IsFractionRing O' Kf')
    (hcomp : IsAdicComplete (IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal O') O') : ExtOf Os Ks O' Kf' where
  φ := (eO : O →+* O').comp X.φ
  φK := (eK : Kf →+* Kf').comp X.φK
  comm := by
    intro x
    simp only [RingHom.comp_apply, RingEquiv.coe_toRingHom]
    rw [X.comm, hc]
  fin := (RingHom.Finite.of_surjective _ eO.surjective).comp X.fin
  frac := hfrac
  complete := hcomp

attribute [local instance] Formalism.BasicNotions.factResChar Formalism.BasicNotions.Host.cmStar

def RingCompatibleAt (D : D7) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) (v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV) : Prop :=
  ∃ (eW : WQ (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v) ≃+* (D.gat u w hg).P.W v)
    (eQ : QQ (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v) ≃+* (D.gat u w hg).P.Qq v)
    (eO : ORec D w ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v) ≃+* (D.gat u w hg).O)
    (eK : KfRec D w ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v) ≃+* (D.gat u w hg).Kf),
    (∀ x, eQ (algebraMap _ _ x) = algebraMap ((D.gat u w hg).P.W v) ((D.gat u w hg).P.Qq v) (eW x)) ∧
    (∀ x, eK (algebraMap _ _ x) = algebraMap (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf (eO x)) ∧
    (∀ y, (D.gat u w hg).P.ι v (eQ y) =
      eK ((extRec D w ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v)).φK (iota (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v) y))) ∧
    (∀ x, (D.gat u w hg).P.j v (eW x) =
      eO ((extRec D w ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v)).φ (jO (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v) x))) ∧
    eO ((extRec D w ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v)).φ (wU (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v))) = (D.gat u w hg).P.w

theorem stdSubAt_of_ring (D : D7) (h : ∀ u w hg v, RingCompatibleAt D u w hg v) : Joints.StdSubAt D := by
  intro u w hg v
  obtain ⟨eW, eQ, eO, eK, hWQ, hOK, hι, hj, hw⟩ := h u w hg v
  have hD := (D.gat u w hg).P.setting v
  exact stdSub_of_iso hD eW eQ hWQ
    ((extRec D w ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v)).map eO eK hOK hD.frac_O hD.complete_O) hι hj hw

def GatIs (D : D7) (H : LevelData D) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) : Prop :=
  ∃ (W Qq O Kf : Type) (_ : CommRing W) (_ : IsDomain W) (_ : IsDiscreteValuationRing W) (_ : Field Qq) (_ : Algebra W Qq)
    (_ : CommRing O) (_ : IsDomain O) (_ : IsDiscreteValuationRing O) (_ : Field Kf) (_ : Algebra O Kf)
    (n' : ℕ) (ι : Qq →+* Kf) (σ : Qq ≃+* Qq) (j : W →+* O) (w0 : O)
    (hS : Formalism.MainNotions.G.HidaSetting (D.what₂.places.resChar w) n' W Qq O Kf ι σ j w0) (nV r m : ℕ)
    (lam0 : Fin nV → Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) (dom : ∀ v, IsDominant (lam0 v)) (hk : m + 1 = D.R.k)
    (I : LocId (H u) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) nV W Qq) (ψ : Formalism.L2.Herm.awayFrom₂ (H u) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) →* Oˣ)
    (e : ℕ), Cmp.HypAtP I ∧ D.gat u w hg = gatOfLevelP (H u) hS nV r lam0 dom hk I ψ e

theorem gatE_of_gatIs (D : D7) (H : LevelData D) (hT : ∀ u, Cmp.TrivStabU (H u))
    (h : ∀ u w hg, GatIs D H u w hg) : TrivialStabiliserFiniteClasses D := by
  intro u w hg
  obtain ⟨W, Qq, O, Kf, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, n', ι, σ, j, w0, hS, nV, r, m, lam0, dom, hk, I, ψ, e, hH, heq⟩ :=
    h u w hg
  rw [heq]
  exact ⟨Cmp.trivStab hS r lam0 dom hk I ψ e (isUnit_det_gram0 D.sp) hH (hT u),
    Cmp.finClasses hS r lam0 dom hk I ψ e (isUnit_det_gram0 D.sp) hH⟩

structure GroupCompatibilityAll (D : D7) (Gt : GatTies D) (c : LatCmp D) where

  herm : Formalism.L2.Herm.Herm0 D

  forms : ∀ u : D.what₂.Word, Forms0 D u

  cmp : Cmp.CmpTied D herm forms Gt c

  ring : ∀ u w hg v, RingCompatibleAt D u w hg v

  gat : ∀ u w hg, GatIs D herm.lev0 u w hg

def GroupCompatibilityFamily {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  Ev Fm fun x i => ∀ hx : Fm.x1 < x, Nonempty (GroupCompatibilityAll (Fm.D x i) (Fm.gatT x i) (Fm.ex x hx i).cmp)


[LGS_compatibility_02] Actual standard-subring, finite-class/stabilizer and rational-descent consequences

theorem stdSubF_of_tied {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (h : GroupCompatibilityFamily Fm) : Ev Fm fun x i => Joints.StdSubAt (Fm.D x i) := by
  obtain ⟨x0, h0⟩ := h.and (Ev.x1 Fm)
  refine ⟨x0, fun x hx i => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨ht, hx1⟩ := h0 x hx i
  obtain ⟨G⟩ := ht hx1
  exact stdSubAt_of_ring _ G.ring

theorem gatEF_of_tied {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (h : GroupCompatibilityFamily Fm) : Ev Fm fun x i => TrivialStabiliserFiniteClasses (Fm.D x i) := by
  obtain ⟨x0, h0⟩ := h.and (Ev.x1 Fm)
  refine ⟨x0, fun x hx i => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨ht, hx1⟩ := h0 x hx i
  obtain ⟨G⟩ := ht hx1
  exact gatE_of_gatIs _ G.herm.lev0
    (fun u => Triv.trivStabU (G.herm.lev0 u) (Fm.D x i).host.g_prime (Fm.D x i).host.g_ne) G.gat

theorem npDescF_of_tied {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (h : GroupCompatibilityFamily Fm) : NPDescF Fm := by

    obtain ⟨G⟩ := h0 x hx i hx1
    exact Cmp.CmpTied.valRat G.cmp⟩

[LGS_compatibility_03] Actual rank, period, embeddings, positions and weights
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalRingCardinality/DataGatTies.lean#L7-L30
Range SHA256: 6cc5fe4cf5e38ff09b6c6cbc3dc52c872d5ad1e56499aa8eb2c7f1466fe7b1be
structure GatTies (D : D7) where

  gat_k : ∀ u w h, (D.gat u w h).P.m + 1 = D.R.k

  gat_n : ∀ u w h (v : Fin (D.gat u w h).P.nV) (P : Ideal (𝓞 D.host.E)) [P.IsPrime]
    [P.LiesOver (Ideal.span {((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : ℤ)})] (σ : D.host.E ≃ₐ[ℚ] D.host.E), IsArithFrobAt ℤ σ P →
    (D.gat u w h).P.n' v = orderOf (D.host.galE σ).2

  gat_pos : ∀ u w h, (Σ v : Fin (D.gat u w h).P.nV, Fin ((D.gat u w h).P.n' v)) ≃ D.what₂.Tau

  gat_step : ∀ u w h (v : Fin (D.gat u w h).P.nV) (s : Fin ((D.gat u w h).P.n' v))
    (P : Ideal (𝓞 D.host.E)) [P.IsPrime] [P.LiesOver (Ideal.span {((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : ℤ)})]
    (σ : D.host.E ≃ₐ[ℚ] D.host.E), IsArithFrobAt ℤ σ P →
    ((D.eTau (gat_pos u w h ⟨v, posAdd s 1⟩) : D.R.Psi) : D.R.GG) =
      ((D.eTau (gat_pos u w h ⟨v, s⟩) : D.R.Psi) : D.R.GG) *
        D.host.galE σ * D.R.cc

  gat_lam : ∀ u w h (v : Fin (D.gat u w h).P.nV) (s : Fin ((D.gat u w h).P.n' v))
    (i : Fin ((D.gat u w h).P.m + 1)),
    (D.gat u w h).P.lam0 v s i =
      ((D.fm u).lam 0 (gat_pos u w h ⟨v, s⟩)).w
        ⟨i.1, by have h1 := gat_k u w h; have h2 := i.2; simp only [Ranks.N]; omega⟩

end Formalism.BasicNotions


[LGS_compatibility_04] Actual comparison lattice, independent basis, Vram and served preimage
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalRingCardinality/DataLatDefs.lean#L11-L53
Range SHA256: ce7164ae78ffd6832eefa519cbfee801fca7e3701f28faf11c6c1e2e975a8934
structure LatCmp (D : D7) where

  embW : ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    D.what₂.LL →+* (D.gat u w hg).Kf

  embW_int : ∀ u w hg (x : D.what₂.LL), (0 : WithTop ℤ) ≤ D.what₂.places.val w x ↔
    embW u w hg x ∈ Set.range (algebraMap (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf)

  cmp : ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    ((D.fm u).lat 0).V →+ (D.gat u w hg).Fn
  cmp_smul : ∀ u w hg (a : D.what₂.LL) (x : ((D.fm u).lat 0).V),
    cmp u w hg (a • x) = embW u w hg a • cmp u w hg x

  cmp_li : ∀ u w hg, LinearIndependent (D.gat u w hg).Kf (fun a => cmp u w hg (((D.fm u).lat 0).b a))

  cmp_M : ∀ u w hg, Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).O (cmp u w hg '' (((D.fm u).lat 0).L : Set ((D.fm u).lat 0).V)) =
    (D.gat u w hg).M

def InLoc {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module (𝓞 K) V]
    (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K)) (A : Submodule (𝓞 K) V) (x : V) : Prop :=
  ∃ s : 𝓞 K, s ∉ w.asIdeal ∧ s • x ∈ A

namespace LatCmp

variable {D : D7} (c : LatCmp D)

def VramDef (Y : LatInst D) : Prop :=
  ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    (Y.Vram u w hg : Set (D.gat u w hg).Fn) =
      Submodule.span (D.gat u w hg).Kf (c.cmp u w hg '' ((D.fm u).ramSub : Set ((D.fm u).lat 0).V))

def NuServedDef {L : SizeData₂ D} (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ≠ ∅ →
    ∀ x : (D.div.Nu u).V, InLoc (D.plEq w) (L.NuLat u) x ↔ c.cmp u w hg (D.nuIn u x) ∈ NP X Y K u w hg

end LatCmp

[LGS_slot_and_joint_01] Signed lamAt/Ar and actual edge/midpoint coefficient definitions
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/UnramifiedQuadraticSetting.lean#L85-L144
Range SHA256: d07af73b5a967ab67c509c4b82a717a6df179d24f2c50ebd14894b23c03fdad9
    Submodule Kf (MvPolynomial (Fin n' × (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1))) Kf) :=
  tensorLat Kf Kf fun s => if Antitone (lamAt lam0 s) then dualWeyl Kf (m + 1) (m + 1) (nuOf (lamAt lam0 s)) else ⊥

def dimV (Kf : Type*) [Field Kf] {n' m : ℕ} (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) : ℕ :=
  Module.finrank Kf (Vsp Kf lam0)

def toV {O Kf : Type*} [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf] {n' m : ℕ} (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ)
    (M : Submodule O (MvPolynomial (Fin n' × (Fin (m + 1) × Fin (m + 1))) Kf)) : Submodule O (Vsp Kf lam0) :=
  M.comap ((Vsp Kf lam0).subtype.restrictScalars O)

def HL {W Qq O Kf : Type*} [CommRing W] [Field Qq] [Algebra W Qq] [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (ι : Qq →+* Kf) (σ : Qq ≃+* Qq) {n' m : ℕ} (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ)
    (L : Submodule W (Fin (m + 1) → Qq)) : Submodule O (Vsp Kf lam0) :=
  toV lam0 (Hten O lam0 fun s => extO O ι σ s.val L)

structure Edge (W Qq : Type*) [CommRing W] [Field Qq] [Algebra W Qq] (k : ℕ) where
  src : Submodule W (Fin k → Qq)
  tgt : Submodule W (Fin k → Qq)

def IsEdge {W Qq : Type*} [CommRing W] [Field Qq] [Algebra W Qq] (p n' r : ℕ) {k : ℕ} (e : Edge W Qq k) : Prop :=
  IsLattice W e.src ∧ IsLattice W e.tgt ∧ e.src ≤ e.tgt ∧ (∀ x ∈ e.tgt, (p : W) • x ∈ e.src) ∧
    Nat.card (↥e.tgt ⧸ Submodule.comap e.tgt.subtype e.src) = p ^ (n' * r)

def IsEdgeBasis {W Qq : Type*} [CommRing W] [Field Qq] [Algebra W Qq] (p r : ℕ) {k : ℕ} (e : Edge W Qq k)
    (g : Matrix (Fin k) (Fin k) Qq) : Prop :=
  IsUnit g.det ∧ e.src = colSpan W g ∧
    e.tgt = colSpan W (g * Matrix.diagonal fun c => if c.val < r then ((p : Qq))⁻¹ else 1)

def LeAt {W Qq O Kf : Type*} [CommRing W] [Field Qq] [Algebra W Qq] [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (p : ℕ) (ι : Qq →+* Kf) (σ : Qq ≃+* Qq) (w : O) (s : ℕ) {k : ℕ} (e : Edge W Qq k) :
    Submodule O (Fin k → Kf) :=
  extO O ι σ s e.src ⊔
    Submodule.span O ((fun v => (algebraMap O Kf w)⁻¹ • twistVec ι σ s ((p : W) • v)) '' (e.tgt : Set (Fin k → Qq)))

def HLe {W Qq O Kf : Type*} [CommRing W] [Field Qq] [Algebra W Qq] [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (p : ℕ) (ι : Qq →+* Kf) (σ : Qq ≃+* Qq) (w : O) {n' m : ℕ} (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ)
    (e : Edge W Qq (m + 1)) : Submodule O (Vsp Kf lam0) :=
  toV lam0 (Hten O lam0 fun s => LeAt p ι σ w s.val e)

def Ar {n' m : ℕ} (r : ℕ) (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) : ℤ :=
  ∑ s : Fin n', ∑ i : Fin (m + 1) with i.val < r, lamAt lam0 s i

def scale {O Kf X : Type*} [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf] [AddCommGroup X] [Module Kf X] [Module O X]
    [IsScalarTower O Kf X] (c : Kf) (M : Submodule O X) : Submodule O X :=
  M.map ((LinearMap.lsmul Kf X c).restrictScalars O)

def Re {W Qq O Kf : Type*} [CommRing W] [Field Qq] [Algebra W Qq] [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (p : ℕ) (ι : Qq →+* Kf) (σ : Qq ≃+* Qq) (w : O) {n' m : ℕ} (r : ℕ) (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ)
    (e : Edge W Qq (m + 1)) : Submodule O (Vsp Kf lam0) :=
  scale (algebraMap O Kf w ^ (-Ar r lam0)) (HLe p ι σ w lam0 e)

def Re' {W Qq O Kf : Type*} [CommRing W] [Field Qq] [Algebra W Qq] [CommRing O] [Field Kf] [Algebra O Kf]
    (p : ℕ) (ι : Qq →+* Kf) (σ : Qq ≃+* Qq) (w : O) {n' m : ℕ} (r : ℕ) (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ)
    (e : Edge W Qq (m + 1)) : Submodule O (Module.Dual Kf (Vsp Kf lam0)) :=
  scale (algebraMap O Kf w ^ (-Ar r lam0)) (dualLat O Kf (HLe p ι σ w lam0 e))

def be {Kf : Type*} [Field Kf] {n' m : ℕ} (p r : ℕ) (lam0 : Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ)
    (F : Vsp Kf lam0) (G : Module.Dual Kf (Vsp Kf lam0)) : Kf :=
  G (((p : Kf) ^ Ar r lam0) • F)



[LGS_slot_and_joint_02] Actual function lattices, ranks, neighbor operators and sandwich
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/DataG.lean#L143-L199
Range SHA256: 95806a6dc2b9de12fa951d3eb0b7886ff3069b0828a19c13fe267dc22080587e

def dna : ℕ := Module.finrank O C.Mna

def d : ℕ := Module.finrank O C.M

def yv (v : Fin P.nV) (F : P.Tup → P.Xsp) : P.Tup → P.Xsp :=
  fun L => ((p : Kf) ^ P.ArV v) •
    ∑ᶠ (L'' : Submodule (P.W v) (Fin (P.m + 1) → P.Qq v)) (_ : IsEdge p (P.n' v) P.r ⟨L'', L v⟩),
      F (Function.update L v L'')

def Sandwich (e : ℕ) : Prop :=
  C.Mna.map (((p : O) ^ e) • LinearMap.id) ≤ C.M ∧ C.M ≤ C.Mna

def TrivStab : Prop := ∀ (γ : C.Γ) (L : P.Tup), P.IsLatTup L → C.smul γ L = L → γ = 1

def FinClasses : Prop :=
  ∃ R : Finset P.Tup, (∀ L ∈ R, P.IsLatTup L) ∧ ∀ L, P.IsLatTup L → ∃ L₀ ∈ R, ∃ γ : C.Γ, L = C.smul γ L₀

end Level

structure GAtP (p : ℕ) where
  O : Type
  Kf : Type
  [ringO : CommRing O]
  [domO : IsDomain O]
  [dvrO : IsDiscreteValuationRing O]
  [fieldK : Field Kf]
  [algOK : Algebra O Kf]
  P : Places p O Kf
  comp : Type
  [finComp : Fintype comp]
  lev : comp → Level P
  e : ℕ

attribute [instance] GAtP.ringO GAtP.domO GAtP.dvrO GAtP.fieldK GAtP.algOK GAtP.finComp

namespace GAtP

variable {p : ℕ} (G : GAtP p)

abbrev Fn : Type := G.comp → G.P.Tup → G.P.Xsp

def funMod (Hv : G.P.Tup → Submodule G.O G.P.Xsp) : Submodule G.O G.Fn :=
  Submodule.pi Set.univ fun i => (G.lev i).funMod Hv

def Mna : Submodule G.O G.Fn := G.funMod G.P.Htup

def M : Submodule G.O G.Fn := G.funMod G.P.HtupW

def d : ℕ := Module.finrank G.O G.M

def dna : ℕ := Module.finrank G.O G.Mna

def yv (v : Fin G.P.nV) (F : G.Fn) : G.Fn := fun i => Level.yv (P := G.P) v (F i)

def Sandwich : Prop :=
  G.Mna.map (((p : G.O) ^ G.e) • LinearMap.id) ≤ G.M ∧ G.M ≤ G.Mna


[LGS_slot_and_joint_03] Original three slots, RestLocChain and RestNat
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/LatticeSystems.lean#L701-L724
Range SHA256: 5b8af8a226969b853654d0bb008fc34dd626a5c7affea5602dd4dbc83a07fbaf
def piLattice₂ (G : GAtP p) (v : Fin G.P.nV) (tv : ℕ) (s : Slot)
    (Lv : Submodule (G.P.W v) (Fin (G.P.m + 1) → G.P.Qq v)) :
    Submodule G.O (MvPolynomial (Fin (G.P.n' v) × (Fin (G.P.m + 1) × Fin (G.P.m + 1))) G.Kf) :=
  match s with
  | Slot.base => Hten G.O (G.P.lam0 v) fun s => extO G.O (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) s.val Lv
  | Slot.ball => ofV (Nsrc p (G.P.n' v) (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) G.P.w G.P.r (G.P.lam0 v) tv Lv)
  | Slot.dual => ofV (predual (Ntgt p (G.P.n' v) (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) G.P.w G.P.r (G.P.lam0 v) tv Lv))

def latSys (G : GAtP p) (t : Fin G.P.nV → ℕ) (σ : Fin G.P.nV → Slot) : Submodule G.O G.Fn :=
  G.funMod (G.P.sys fun v => piLattice₂ G v (t v) (σ v))

theorem latSys_base (G : GAtP p) (t : Fin G.P.nV → ℕ) : latSys G t (fun _ => Slot.base) = G.Mna := rfl

def RestLocChain (G : GAtP p) (v : Fin G.P.nV) (tv : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∀ Lv : Submodule (G.P.W v) (Fin (G.P.m + 1) → G.P.Qq v), IsLattice (G.P.W v) Lv →
    piLattice₂ G v tv Slot.base Lv ≤ piLattice₂ G v tv Slot.ball Lv ∧
    piLattice₂ G v tv Slot.dual Lv ≤ piLattice₂ G v tv Slot.base Lv ∧
    (∀ z ∈ piLattice₂ G v tv Slot.ball Lv, G.P.w ^ tv • z ∈ piLattice₂ G v tv Slot.base Lv) ∧
    (∀ z ∈ piLattice₂ G v tv Slot.base Lv, G.P.w ^ tv • z ∈ piLattice₂ G v tv Slot.dual Lv)

def RestNat (G : GAtP p) (VV : Finset (Fin G.P.nV)) (t : Fin G.P.nV → ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∀ σ, ∀ v ∈ VV, ∀ x ∈ latSys G t (Function.update σ v Slot.ball),
    G.yv v x ∈ latSys G t (Function.update σ v Slot.dual)



[LGS_slot_and_joint_04] Ordered product ideal, exact tau, symmetric tails and original local conclusion
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/SelectionLemmas.lean#L16-L76
Range SHA256: 576ad560ad9a6342805b5cb2286bb2f2e63ee47b5f374f3a8a42171eae2d47a1
def esymmOn {ι : Type} (VV : Finset ι) (κ : ι → ℝ) (k : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  ∑ B ∈ VV.powersetCard k, ∏ v ∈ B, κ v

def monoN (G : GAtP p) (VV B : Finset (Fin G.P.nV)) (m : ℕ) (Sv : Fin G.P.nV → ℕ) (x : G.Fn) : G.Fn :=
  ((p : G.O) ^ (∑ v ∈ VV \ B, Sv v)) •
    ((B.sort (· ≤ ·)).foldr (fun v f => (G.yv v)^[m] ∘ f) id) x

def prodIdeal (G : GAtP p) (VV : Finset (Fin G.P.nV)) (m : ℕ) (Sv : Fin G.P.nV → ℕ)
    (M : Submodule G.O G.Fn) : Submodule G.O G.Fn :=
  Submodule.span G.O (⋃ B ∈ VV.powerset, monoN G VV B m Sv '' (M : Set G.Fn))

def tau {ι : Type} (VV : Finset ι) (m D : ℕ) (t : ι → ℕ) : ℕ := ∑ v ∈ VV, m * (t v + D + 1)

def IsSaturatedIn {X : Type} [AddCommGroup X] [Module O X] (M' M : Submodule O X) : Prop :=
  M' ≤ M ∧ ∀ (a : O) (x : X), x ∈ M → a ≠ 0 → a • x ∈ M' → x ∈ M'

def adm1 (_D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) (G : GAtP p) : Prop := G.TrivStab

def adm2 (_D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) (G : GAtP p) : Prop := G.FinClasses

def adm3 (_D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) (G : GAtP p) : Prop :=
  ∀ (u v : Fin G.P.nV) (F : G.Fn), G.yv u (G.yv v F) = G.yv v (G.yv u F)

def admitted (D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) (G : GAtP p) : Prop := adm1 D G ∧ adm2 D G ∧ adm3 D G

def Conclusion1 (_D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) (G : GAtP p) : Prop :=
  ∀ (VV : Finset (Fin G.P.nV)) (t Sv : Fin G.P.nV → ℕ) (Dd cStar S m : ℕ) (κ : Fin G.P.nV → ℝ),
    1 ≤ m → (∀ v ∈ VV, Sv v ≤ S) →
    (∀ v ∈ VV, LPprime (W := G.P.W v) (Qq := G.P.Qq v) (O := G.O) (Kf := G.Kf) p (G.P.n' v) (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) G.P.w G.P.r
      (G.P.lam0 v) (t v) Dd (κ v) cStar) →
    let M := G.Mna
    let d : ℕ := G.dna
    let N := prodIdeal G VV m Sv M
    let τ : ℕ := tau VV m Dd t
    ((∀ β : ℕ, Formalism.G0.AllButLE (p : G.O) M N
        ⌊(d : ℝ) * (m : ℝ) ^ (β + 1) * esymmOn VV κ (β + 1)⌋₊ (τ + β * S)) ∧
      Formalism.G0.AllButLE (p : G.O) M N 0 (∑ v ∈ VV, Sv v)) ∧
    ((m : ℝ) * ∑ v ∈ VV, κ v ≤ 1 / 2 →
      let Sig : ℝ := (m : ℝ) * ∑ v ∈ VV, κ v
      let Nst := N ⊓ (Ideal.span {(p : G.O) ^ τ}) • M
      (∀ β : ℕ, (d : ℝ) * (m : ℝ) ^ (β + 1) * esymmOn VV κ (β + 1) ≤
          (d : ℝ) * Sig ^ (β + 1) / ((β + 1).factorial : ℝ)) ∧
      (∃ ℓ : ℕ, Module.length G.O (↥M ⧸ Submodule.comap M.subtype Nst) = ℓ ∧
        (ℓ : ℝ) ≤ ((Module.length G.O (G.O ⧸ Ideal.span {(p : G.O)})).toNat : ℝ) *
          ((d : ℝ) * τ + 2 * S * Sig * d)) ∧
      ∀ (M' : Submodule G.O G.Fn) (hM' : IsSaturatedIn M' M),
        Nst ⊓ M' ≤ (Ideal.span {(p : G.O) ^ τ}) • M' ∧
        Function.Injective (Submodule.mapQ (Submodule.comap M'.subtype Nst) (Submodule.comap M.subtype Nst)
          (Submodule.inclusion hM'.1) (fun _ hx => hx)) ∧
        ∀ (Y : Type) [AddCommGroup Y] [Module G.O Y] (K : G.Fn →ₗ[G.O] Y) (Z : Submodule G.O Y),
          N.map K ≤ Z → (Nst ⊓ M').map K ≤ Z)

def IntegralityFromMetric (D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) : Prop :=
  ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (h : Formalism.BasicNotions.GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    admitted D (D.gat u w h) → Conclusion1 D (D.gat u w h)

def RationalPlaneProblemLocal (D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) : Prop :=
  ∀ (p : ℕ) (G : GAtP p), admitted D G → Conclusion1 D G

def FamilyGlobalDerivation {eps : ℝ} (Fm : Formalism.MainNotions.FamP eps) : Prop := ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), IntegralityFromMetric (Fm.D x i)

def adm12 (_D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) {p : ℕ} (G : GAtP p) : Prop :=
  (∀ v, ∀ F ∈ G.Mna, G.yv v F ∈ G.Mna) ∧ ∀ v, ∀ F ∈ G.M, G.yv v F ∈ G.M

def adm13 (D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) {p : ℕ} (G : GAtP p) : Prop := Formalism.MainNotions.SelectionLemmas.adm2 D G

def Adm6 (D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) {p : ℕ} (G : GAtP p) : Prop :=
  adm9 D G ∧ adm10 D G ∧ adm12 D G ∧ adm13 D G ∧ Formalism.MainNotions.SelectionLemmas.adm3 D G

def Conclusion6 (_D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) {p : ℕ} (G : GAtP p) : Prop :=
  ∀ (VV : Finset (Fin G.P.nV)) (t Sv : Fin G.P.nV → ℕ) (Dd cStar S m : ℕ) (κ : Fin G.P.nV → ℝ)
    (V' : Submodule G.O G.Fn),
    1 ≤ m → (∀ v ∈ VV, Sv v ≤ S) →
    (∀ v ∈ VV, LPprime (W := G.P.W v) (Qq := G.P.Qq v) (O := G.O) (Kf := G.Kf) p (G.P.n' v) (G.P.ι v) (G.P.σ v) G.P.w
      G.P.r (G.P.lam0 v) (t v) Dd (κ v) cStar) →
    Formalism.MainNotions.SelectionLemmas.IsSaturatedIn (G.M ⊓ V') G.M →
    (m : ℝ) * ∑ v ∈ VV, κ v ≤ 1 / 2 →
    let tauP : ℕ := G.e + Formalism.MainNotions.SelectionLemmas.tau VV m Dd t
    let Mp := G.M ⊓ V'
    let NPp := Formalism.MainNotions.SelectionLemmas.prodIdeal G VV m Sv G.M ⊓ (Ideal.span {(p : G.O) ^ tauP}) • G.M ⊓ V'
    NPp ≤ (Ideal.span {(p : G.O) ^ tauP}) • Mp ∧
    ∃ ℓ : ℕ, Module.length G.O (↥Mp ⧸ Submodule.comap Mp.subtype NPp) = ℓ ∧
      (ℓ : ℝ) ≤ ((Module.length G.O (G.O ⧸ Ideal.span {(p : G.O)})).toNat : ℝ) *
        ((G.d : ℝ) * ((tauP : ℝ) + 2 * S * ((m : ℝ) * ∑ v ∈ VV, κ v)))

def ShareFromInputs (D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) : Prop :=
  ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (h : Formalism.BasicNotions.GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    0 < (D.gat u w h).P.nV → Adm6 D (D.gat u w h) → Conclusion6 D (D.gat u w h)

def LocalServiceStatement (D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) : Prop := ∀ (p : ℕ) (G : GAtP p), 0 < G.P.nV → Adm6 D G → Conclusion6 D G

def LemmaVVolumeGeneral (D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) (cStar : ℕ),
    let p := D.what₂.places.resChar w
    let G := D.gat u w hg
    let Mram := G.M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg
    let VVp := Formalism.MainNotions.G.VV X K (aL L) (bL L) p
    (G.Mna.map (((p : G.O) ^ X.ep p) • LinearMap.id) ≤ G.M ∧ G.M ≤ G.Mna) → 1 ≤ D.R.m → Set.InjOn (Y.vOf u w hg) (VVp : Set (HH D)) →
    (∀ t0 ∈ VVp, LPprime (W := G.P.W (Y.vOf u w hg t0)) (Qq := G.P.Qq (Y.vOf u w hg t0)) (O := G.O) (Kf := G.Kf) p
      (G.P.n' (Y.vOf u w hg t0)) (G.P.ι (Y.vOf u w hg t0)) (G.P.σ (Y.vOf u w hg t0)) G.P.w G.P.r
      (G.P.lam0 (Y.vOf u w hg t0)) ⌈tv X K (aL L) (bL L) p t0⌉₊ (Dp K L p) (kapv X K (aL L) (bL L) p t0) cStar) →
    Formalism.MainNotions.SelectionLemmas.IsSaturatedIn Mram G.M →
    (D.R.m : ℝ) * ∑ t0 ∈ VVp, kapv X K (aL L) (bL L) p t0 ≤ 1 / 2 →
    NP X Y K u w hg ≤ Mram.map (((p : G.O) ^ ⌈tauP X K (aL L) (bL L) p⌉.toNat) • LinearMap.id) ∧
    (∃ l : ℕ, Module.length G.O (↥Mram ⧸ (NP X Y K u w hg).comap Mram.subtype) = l ∧
      (l : ℝ) ≤ vUnit G * (G.d : ℝ) * (tauP X K (aL L) (bL L) p + ioP X K (aL L) (bL L) p))

[LGS_local_growth_01] Exact integer Sv, tauP and ioP definitions
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/IsResidueClassZero.lean#L330-L354
Range SHA256: 3f1baa7ade023fd44ebf691e3f2b48c2c3fa0033869ca88d9d4cc9534c0a5562
def MeanKappaBound (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ p, Cand D L K p → ∀ t0 ∈ goodReps X p,
    meanLoc D K L.sc (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0)
      (fun a b => kapLoc X K p (addOrderOf (hP D p)) (topsPos D p t0) a b) ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (-12 : ℝ) ∧
    ((p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k → ∀ a b, kapv X K a b p t0 ≤ (Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (-12 : ℝ))

def Sv (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) (t0 : HH D) : ℝ :=
  (⌈∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, max 0 (gU X K a b p j t0 - Pr X K b p j t0 - max (g0 X K a b p j t0) 0)⌉ : ℤ)

open Classical in

def tauP (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  if VV X K a b p = ∅ then 0 else
    (X.ep p : ℝ) + ∑ t0 ∈ VV X K a b p,
      (D.R.m : ℝ) * ((⌈tv X K a b p t0⌉₊ : ℝ) + (Dp K L p : ℝ) + 1)

open Classical in

def ioP (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (a b : HH D → ℤ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  if h : (VV X K a b p).Nonempty then
    2 * Sig X K a b p * (VV X K a b p).sup' h (fun t0 => Sv X K a b p t0)
  else 0

open Classical in



[LGS_local_growth_02] Complete LatInst/LatTied/yProd/NP and LatticeLengthsAtPlace definitions
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/IsResidueClassZero.lean#L410-L483
Range SHA256: d558290e24d6ef76dfd3edc849a0226bb6b3a8baee7ea8583c05ec4de45a07f7
  La K L.sc = L.LaG

def vUnit {p : ℕ} (G : GAtP p) : ℝ := ((IsDiscreteValuationRing.addVal G.O (p : G.O)).toNat : ℝ)

def vGeLat {p : ℕ} (G : GAtP p) {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module G.Kf V] [Module G.O V]
    [IsScalarTower G.O G.Kf V] (N : Submodule G.O V) (F : V) (g : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∃ ϖ : G.O, Irreducible ϖ ∧ F ∈ scale ((algebraMap G.O G.Kf ϖ) ^ ⌈g * vUnit G⌉) N

structure LatInst (D : D7) where
  vOf : ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)), HH D → Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV
  Fl : ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)), Fills (D.gat u w hg)
  Lv : ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)), Levels (Fl u w hg)
  Ch : ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)), Chain (Lv u w hg)
  Vram : ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    Submodule (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Fn

def LatInst.vKge (Y : LatInst D) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) (F : (D.gat u w hg).Fn) (g : ℝ) : Prop :=
  vGeLat (D.gat u w hg) ((Y.Lv u w hg).Int D.R.m) ((Y.Ch u w hg).chainK D.R.m F) g

def LatTied (hT : GatTies D) (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) : Prop :=
  ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    let p := D.what₂.places.resChar w
    (Y.Fl u w hg).mm = D.R.m ∧ (Y.Fl u w hg).t = D.R.t ∧
    ((Y.Fl u w hg).c : ℝ) ≤ cG L ∧
    (D.gat u w hg).P.r = (if (p : ℝ) < L.sc.p0 D.R.k then rG L.sc else 1) ∧
    Set.InjOn (Y.vOf u w hg) (reps X p) ∧
    (∀ (v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV) (s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v)) (t : HH D),
      ((D.eTau (hT.gat_pos u w hg ⟨v, s⟩) : D.R.Psi) : D.R.GG) = D.R.tau2 t →
        s ∉ (Y.Fl u w hg).TT v ∧
        ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ((Y.Fl u w hg).w v j s : ℝ) = fillG X (bL L) j (true, t)) ∧
    ∀ t0 ∈ reps X p,
      (D.gat u w hg).P.n' (Y.vOf u w hg t0) = addOrderOf (hP D p) ∧
      ∀ s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' (Y.vOf u w hg t0)),
        ((D.eTau (hT.gat_pos u w hg ⟨Y.vOf u w hg t0, s⟩) : D.R.Psi) : D.R.GG) =
          D.R.tau1 (t0 + s.val • hP D p) ∧
        (s ∈ (Y.Fl u w hg).TT (Y.vOf u w hg t0) ↔ t0 + s.val • hP D p ∈ D.host.TT) ∧
        (s ∈ (Y.Fl u w hg).TT (Y.vOf u w hg t0) →
          (Y.Fl u w hg).a (Y.vOf u w hg t0) s = aL L (t0 + s.val • hP D p) ∧
          (Y.Fl u w hg).b (Y.vOf u w hg t0) s = bL L (t0 + s.val • hP D p)) ∧
        ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D,
          ((Y.Fl u w hg).w (Y.vOf u w hg t0) j s : ℝ) = fillG X (bL L) j (false, t0 + s.val • hP D p)

def yProd (Y : LatInst D) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w))
    (VV' : Finset (HH D)) (F : (D.gat u w hg).Fn) : (D.gat u w hg).Fn :=
  (VV'.toList.map (fun t0 => ((D.gat u w hg).yv (Y.vOf u w hg t0))^[D.R.m])).foldr
    (fun f g => f ∘ g) id F

def lowFE (L : SizeData₂ D) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) : ℝ :=
  ∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ((L.F j w : ℝ) - (L.E u j w : ℝ))

open Classical in

def NP (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) :
    Submodule (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Fn :=
  let p := D.what₂.places.resChar w
  let G := D.gat u w hg
  let VVp := VV X K (aL L) (bL L) p
  Submodule.span G.O {F' | ∃ VV' ⊆ VVp, ∃ F ∈ G.M,
      F' = yProd Y u w hg VV' (((p : G.O) ^ (∑ t0 ∈ VVp \ VV', ⌈Sv X K (aL L) (bL L) p t0⌉.toNat)) • F)} ⊓
    G.M.map (((p : G.O) ^ ⌈tauP X K (aL L) (bL L) p⌉.toNat) • LinearMap.id) ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg

def LatticeLengthsAtPlace (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) : Prop :=
  (w ∈ L.supp → ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    ∃ l : ℕ, Module.length (D.gat u w hg).O
        (↥((D.gat u w hg).M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg) ⧸
          (NP X Y K u w hg).comap ((D.gat u w hg).M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg).subtype) = l ∧
      (l : ℝ) = (L.lenMN u w : ℝ) * vUnit (D.gat u w hg)) ∧
  ∀ u : D.what₂.Word, VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) = ∅ →
    D.what₂.places.resChar w ≠ (D.mem u).A.T → L.lenMN u w = 0

end Formalism.MainNotions.G

def admG1 (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  ∀ hT : GatTies D, LatTied hT X Y → ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    let p := D.what₂.places.resChar w
    let G := D.gat u w hg
    let Mram := G.M ⊓ Y.Vram u w hg
    NP X Y K u w hg ≤ Mram.map (((p : G.O) ^ ⌈tauP X K (aL L) (bL L) p⌉.toNat) • LinearMap.id) ∧
    (∃ l : ℕ, Module.length G.O (↥Mram ⧸ (NP X Y K u w hg).comap Mram.subtype) = l ∧
      (l : ℝ) ≤ vUnit G * (G.d : ℝ) * (tauP X K (aL L) (bL L) p + ioP X K (aL L) (bL L) p)) ∧
    ∀ F ∈ NP X Y K u w hg,
      Y.vKge u w hg F ((∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, gainP X K (aL L) (bL L) p j) + lowFE L u w)

def TypePrimeSizeConds (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (K : Consts) : Prop :=
  Formalism.MainNotions.SizesAt L ∧ 4 * L.Sc ≤ La K L.sc * L.sc.b' / (D.R.k : ℝ) ∧ Nm D + 1 ≤ L.sc.w'' D.R.n

structure AConsts where
  C31 : ℝ
  Ce : ℝ
  Cm : ℝ
  δ : ℝ

def admitted (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (A : AConsts) : Prop :=
  adm1 D L X A.C31 ∧ adm2 D L X ∧ adm3 D L ∧ ArchimedeanSignatureMatching D L K ∧ adm5 D L K A.δ ∧ adm6 D L K ∧
  adm7 D L X K ∧ adm8 D L X K A.Cm ∧ adm9 D L X A.Ce ∧ unitBoundAtTwistedPlaces D L X Y K ∧ admG1 D L X Y K ∧
  TypePrimeSizeConds D L K

def Conclusion₂ (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (C bo : ℝ) : Prop :=
  Conclusion1 D L X K C bo ∧ Conclusion2 D L X K ∧ PointsLemma D L X K ∧ WeightVectorExists D L X K ∧ Conclusion0 D L X ∧ WeightedPrimeSumBound D L X K C

theorem eventually_large_scale_bound {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Ev Fm fun x i => CornerGLContinuity.LargeScaleSmallness (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) := by
  obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := Ev.x1 Fm
  refine ⟨max x0 ((2 : ℝ) ^ (1 / eps)), fun x hx i => ?_⟩
  have hx1 := h x (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) hx) i
  have he : 0 < eps := by rw [← Fm.L_eps x i]; exact (Fm.L x i).sc.eps_pos
  refine ⟨le_trans one_le_two (Fm.D x i).R.two_le_m, ?_⟩
  rw [Fm.L_x x i hx1, Fm.L_eps x i]
  have h3 : (2 : ℝ) ^ (1 / eps) ≤ x := le_trans (le_max_right _ _) hx
  have hx0 : 0 < x := lt_of_lt_of_le (by positivity) h3
  have h4 : ((2 : ℝ) ^ (1 / eps)) ^ eps ≤ x ^ eps := Real.rpow_le_rpow (by positivity) h3 he.le
  rw [← Real.rpow_mul (by norm_num)] at h4
  have h5 : 1 / eps * eps = 1 := by field_simp
  rw [h5, Real.rpow_one] at h4
  rw [Real.rpow_neg hx0.le, one_div]
  exact inv_anti₀ (by norm_num) h4

https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/DataGChain.lean#L30-L90

  mm : ℕ

namespace Fills

variable {p : ℕ} {G : GAtP p} (Fl : Fills G)

def Pj : ℕ → Places p G.O G.Kf
  | 0 => G.P
  | j + 1 => G.P.withWt (G.P.m + (j + 1) * (Fl.t + 1))
      (fun v s => liftWt Fl.t G.P.m (G.P.lam0 v s) (fun i => Fl.w v i s) (j + 1)) (Fl.domJ (j + 1))

end Fills

structure Levels {p : ℕ} {G : GAtP p} (Fl : Fills G) where
  compS : ℕ → Type
  [finS : ∀ j, Fintype (compS j)]
  levS : ∀ j, compS j → Level (Fl.Pj (j + 1))

attribute [instance] Levels.finS

namespace Levels

variable {p : ℕ} {G : GAtP p} {Fl : Fills G} (Lv : Levels Fl)

def comp : ℕ → Type
  | 0 => G.comp
  | j + 1 => Lv.compS j

def lev : (j : ℕ) → Lv.comp j → Level (Fl.Pj j)
  | 0 => G.lev
  | j + 1 => Lv.levS j

abbrev Fm (j : ℕ) : Type := Lv.comp j → (Fl.Pj j).Tup → (Fl.Pj j).Xsp

def Int (j : ℕ) : Submodule G.O (Lv.Fm j) := Submodule.pi Set.univ fun i => (Lv.lev j i).M

def Tm (j : ℕ) (v : Fin (Fl.Pj j).nV) (i : ℕ) (F : Lv.Fm j) : Lv.Fm j := fun c => (Fl.Pj j).Tminus v i (F c)

end Levels

structure Chain {p : ℕ} {G : GAtP p} {Fl : Fills G} (Lv : Levels Fl) where
  Kz : ∀ j, Lv.Fm j →ₗ[G.Kf] Lv.Fm (j + 1)

namespace Chain

variable {p : ℕ} {G : GAtP p} {Fl : Fills G} {Lv : Levels Fl} (Ch : Chain Lv)

def chainK : (j : ℕ) → Lv.Fm 0 →ₗ[G.Kf] Lv.Fm j
  | 0 => LinearMap.id
  | j + 1 => (Ch.Kz j).comp (chainK j)

end Chain

def zeroCh (Y : LatInst D) : LatInst D where
  vOf := Y.vOf
  Fl := Y.Fl
  Lv := Y.Lv
  Ch := fun _ _ _ => ⟨fun _ => 0⟩
  Vram := Y.Vram

theorem chainK_zero {p : ℕ} {G : GAtP p} {Fl : Fills G} (Lv : Levels Fl) :
    ∀ m : ℕ, 1 ≤ m → ∀ F : Lv.Fm 0, (⟨fun _ => 0⟩ : Chain Lv).chainK m F = 0
  | 0, h, _ => absurd h (by omega)
  | n + 1, _, F => by simp [Chain.chainK]

theorem vKge_zero (Y : LatInst D) (hm : 1 ≤ D.R.m) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) (F : (D.gat u w hg).Fn) (g : ℝ) :
    (zeroCh Y).vKge u w hg F g := by
  have h0 : ((zeroCh Y).Ch u w hg).chainK D.R.m F = 0 := chainK_zero _ D.R.m hm F
  unfold LatInst.vKge vGeLat
  obtain ⟨ϖ, hϖ⟩ := IsDiscreteValuationRing.exists_irreducible (D.gat u w hg).O
  refine ⟨ϖ, hϖ, ?_⟩
  rw [h0]
  exact Submodule.zero_mem _

theorem admissible_family_no_kernel {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps)
    (h1 : Adm1F Fm) (h2 : Adm2F Fm) (h3 : Adm3F Fm) (h4 : Adm4F Fm) (h5 : Adm5F Fm) (h6 : Adm6F Fm) (h7 : Adm7F Fm)

    (hE : Ev Fm fun x i => TrivialStabiliserFiniteClasses (Fm.D x i)) (hY : Ev Fm fun x i => OperatorsPreserveLattices (Fm.D x i))
    (hLP : Ev Fm fun x i => CornerGLContinuity.OrbitLatticeBounds (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) (Fm.Gy x i) Fm.GK)
    (hEp : Ev Fm fun x i => CornerGLContinuity.LatticeSandwich (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i))
    (hd : NPDescF Fm) : AuxBlock8.AdmF Fm Fm.GK Fm.Gx (fun x i => zeroCh (Fm.Gy x i)) ∧
    Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.unitBoundAtTwistedPlaces (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) (zeroCh (Fm.Gy x i)) Fm.GK ∧
      AuxBlock8.admG1 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) (zeroCh (Fm.Gy x i)) Fm.GK := by
  have hG1 : Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.unitBoundAtTwistedPlaces (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) (zeroCh (Fm.Gy x i)) Fm.GK ∧
      AuxBlock8.admG1 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) (zeroCh (Fm.Gy x i)) Fm.GK := by
    obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := ((((hE.and hY).and hLP).and hEp).and (eventually_large_scale_bound Fm)).and (Ev.x1 Fm)
    refine ⟨x0, fun x hx i => ?_⟩
    obtain ⟨⟨⟨⟨⟨hE, hY⟩, hLP⟩, hEp⟩, hB⟩, hx1⟩ := h x hx i
    have hU : AuxBlock8.unitBoundAtTwistedPlaces (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) (zeroCh (Fm.Gy x i)) Fm.GK := by
      intro _ _ u w hg _ _ _ _ _ _ _
      exact vKge_zero _ hB.1 u w hg _ _
    have hS1 : CornerGLContinuity.ServiceLowerValuation (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) (zeroCh (Fm.Gy x i)) Fm.GK := by
      intro _ _ u w hg _ _
      exact vKge_zero _ hB.1 u w hg _ _
    exact ⟨hU, TopChoiceBounds.gluing_statement_of_proof _ _ _ (TopChoiceBounds.gluing_statement_holds Fm.GK (Fm.Gx x i) (zeroCh (Fm.Gy x i))
      ⟨hU, stability_of_trivial_stabilisers hE, service_statement_of_stability _ _ _ (FormalGroupSystem.Pf.stmt6G _ _ _ _ _) hE hY, hLP, hEp,
        saturated_ramified_lattice _ (Fm.ex x hx1 i).cmp (Fm.ex x hx1 i).lat.vram, hB, hS1⟩).1⟩
  have hA : AuxBlock8.AdmF Fm Fm.GK Fm.Gx (fun x i => zeroCh (Fm.Gy x i)) := by
    obtain ⟨C31, hC31, h1⟩ := h1
    obtain ⟨Cm, hCm, h8⟩ := h8

    obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := ((((((((((h1.and h2).and h3).and h4).and (h5 δ hδ)).and h6).and h7).and h8).and h9).and
      hG1).and (eventually_sizes_admitted Fm))

    obtain ⟨⟨⟨⟨⟨⟨⟨⟨⟨⟨a1, a2⟩, a3⟩, a4⟩, a5⟩, a6⟩, a7⟩, a8⟩, a9⟩, aU, aG⟩, aS⟩ :=
      h x (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) hx) i
    exact ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6, a7, a8, a9, aU, aG, aS⟩
  exact ⟨hA, hG1⟩

def NuDef (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) : Prop :=
  (∀ u w, D.what₂.places.resChar w ≠ (D.mem u).A.T → LocLe (D.plEq w) 0 (L.NuLat u) (MramL D u)) ∧
  (∀ u w, D.what₂.places.resChar w ≠ (D.mem u).A.T →
    (D.what₂.places.resChar w ∈ L.drop ∨ ¬ InertK D (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) →
      LocEq (D.plEq w) (L.NuLat u) (MramL D u)) ∧
  (∀ u w, ∀ l : ℕ, lenLoc (D.plEq w) (L.NuLat u) (MramL D u) = l →
    (l : ℝ) = (L.lenMN u w : ℝ) * (D.what₂.places.ramIdx w : ℝ))

[LGS_admission_01] TypePrimeSizeConds, complete admitted and exact AuxBlock8.AdmF quantifiers
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/SmallOrderPrimes.lean#L238-L269
Range SHA256: 5816a7244fb97b1101ff1ee1eef4994891a7ae4ed9d8c6dcb0911a23165db5bc
  Cm : ℝ
  δ : ℝ

def admitted (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (A : AConsts) : Prop :=
  adm1 D L X A.C31 ∧ adm2 D L X ∧ adm3 D L ∧ ArchimedeanSignatureMatching D L K ∧ adm5 D L K A.δ ∧ adm6 D L K ∧
  adm7 D L X K ∧ adm8 D L X K A.Cm ∧ adm9 D L X A.Ce ∧ unitBoundAtTwistedPlaces D L X Y K ∧ admG1 D L X Y K ∧
  TypePrimeSizeConds D L K

def Conclusion₂ (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (X : Inst D L) (K : Consts) (C bo : ℝ) : Prop :=
  Conclusion1 D L X K C bo ∧ Conclusion2 D L X K ∧ PointsLemma D L X K ∧ WeightVectorExists D L X K ∧ Conclusion0 D L X ∧ WeightedPrimeSumBound D L X K C

def Stmt (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) (K : Consts) (A : AConsts) (C bo : ℝ) : Prop :=
  K.Nonneg → ∀ (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D), admitted D L X Y K A → Conclusion₂ D L X K C bo

def ClosedFormOfAdmitted (D : D7) (L : SizeData₂ D) : Prop :=
  ∀ K : Consts, K.Nonneg → ∀ A : AConsts, ∃ C bo : ℝ, 0 ≤ C ∧ 0 ≤ bo ∧
    ∀ (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D), admitted D L X Y K A → Conclusion₂ D L X K C bo

def AdmF {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (K : Consts) (X : ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), Inst (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i))
    (Y : ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), LatInst (Fm.D x i)) : Prop :=
  ∃ (C31 Ce Cm : ℝ), 0 < C31 ∧ 0 ≤ Ce ∧ 0 ≤ Cm ∧
    ∀ δ > 0, ∃ x0 : ℝ, 1 < x0 ∧ ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x,
      admitted (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (X x i) (Y x i) K ⟨C31, Ce, Cm, δ⟩

def ConclusionOnFamily {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (K : Consts) (X : ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), Inst (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i)) : Prop :=
  ∃ (C : ℝ) (δ : ℝ → ℝ), Filter.Tendsto δ Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧ ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x,
    Conclusion₂ (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (X x i) K C ((Fm.L x i).sc.b ^ δ x)

def KudlaFamilyStatement {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  ∀ (K : Consts), K.Nonneg → ∀ (X : ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), Inst (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i))
    (Y : ∀ x (i : Fm.Ix x), LatInst (Fm.D x i)), AdmF Fm K X Y → ConclusionOnFamily Fm K X

theorem eventually_sizes_admitted {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) :
    Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.TypePrimeSizeConds (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) Fm.GK := by

  refine ⟨x0, fun x hx i => ?_⟩

  obtain ⟨w⟩ := exists_place (Fm.D x i)

  have P := Fm.params x hx1 i

  have hkS := P.kS_le
  have hkSc : 5 * (Fm.L x i).Sc ≤ ((Fm.D x i).R.k : ℝ) * (Fm.L x i).Sc := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hk (by linarith)
  refine ⟨Fm.sizes x hx1 i, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [hLa]
    exact P.S_le
  · unfold Nm
    linarith

  Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.adm2 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i)

def Adm3F {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop := Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.adm3 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i)

def Adm4F {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop := Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.ArchimedeanSignatureMatching (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) Fm.GK

def Adm5F {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  ∀ δ > 0, Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.adm5 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) Fm.GK δ

def Adm6F {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop := Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.adm6 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) Fm.GK

def Adm7F {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.adm7 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) Fm.GK

def Adm8F {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  ∃ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C ∧ Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.adm8 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) Fm.GK C

def Adm9F {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  ∃ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C ∧ Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.adm9 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) C

def AdmUF {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.unitBoundAtTwistedPlaces (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) (Fm.Gy x i) Fm.GK

theorem family_admitted_of_parts {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (h1 : Adm1F Fm) (h2 : Adm2F Fm) (h3 : Adm3F Fm) (h4 : Adm4F Fm)
    (h5 : Adm5F Fm) (h6 : Adm6F Fm) (h7 : Adm7F Fm) (h8 : Adm8F Fm) (h9 : Adm9F Fm) (hU : AdmUF Fm)
    (hG1 : FamilyFirstGrowthBound Fm) : AuxBlock8.AdmF Fm Fm.GK Fm.Gx Fm.Gy := by
  obtain ⟨C31, hC31, h1⟩ := h1
  obtain ⟨Cm, hCm, h8⟩ := h8
  obtain ⟨Ce, hCe, h9⟩ := h9
  refine ⟨C31, Ce, Cm, hC31, hCe, hCm, fun δ hδ => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := (((((((((((h1.and h2).and h3).and h4).and (h5 δ hδ)).and h6).and h7).and h8).and h9).and
    hU).and hG1).and (eventually_sizes_admitted Fm))
  refine ⟨max x0 2, lt_of_lt_of_le one_lt_two (le_max_right _ _), fun x hx i => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨⟨⟨⟨⟨⟨⟨⟨⟨⟨⟨a1, a2⟩, a3⟩, a4⟩, a5⟩, a6⟩, a7⟩, a8⟩, a9⟩, aU⟩, aG⟩, aS⟩ :=
    h x (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) hx) i
  exact ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6, a7, a8, a9, aU, aG, aS⟩
