[LGS_admission_04] Actual selected tops and bad-top family predicates
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GlobalSchurBounds/Chosen.lean#L15-L70
Range SHA256: 494b55f806b558c7fec84af897813cf4f72cd5935b9932ffa93284a1edc61a43

def ThickSetLowerBound (_L : SizeData₂ D) : Prop := D.host.secondSet_card_lower_bound

def ChTT_at : Prop :=
  L.sc.delta1 * D.R.n / 2 ≤ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ∧ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ≤ 2 * L.sc.delta1 * D.R.n

open Classical in

def largeOrderResiduePrimes : Finset ℕ :=
  (Finset.range ⌈L.sc.X'⌉₊).filter (fun p =>
    p.Prime ∧ G.IsResidueClassZero D p ∧ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) ≤ (addOrderOf (G.hP D p) : ℝ))

variable {L}

open Classical in

def badTops (X : G.Inst D L) (p : ℕ) : Finset (G.HH D) :=
  D.host.TT.filter (fun t => ¬ G.Good X p (X.bs (G.stepP D p) t))

def ChBad_at (X : G.Inst D L) (Cb : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∑ p ∈ largeOrderResiduePrimes L, Real.log p / p * ((badTops X p).card : ℝ) ≤
    4 * (Cb * L.sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ)) * L.sc.delta1 * D.R.n * ∑ p ∈ largeOrderResiduePrimes L, Real.log p / p

def BoundsAtX (x eps C : ℝ) (f : ℕ → ℂ) (M : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N), GlobalTheoryTheory.Induces χ f M →
    ‖GlobalTheoryTheory.gChi χ x‖ ≤ x ^ ((1 : ℝ) / 2 + eps) ∧ ‖GlobalTheoryTheory.logDerivativeAtOne χ‖ ≤ C * Real.log x

def ChQD_at (D : D7) (x eps C : ℝ) : Prop :=
  BoundsAtX x eps C (GlobalTheoryTheory.psDir D.host.q) (8 * D.host.q * D.host.d) ∧
  BoundsAtX x eps C (GlobalTheoryTheory.chDir D.host.d) (8 * D.host.q * D.host.d) ∧
  BoundsAtX x eps C (fun a => GlobalTheoryTheory.chDir D.host.d a * GlobalTheoryTheory.psDir D.host.q a) (8 * D.host.q * D.host.d)

def ChThin_at (D : D7) (x eps c : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N), GlobalTheoryTheory.IsCharED D χ →
    ∀ y : ℝ, Real.exp (x ^ (eps / 3)) / 3 ≤ y → ‖GlobalTheoryTheory.gChi χ y‖ ≤ y * Real.exp (-(c * x ^ (eps / 8)))

variable (L)

def ChProviso_at (C0 : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∀ u, C0⁻¹ ≤ (D.duS u : ℝ) * GamL L ^ 2 / D.what₂.Dstar ∧ (D.duS u : ℝ) * GamL L ^ 2 / D.what₂.Dstar ≤ C0

def ChCode_at (C0 CH : ℝ) : Prop :=
  C0 * GamL L ^ 2 * L.sc.x ^ CH ≤ (Fintype.card D.what₂.Word : ℝ)

end AtRecord

section Family

variable {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps)

def ThickSetEventually : Prop := ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, ThickSetLowerBound (Fm.L x i)

def ChosenTT : Prop := ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, ChTT_at (Fm.L x i)

def ChosenBad (Cb : ℝ) : Prop := ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, ChBad_at (Fm.Gx x i) Cb



[LGS_admission_05] Full TopsF to Adm2F consumer on the same selected subset
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/SmoothDesignExistence/AdmissibleTopsMertens.lean#L15-L146
Range SHA256: 32d4c794a3f9b5d575c5d5eb1051f6a9142ad56517db326a11d3a436ab3935a1
def TopsF {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop := ChosenTT Fm ∧ ∃ Cb : ℝ, 0 < Cb ∧ ChosenBad Fm Cb

theorem topsF_of_chosenE {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) {C0 CH : ℝ} (h : ChosenE Fm C0 CH) : TopsF Fm := by
  obtain ⟨C5, c6, Cb, -, -, hCb, -, hTT, hBad, -⟩ := h
  exact ⟨hTT, Cb, hCb, hBad⟩

private lemma sum_log_div_le_two_mul_mul_add_two (s : Finset ℕ) (T : ℝ) (hT : 0 ≤ T)
    (hs : ∀ p ∈ s, 1 ≤ p ∧ p < ⌈Real.exp T⌉₊) :
    ∑ p ∈ s, Real.log p / p ≤ 2 * T * (T + 2) := by
  have hterm : ∀ p ∈ s, Real.log p / p ≤ T * (2 / ((p : ℝ) + 1)) := by
    intro p hp
    obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := hs p hp
    have hp1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ p := by exact_mod_cast h1
    have hp0 : (0 : ℝ) < p := by linarith
    have hpe : (p : ℝ) < Real.exp T := Nat.lt_ceil.1 h2
    have hlog : Real.log p ≤ T := by
      have h := Real.log_le_log hp0 hpe.le
      rwa [Real.log_exp] at h
    have e1 : Real.log p / p ≤ T / p := by gcongr
    have h3 : 1 / (p : ℝ) ≤ 2 / ((p : ℝ) + 1) := by
      first
      | (rw [div_le_div_iff₀ hp0 (by linarith)]; linarith)
      | (rw [div_le_div_iff hp0 (by linarith)]; linarith)
    have e2 : T / p ≤ T * (2 / ((p : ℝ) + 1)) := by
      calc T / p = T * (1 / p) := by ring
        _ ≤ T * (2 / ((p : ℝ) + 1)) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h3 hT
    linarith
  have hsub : s ⊆ Finset.range ⌈Real.exp T⌉₊ := fun p hp => Finset.mem_range.2 (hs p hp).2
  have hh : ∑ p ∈ Finset.range ⌈Real.exp T⌉₊, ((p : ℝ) + 1)⁻¹ = (harmonic ⌈Real.exp T⌉₊ : ℝ) := by
    first
    | simp [harmonic]
    | (rw [harmonic]; push_cast; rfl)
  have hlN : Real.log (⌈Real.exp T⌉₊ : ℝ) ≤ T + 1 := by
    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos ⌈Real.exp T⌉₊ with h0 | h0
    · rw [h0]
      simp only [Nat.cast_zero, Real.log_zero]
      linarith
    · have hN0 : (0 : ℝ) < (⌈Real.exp T⌉₊ : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast h0
      have hE : (1 : ℝ) ≤ Real.exp T := Real.one_le_exp hT
      have hc := Nat.ceil_lt_add_one (Real.exp_pos T).le
      have hNle : (⌈Real.exp T⌉₊ : ℝ) ≤ 2 * Real.exp T := by linarith
      have h2 := Real.log_le_log hN0 hNle
      rw [Real.log_mul (by norm_num) (Real.exp_pos T).ne', Real.log_exp] at h2
      have hl2 : Real.log 2 ≤ 1 := by
        have h := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos (show (0 : ℝ) < 2 by norm_num)
        linarith
      linarith
  calc ∑ p ∈ s, Real.log p / p ≤ ∑ p ∈ s, T * (2 / ((p : ℝ) + 1)) := Finset.sum_le_sum hterm
    _ ≤ ∑ p ∈ Finset.range ⌈Real.exp T⌉₊, T * (2 / ((p : ℝ) + 1)) :=
        Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset_of_nonneg hsub (fun p _ _ => by positivity)
    _ = 2 * T * ∑ p ∈ Finset.range ⌈Real.exp T⌉₊, ((p : ℝ) + 1)⁻¹ := by
        rw [Finset.mul_sum]
        exact Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun p _ => by ring)
    _ ≤ 2 * T * (1 + Real.log (⌈Real.exp T⌉₊ : ℝ)) := by
        rw [hh]
        exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (harmonic_le_one_add_log _) (by positivity)
    _ ≤ 2 * T * (T + 2) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) (by positivity)

theorem admissible_tops_mertens {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (h : TopsF Fm) : G.Adm2F Fm := by
  obtain ⟨⟨xa, hTT⟩, Cb, hCb, xb, hBad⟩ := h
  unfold G.Adm2F G.Ev
  refine ⟨max (max xa xb) (max (Fm.x1 + 1) ((16 * Cb + 2) ^ (3 / eps))), fun x hx i => ?_⟩
  have hxa : xa ≤ x := le_trans (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) (le_max_left _ _)) hx
  have hxb : xb ≤ x := le_trans (le_trans (le_max_right _ _) (le_max_left _ _)) hx
  have hx1' : Fm.x1 + 1 ≤ x := le_trans (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) (le_max_right _ _)) hx
  have hx1 : Fm.x1 < x := by linarith
  have hxc : (16 * Cb + 2) ^ (3 / eps) ≤ x := le_trans (le_trans (le_max_right _ _) (le_max_right _ _)) hx
  obtain ⟨hT1, hT2⟩ := hTT x hxa i
  have hB := hBad x hxb i
  unfold ChBad_at at hB
  have hLx : (Fm.L x i).sc.x = x := Fm.L_x x i hx1
  have hLe : (Fm.L x i).sc.eps = eps := Fm.L_eps x i
  have he' : 0 < (Fm.L x i).sc.eps := (Fm.L x i).sc.eps_pos
  have he : 0 < eps := by rw [← hLe]; exact he'
  have hy1 : 1 < (Fm.L x i).sc.x := (Fm.L x i).sc.one_lt_alt
  have hy0 : 0 < (Fm.L x i).sc.x := by linarith
  have hT0 : 0 ≤ (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ ((Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3) := by positivity
  have hTbig : 16 * Cb + 2 ≤ (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ ((Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3) := by
    rw [hLx, hLe]
    have hc : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 16 * Cb + 2 := by linarith
    have hne : eps ≠ 0 := he.ne'
    have hm : 3 / eps * (eps / 3) = 1 := by
      first
      | (field_simp; ring)
      | field_simp
    calc 16 * Cb + 2 = ((16 * Cb + 2) ^ (3 / eps)) ^ (eps / 3) := by
          rw [← Real.rpow_mul hc, hm, Real.rpow_one]
      _ ≤ x ^ (eps / 3) := Real.rpow_le_rpow (by positivity) hxc (by positivity)
  have hS := sum_log_div_le_two_mul_mul_add_two (largeOrderResiduePrimes (Fm.L x i)) ((Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ ((Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3)) hT0
    (fun p hp => by
      simp only [largeOrderResiduePrimes, Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_range] at hp
      exact ⟨hp.2.1.one_lt.le, hp.1⟩)
  have hBTT : AuxBlock8.BTT (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) ≤
      ∑ p ∈ largeOrderResiduePrimes (Fm.L x i), Real.log p / p * ((badTops (Fm.Gx x i) p).card : ℝ) := by
    unfold AuxBlock8.BTT
    apply Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset_of_nonneg
    · intro p hp
      simp only [AuxBlock8.primesResidueBelow, largeOrderResiduePrimes, Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_range] at hp ⊢
      exact ⟨hp.1.1, hp.1.2.1, hp.1.2.2.2, hp.2⟩
    · intro p _ _
      have hl : 0 ≤ Real.log (p : ℝ) := by
        rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos p with h0 | h0
        · simp [h0]
        · exact Real.log_nonneg (by exact_mod_cast h0)
      exact mul_nonneg (div_nonneg hl (Nat.cast_nonneg p)) (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
  have hdn : 0 ≤ (Fm.L x i).sc.delta1 * (Fm.D x i).R.n := by
    have hc0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ ((Fm.D x i).host.TT.card : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
    linarith
  have hA : 0 < (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ) := by positivity
  have hpow : (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ (-2 + (Fm.L x i).sc.eps) =
      (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ) * ((Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ ((Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3)) ^ 3 := by
    have h3 : (Fm.L x i).sc.eps = (Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3 * ((3 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by push_cast; ring
    rw [Real.rpow_add hy0, ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hy0.le, ← h3]
  unfold AuxBlock8.adm2
  refine ⟨⟨by linarith, by linarith⟩, ?_⟩
  rw [hpow]
  obtain ⟨T, hTe⟩ : ∃ T, T = (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ ((Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3) := ⟨_, rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨A, hAe⟩ : ∃ A, A = (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ) := ⟨_, rfl⟩
  rw [← hTe] at hS hTbig hT0 ⊢
  rw [← hAe] at hA hB ⊢
  have k1 : 16 * Cb * (T + 2) ≤ T ^ 2 := by
    nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hTbig hT0]
  have k2 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left k1
    (div_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hdn hA.le) hT0) (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 2))
  have k3 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hS
    (mul_nonneg (by positivity : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 4 * (Cb * A)) hdn)
  have k4 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hT1 (by positivity : (0 : ℝ) ≤ A * T ^ 3)
  linarith [hBTT, hB, k2, k3, k4]

end Formalism.MainNotions.Pk

end


[LGS_global_endpoint_01] Original loG/flG and all FIVE LinkG_at conjuncts, full LinkG
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GlobalTheoremInputs.lean#L97-L140
Range SHA256: 92b096aa52bdef5669406c0e8ff34c2c77a66e617562a63c80c79821abb28825
def InertK (D : D7) (p : ℕ) : Prop :=
  D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.signal ∨ D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass

def flG {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) (C : ℝ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  C * (D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a +
    C * (D.R.f : ℝ) * (Real.logb p L.sc.a) ^ 2

def rG (eps : ℝ) : ℕ := ⌈8 * ((2 + 8 * eps) + 2) / eps⌉₊ + 1

def leastPowerExponent {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) (p : ℕ) : ℕ :=
  ⌈Real.logb p ((D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * L.sc.x ^ (3 * Scale.A))⌉₊ + 1

def loG {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) (C bo : ℝ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  let s := L.sc
  let pr : ℝ := if D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass then
      (if (p : ℝ) < s.p0 D.R.k then C * (rG s.eps : ℝ) * s.b' * Real.log s.x
       else if (p : ℝ) < s.X' then
         C * (s.b' / s.x + L.etaSz + L.Sc + (s.w'' D.R.n + D.R.t) * Real.log s.x / s.delta1) + bo
       else 0) else 0
  let orr : ℝ := if D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass ∧ (p : ℝ) < s.X' then
      C * Real.log s.x * (L.LaG * s.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) +
        (if (p : ℝ) < s.p0 D.R.k then s.a * (p : ℝ) ^ (-(leastPowerExponent L p : ℝ)) else s.a / (p : ℝ) ^ 2))
      else 0
  C * (L.Sc + D.R.k + L.etaSz) * Real.logb p (2 * s.a) + pr + orr

def LinkG_at {D : D7} {L : SizeData₂ D} (X : G.Inst D L) (K : G.Consts) (C bo : ℝ) : Prop :=
  (∀ u w, D.what₂.places.resChar w ≠ (D.mem u).A.T →
    DV.LocLe (D.plEq w) ⌊DV.ur D w (L.tau w)⌋₊ (L.NuLat u) (DV.MramL D u) ∧
    (L.lenMN u w : ℝ) ≤ (D.duS u : ℝ) * (L.tau w + L.io w) ∧
    (G.VV X K (G.aL L) (G.bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) = ∅ → L.tau w = 0 ∧ L.io w = 0 ∧ L.lenMN u w = 0)) ∧
  (∀ p, ∀ t0 ∈ G.VV X K (G.aL L) (G.bL L) p,
    G.Sv X K (G.aL L) (G.bL L) p t0 ≤ 2 * (D.R.m : ℝ) * (G.Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * L.sc.a / p) ∧
  (∀ w, InertK D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) → (D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.X'' D.R.n →
    (∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, 0 ≤ L.gain j w ∧
      L.gain j w ≤ 4 * (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) * L.sc.a / (D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℝ)) ∧
    (∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∑ t ∈ L.TTw w,
        (L.vonMangoldtClassSum j t (D.what₂.places.resChar w) - loG L C bo (D.what₂.places.resChar w))) -
      (D.R.m : ℝ) * flG L C (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ≤
      ((∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, L.gain j w) - L.tau w)) ∧
  G.ServedOrbitsContained X K (G.aL L) (G.bL L) ∧ G.MeanKappaBound X K

def LinkG {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  ∃ (C x0 : ℝ) (δ : ℝ → ℝ), Filter.Tendsto δ Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
    ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, LinkG_at (Fm.Gx x i) Fm.GK C ((Fm.L x i).sc.b ^ δ x)


[LGS_global_endpoint_02] Exact LinkG_at and same-family installation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GrowthBounds/PlaneCountTheoremWithConstants.lean#L718-L878

theorem linkG_at_core (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (C bo : ℝ) (hT : GatTies D)
    (hfill : ∀ w, ParametersFill X K w)
    (hlat0 : ∀ w (u : D.what₂.Word), VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) = ∅ →
      D.what₂.places.resChar w ≠ (D.mem u).A.T → L.lenMN u w = 0)
    (hlen : LenServed X Y K)
    (hsupp : ∀ w, ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.X'' D.R.n → w ∈ L.supp)
    (hTied : LatTied hT X Y) (hNu : DV.NuDef D L)
    (h1 : AuxBlock8.Conclusion1 D L X K C bo) (h2 : AuxBlock8.Conclusion2 D L X K) (h0 : AuxBlock8.Conclusion0 D L X) (hG1 : AuxBlock8.admG1 D L X Y K)
    (hServed : ServedGIdx X K) (hSv : SvBound X K) (hIncl : NuServed X K) :
    LinkG_at X K (4 * |C| + 1) bo := by
  refine ⟨?_, hSv, ?_, served_orbits_in_set X K _ _, h2⟩
  · intro u w hw
    obtain ⟨_, _, _, _, htau, hio, _⟩ := hfill w
    by_cases hV : VV X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) = ∅
    · have ht0 : L.tau w = 0 := by rw [htau]; unfold tauP; rw [if_pos hV]
      have hi0 : L.io w = 0 := by
        rw [hio]; unfold ioP
        rw [dif_neg (by rw [hV]; exact Finset.not_nonempty_empty)]
      have hl0 := hlat0 w u hV hw
      refine ⟨?_, ?_, fun _ => ⟨ht0, hi0, hl0⟩⟩
      · rw [ht0]
        have h0 : ⌊DV.ur D w 0⌋₊ = 0 := by unfold DV.ur; simp
        rw [h0]
        exact hNu.1 u w hw
      · rw [hl0, ht0, hi0]
        simp
    · refine ⟨by rw [htau]; exact hIncl u w hV hw, ?_, fun h => absurd h hV⟩
      have hg := hServed u _ hV hw
      obtain ⟨_, ⟨l, hl, hle⟩, _⟩ := hG1 hT hTied u w hg
      obtain ⟨l', hl', heq⟩ := hlen u w hg hV
      have hll : l = l' := by
        have := hl.symm.trans hl'
        exact_mod_cast this
      subst hll
      have hv := vUnit_pos (D.gat u _ hg) (Y.vOf u _ hg 0)
      have hd : ((D.gat u _ hg).d : ℝ) = (D.duS u : ℝ) := by rw [D.gat_d]; rfl
      rw [htau, hio]
      rw [heq, hd] at hle
      have h3 : (L.lenMN u w : ℝ) * vUnit (D.gat u _ hg) ≤
          ((D.duS u : ℝ) * (tauP X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) +
            ioP X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w))) * vUnit (D.gat u _ hg) := by
        linarith
      exact le_of_mul_le_mul_right h3 hv
  · intro w hin hX
    have hp := D.what₂.places.resChar_prime w
    have hws := hsupp w hX
    have hin' : IsResidueClassZero D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ∨ IsSg D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) := hin.symm
    obtain ⟨_, hwp, hTT, hF, htau, _, hLa⟩ := hfill w
    have hgain := hF hws hin'
    obtain ⟨hA, hB⟩ := h1 _ hp hin' hX
    refine ⟨fun j hj => by rw [hgain j hj]; exact hA j hj, ?_⟩
    rw [htau, Finset.sum_congr rfl hgain]
    refine le_trans ?_ hB
    have hlo := lo_le_loG K L hK.1 hLa C bo _ hp
    have hsum : ∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D,
        ∑ t ∈ L.TTw w, (L.vonMangoldtClassSum j t (D.what₂.places.resChar w) -
          loG L (4 * |C| + 1) bo (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) ≤
        ∑ s ∈ TTP X K (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w),
          (askG X (aL L) (bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) j s - lo K L C bo (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) := by
      intro j hj
      rw [← hTT, Finset.sum_map]
      apply Finset.sum_le_sum
      intro t _
      rw [ask_eq_askP X _ hp hwp j t]
      have haw : ∀ s ∈ D.host.TT, (aL L s : ℝ) ≤ 2 * L.sc.a := by
        intro s hs
        have h1 : aL L s = L.aT ⟨s, hs⟩ := by unfold aL; rw [dif_pos hs]
        obtain ⟨_, h2⟩ := L.aT_win ⟨s, hs⟩
        have h3 : L.sc.a / ((D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2)) ≤ L.sc.a := by
          have ha := sc_one_lt_a L.sc
          have hk : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) * L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) := by
            have hk1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.k : ℝ) := by
              have := D.R.m_add_three_le_k
              exact_mod_cast (show 1 ≤ D.R.k by omega)
            have hx1 : 1 ≤ L.sc.x ^ (Scale.A / 2) := Real.one_le_rpow L.sc.one_lt_alt.le (by unfold Scale.A; norm_num)
            nlinarith
          exact div_le_self (by linarith) hk
        rw [h1]
        linarith
      have hbw : ∀ s ∈ D.host.TT, (0 : ℝ) ≤ (bL L s : ℝ) := by
        intro s hs
        have h1 : bL L s = L.bT ⟨s, hs⟩ := by unfold bL; rw [dif_pos hs]
        rw [h1]
        exact le_trans (sc_b'_nonneg L.sc) (L.bT_ge ⟨s, hs⟩)
      have hPG := (h0 (aL L) (bL L) haw hbw _ hp hin' j hj t.1 t.2).1
      simp only [Function.Embedding.coe_subtype]
      linarith
    have hfl := fl_le_flG L C _ hp
    have hm : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (D.R.m : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
    have h4 := Finset.sum_le_sum hsum
    have h5 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hfl hm
    linarith

theorem linkG_at_of (X : Inst D L) (Y : LatInst D) (K : Consts) (hK : K.Nonneg) (C bo : ℝ) (hT : GatTies D)
    (hfill : ∀ w, ParametersFill X K w) (hlat : ∀ w, LatticeLengthsAtPlace X Y K w)
    (hsupp : ∀ w, ((D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.X'' D.R.n → w ∈ L.supp)
    (hTied : LatTied hT X Y) (hNu : DV.NuDef D L)
    (h1 : AuxBlock8.Conclusion1 D L X K C bo) (h2 : AuxBlock8.Conclusion2 D L X K) (h0 : AuxBlock8.Conclusion0 D L X) (hG1 : AuxBlock8.admG1 D L X Y K)
    (hServed : ServedGIdx X K) (hSv : SvBound X K) (hIncl : NuServed X K) :
    LinkG_at X K (4 * |C| + 1) bo :=
  linkG_at_core X Y K hK C bo hT hfill (fun w => (hlat w).2)
    (fun u w hg hV => (hlat w).1 (hsupp w (le_trans (cand_of_VV_ne X K _ _ _ hV).2.1.le
      (X'_le_X'' L.sc D.R.n D.R.n_pos'))) u hg)
    hsupp hTied hNu h1 h2 h0 hG1 hServed hSv hIncl

def Ev {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (P : ∀ x, Fm.Ix x → Prop) : Prop := ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i, P x i

theorem Ev.and {eps : ℝ} {Fm : FamP eps} {P Q : ∀ x, Fm.Ix x → Prop} (hP : Ev Fm P) (hQ : Ev Fm Q) :
    Ev Fm (fun x i => P x i ∧ Q x i) := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := hP
  obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := hQ
  exact ⟨max a b, fun x hx i => ⟨ha x (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) hx) i, hb x (le_trans (le_max_right _ _) hx) i⟩⟩

theorem Ev.x1 {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Ev Fm (fun x _ => Fm.x1 < x) :=
  ⟨Fm.x1 + 1, fun x hx _ => by linarith⟩

def FamilyFirstGrowthBound {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  Ev Fm fun x i => AuxBlock8.admG1 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) (Fm.Gy x i) Fm.GK

theorem servedGIdx_ev {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Ev Fm fun x i => ServedGIdx (Fm.Gx x i) Fm.GK := by
  obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := Ev.x1 Fm
  refine ⟨max x0 (((4 : ℕ) : ℝ) ^ (5 / Fm.ez)), fun x hx i => ?_⟩
  have hx1 := h x (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) hx) i
  have he : 0 < Fm.ez := by rw [← Fm.L_ez x i]; exact (Fm.L x i).sc.ez_pos
  apply servedGIdx_of _ _ _ (frobNe _) (level_primes_not_class_zero (Fm.params x hx1 i))
  refine UpperTheory.Host.le_floor_ez _ he (Fm.L_ez x i).ge 4 ?_
  rw [Fm.L_x x i hx1]
  exact le_trans (le_max_right _ _) hx

theorem svBound_ev {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Ev Fm fun x i => SvBound (Fm.Gx x i) Fm.GK := by
  obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := Ev.x1 Fm
  exact ⟨x0, fun x hx i => svBound_of _ _ Fm.GK_nonneg (Fm.params x (h x hx i) i)⟩

theorem nuServed_ev {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Ev Fm fun x i => NuServed (Fm.Gx x i) Fm.GK := by
  obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := (servedGIdx_ev Fm).and (Ev.x1 Fm)
  exact ⟨x0, fun x hx i => nuServed_of _ (Fm.Gy x i) _ (Fm.ex x (h x hx i).2 i).cmp (Fm.ex x (h x hx i).2 i).lat.served
    (h x hx i).1⟩

def NPDescF {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  Ev Fm fun x i => ∀ hx : Fm.x1 < x, (Fm.ex x hx i).cmp.NPDesc (Fm.Gx x i) (Fm.Gy x i) Fm.GK

theorem sizeDataLat_ev {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (hd : NPDescF Fm) :
    Ev Fm fun x i => ∀ w, LatticeLengthsAtPlace (Fm.Gx x i) (Fm.Gy x i) Fm.GK w := by
  obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := hd.and (Ev.x1 Fm)
  refine ⟨x0, fun x hx i w => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨hdd, hx1⟩ := h x hx i
  exact LatCmp.sizeDataLat_of_latDefs (Fm.ex x hx1 i).cmp (Fm.gatT x i) _ _ _ (Fm.nuDef x hx1 i) (Fm.ex x hx1 i).lat
    (Fm.ex x hx1 i).nu_unserved (hdd hx1) w

theorem link_of_family_theorem {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (hP : AuxBlock8.ConclusionOnFamily Fm Fm.GK Fm.Gx) (hG1 : FamilyFirstGrowthBound Fm)

    (sizeDataLat_ev Fm hFl)).and (Ev.x1 Fm)

theorem linkG_fam_noKer {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (h14 : SecondTheoremAtFamily Fm) (h13 : FirstTheoremAtFamily Fm)
    (hLa : 0 < Fm.GK.La) (hCe : 1 ≤ Fm.cX.Ce) (hCb : Ev Fm fun x i => CbLarge Fm.GK (Fm.L x i).sc)
    (hE : Ev Fm fun x i => TrivialStabiliserFiniteClasses (Fm.D x i)) (hS : Ev Fm fun x i => Joints.StdSubAt (Fm.D x i))
    (h2 : Adm2F Fm) (hd : NPDescF Fm) (hCN : CornerGLContinuity.FamilyWindowLogBound Fm) : LinkG Fm := by
  obtain ⟨C, hC0, a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6, hp14⟩ := h14
  obtain ⟨CH, C', b0, b1, b2, b3, b4, b5, b6, hp13⟩ := h13
  have h6 := family_second_bound_of_theorem Fm C hC0 a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 hp14
  have h7 := family_kappa_bound Fm (kapIDefF Fm)
  have h8 := family_first_bound_of_theorem Fm CH C' (kapIDefF Fm) b0 b1 b2 b3 b4 b5 b6 hp13
  have hCe0 : 0 ≤ Fm.cX.Ce := le_trans zero_le_one hCe
  obtain ⟨C₅, h59⟩ := UnusedSlot33.field_lemmas_except_seven
  have hO : CornerGLContinuity.FamilyGlobalOrderEstimate Fm 100 := fun x i => (h59 (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i).sc).2.2.2.1
  have h9 : Adm9F Fm := by
    obtain ⟨x1, hx1⟩ := Ev.x1 Fm
    exact ⟨Fm.cX.Ce, hCe0, x1, fun x hx i => Joints.weyl_sandwich_of_factors _ _ _ Fm.cX.Ce hCe0 (Fm.ex x (hx1 x hx i) i).ep_def
      (Joints.weylSandwich_of_model _ (Model.modelSandwichG_of_ties _ (Fm.gatT x i)))
      (Joints.expBound_of _ _ _ Fm.cX.Ce hCe (Fm.ex x (hx1 x hx i) i).ep_def (Fm.params x (hx1 x hx i) i)
        (Fm.gatT x i))⟩
  have hLP : Ev Fm fun x i => CornerGLContinuity.OrbitLatticeBounds (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) (Fm.Gy x i) Fm.GK := by
    obtain ⟨x0, h⟩ := ((((h6.and h7).and hS).and (hypIAt_ev Fm hLa hCN)).and
      (Joints.hypIIAt_fam Fm hCN)).and hCb
    exact ⟨x0, fun x hx i => Joints.linear_programming_input_of_hypotheses _ _ _ _ _ (h x hx i).1.1.1.1.1 (h x hx i).1.1.1.1.2
        (h x hx i).1.1.1.2 Model.lpBaseChange (h x hx i).1.1.2 (h x hx i).1.2
        (rho_le_rhoB _ _ _ Fm.GK_nonneg.2.1 (La_nonneg Fm.GK_nonneg.1 _) (h x hx i).2)⟩
  have hEp : Ev Fm fun x i => CornerGLContinuity.LatticeSandwich (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) := by
    obtain ⟨Ce, _, x0, h⟩ := id h9
    exact ⟨x0, fun x hx i => TopChoiceBounds.sandwich_of_exponent_clause _ Ce (Fm.gatT x i) (h x hx i)⟩
  exact linkG_without_kernels Fm (family_first_lemma Fm) h2 (family_orders_first_part Fm 100 (TopChoiceBounds.family_order_bound_holds Fm 100) hO)
    (family_orders_bound_of_lemma Fm (TopChoiceBounds.family_band_ratio_holds Fm) hCN) (family_sizes_bound Fm hLa) h6 h7 h8 h9 hE
    ⟨0, fun x _ i u w hg => Model.operators_preserve_lattices _ _⟩ hLP hEp hd

theorem link_of_local_closed (𝒞 : FamClass) (h : Loc)
    (hE : Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => Ev Fm fun x i => TrivialStabiliserFiniteClasses (Fm.D x i))
    (hS : Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => Ev Fm fun x i => Joints.StdSubAt (Fm.D x i))
    (h2 : Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => Adm2F Fm) (hd : Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => NPDescF Fm) :
    Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => LinkG Fm :=
  ((((((((((second_theorem_closed_form 𝒞 (second_theorem_absolute_constant (extremal_projector_tools_of_loc h) (local_model_results h))).and
    (first_theorem_closed_form 𝒞 (first_theorem_threshold_functions (extremal_projector_tools_of_loc h)
      (loc8All_of (extremal_projector_tools_of_loc h) (local_model_results h) (local_flatness_results h))))).and (signs_closed 𝒞)).and
    (ones_closed 𝒞)).and (cb_closed 𝒞)).and hE).and hS).and h2).and hd).and (TopChoiceBounds.family_log_bound_closed 𝒞)).imp
    fun Fm _ _ h => linkG_fam_noKer Fm h.1.1.1.1.1.1.1.1.1 h.1.1.1.1.1.1.1.1.2 h.1.1.1.1.1.1.1.2.1
      h.1.1.1.1.1.1.2.2.2 h.1.1.1.1.1.2 h.1.1.1.1.2 h.1.1.1.2 h.1.1.2 h.1.2 h.2

theorem linkG_concrete5 (𝒞 : FamClass) (hT : Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => GroupCompatibilityFamily Fm) (h2 : Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => Adm2F Fm) :
    Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => LinkG Fm :=
  linkG_concrete_noKer 𝒞 loc_proved hT h2

def cLast : List ℝ := [Fm.CkLow, Fm.Ck6, Fm.CfL, Fm.cLL⁻¹, Fm.Crho, Fm.Crho, Fm.CDelG, Fm.Cw, Fm.CDel,

example := @cOut.eq_1

end FamP

structure TransportedAlt where
  pre : ℝ
  t : List ℝ → ℝ
  mid : List ℝ → ℝ
  last : List ℝ → ℝ

def TransportedAlt.le (K K' : TransportedAlt) : Prop :=
  K.pre ≤ K'.pre ∧ (∀ l, K.t l ≤ K'.t l) ∧ (∀ l, K.mid l ≤ K'.mid l) ∧ ∀ l, K.last l ≤ K'.last l

def TransportedAlt.sup (K K' : TransportedAlt) : TransportedAlt :=
  ⟨max K.pre K'.pre, fun l => max (K.t l) (K'.t l), fun l => max (K.mid l) (K'.mid l), fun l => max (K.last l) (K'.last l)⟩

def FamP.Large {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (K : TransportedAlt) (CH : ℝ) : Prop :=
  (∀ c ∈ Fm.cPre, K.pre ≤ c) ∧ True ∧ (∀ c ∈ Fm.cMid CH, K.mid Fm.cOut ≤ c) ∧
    ∀ c ∈ Fm.cLast, K.last (Fm.cOut ++ Fm.cMid CH) ≤ c

theorem FamP.Large.mono {eps : ℝ} {Fm : FamP eps} {K K' : TransportedAlt} {CH : ℝ} (h : Fm.Large K' CH) (hK : K.le K') :
    Fm.Large K CH :=
  ⟨fun c hc => hK.1.trans (h.1 c hc), trivial, fun c hc => (hK.2.2.1 _).trans (h.2.2.1 c hc),
    fun c hc => (hK.2.2.2 _).trans (h.2.2.2 c hc)⟩

theorem FamP.large_sup {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (K K' : TransportedAlt) (CH : ℝ) :
    Fm.Large (K.sup K') CH ↔ Fm.Large K CH ∧ Fm.Large K' CH := by
  constructor
  · intro h
    exact ⟨h.mono ⟨le_max_left _ _, fun _ => le_max_left _ _, fun _ => le_max_left _ _, fun _ => le_max_left _ _⟩,
      h.mono ⟨le_max_right _ _, fun _ => le_max_right _ _, fun _ => le_max_right _ _, fun _ => le_max_right _ _⟩⟩
  · rintro ⟨h, h'⟩
    exact ⟨fun c hc => max_le (h.1 c hc) (h'.1 c hc), trivial,
      fun c hc => max_le (h.2.2.1 c hc) (h'.2.2.1 c hc), fun c hc => max_le (h.2.2.2 c hc) (h'.2.2.2 c hc)⟩

abbrev FamClass : Type 1 := ∀ {eps : ℝ}, FamP eps → Prop

def Closed (𝒞 : FamClass) (P : ∀ {eps : ℝ}, FamP eps → ℝ → Prop) : Prop :=
  ∃ ε1 : ℝ, 0 < ε1 ∧ ∀ eps : ℝ, 0 < eps → eps ≤ ε1 → ∃ K : TransportedAlt, ∀ (Fm : FamP eps) (CH : ℝ),
    𝒞 Fm → Fm.Large K CH → P Fm CH

theorem Closed.and {𝒞 : FamClass} {P Q : ∀ {eps : ℝ}, FamP eps → ℝ → Prop} (hP : Closed 𝒞 P) (hQ : Closed 𝒞 Q) :
    Closed 𝒞 (fun Fm CH => P Fm CH ∧ Q Fm CH) := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha, hP⟩ := hP
  obtain ⟨b, hb, hQ⟩ := hQ
  refine ⟨min a b, lt_min ha hb, fun e he hle => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨K, hK⟩ := hP e he (hle.trans (min_le_left _ _))
  obtain ⟨K', hK'⟩ := hQ e he (hle.trans (min_le_right _ _))
  exact ⟨K.sup K', fun Fm CH hc h =>
    ⟨hK Fm CH hc ((Fm.large_sup K K' CH).1 h).1, hK' Fm CH hc ((Fm.large_sup K K' CH).1 h).2⟩⟩

theorem Closed.imp {𝒞 : FamClass} {P Q : ∀ {eps : ℝ}, FamP eps → ℝ → Prop} (hP : Closed 𝒞 P)
    (h : ∀ {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (CH : ℝ), 0 < eps → P Fm CH → Q Fm CH) : Closed 𝒞 Q := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha, hP⟩ := hP
  refine ⟨a, ha, fun e he hle => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨K, hK⟩ := hP e he hle
  exact ⟨K, fun Fm CH hc hL => h Fm CH he (hK Fm CH hc hL)⟩

def TuSize {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, (Fm.L x i).TuGe ∧
